# Kompara v2 — product & UX elevation spec

**not built** · **Scope:** the whole Android product (app + overlay), design only.
> **Bar:** comparable in use and feel to the Uber Driver app — structure and discipline, not brand.
> **Method:** two independent principal-designer audits (Codex CLI on GPT-6 Astra at ultra reasoning; Claude Opus 5.5) from one brief and one as-built inventory → cross-critique on 16 explicit forks → synthesis (Claude Fable 5.1) → Round 3: both reviewers and a six-lens adversarial judge panel scored the result and listed defects → fix pass → re-score (Rounds 4–10; both reviewers passed Round 10 on the structural rubric). Round 11: both reviewers failed the Round 10 look on a second rubric ([`03-rubric-r11-craft.md`](ux-elevation/debate/03-rubric-r11-craft.md), 16/40 each) → independent redesign proposals → cross-critique → synthesis ([`120`](ux-elevation/debate/120-round11-synthesis.md)) → rebuild on the v3 "paper, ink and the raya" system → Round 12 scored v3 on both rubrics (Codex PASS 68/80 + 34/40; Opus NOT_YET 71/80 + 33/40) → Round 13 fixes and a critic pass per section (§9h) → Round 13 scored that version on both rubrics: **both PASS** (Codex 71/80 + 35/40, Opus 73/80 + 35/40) → a residual pass on both reviewers' bounded lists (§9i) → Round 13b confirmed that pass with no rendered regression: **both PASS** (Codex 71/80 + 35/40, Opus 74/80 + 36/40; [`171`](ux-elevation/debate/171-round13b-codex-astra.md), [`172`](ux-elevation/debate/172-round13b-opus-5-5.md)); their last cosmetic notes (E7's one-line qualifier placeholder, softened perception claims in the D1/N3 captions, the C11 caption, the Mes bars as a shared class) are applied in this version. Round 14: a blind template test against a generic baseline from fresh models, scored by six context-free judges (§9j), replaced the chip with "la regla" and set Hoy's anatomy and the white day surface; a consolidation pass then moved the section agents' inline styles into shared rules and a cue test replaced the amarillo bar with ◆ (§9j). Round 14's reviewers split — Opus PASS, Codex NOT_YET with glance physics at 7 (the chip out-shouted the host's fare, C10's foot was 12dp, C8 covered the pickup pin) — and six judges comparing eight screens with Uber Driver asked, six of six, for a fixed reading order on the chip and seven screen fixes. Round 15 applied the screen fixes; two blind chip rounds with the host's fare in the crop (lineups 7 and 8) replaced the moving band with la regla v16 (Round 16, §9j). Round 15's re-score passed both reviewers on both rubrics — Codex 71/80 + 34/40 (glance physics back to 8), Opus 74/80 + 34/40 (signature 9 → 8, craft exactly at the bar) ([`211`](ux-elevation/debate/211-round15-codex-astra.md), [`212`](ux-elevation/debate/212-round15-opus-5-5.md)) — and the Round 15b residual pass applied both lists (§9k). Round 17 debated the boards' recurring asks — one money definition, cutting the second tier, a drawn icon set — and each reviewer's one added change ([`240`](ux-elevation/debate/240-round17-debate-brief.md), [`241`](ux-elevation/debate/241-round17-codex-astra.md), [`242`](ux-elevation/debate/242-round17-opus-5-5.md)), and a usability walkthrough (six model judges doing eight driver tasks on the contact sheets) found the money defect six times of six (§9l). Round 17's re-score split the reviewers — Opus PASS (74/80 + 34/40), Codex NOT_YET (67/80 + 34/40: A1's legend on a 40dp pitch, the limits and the recovery unreachable from Hoy, A3's realistic dialog; [`251`](ux-elevation/debate/251-round17-codex-astra.md), [`252`](ux-elevation/debate/252-round17-opus-5-5.md)) — and the walkthrough's second run held at mean ease 5.5/7, with the first day, the limits and a missing overlay the weak tasks; Round 18 answered both reviewers' lists and those three tasks (§9m). Round 18's re-score passed both reviewers on both rubrics — Codex 70/80 + 34/40, Opus 76/80 + 34/40 ([`261`](ux-elevation/debate/261-round18-codex-astra.md), [`262`](ux-elevation/debate/262-round18-opus-5-5.md)) — and both kept "comparable in use" at NOT_YET: the walkthrough's third run measured mean ease 5.6/7 against the gate's 6.0; Round 19 drew the residuals both named and run 3's judges found (§9n); its re-score split the reviewers on one craft hard fail (Codex NOT_YET, A3b's K tile; Opus PASS), the walkthrough's fourth run stayed on the plateau (≈5.4/7), and Round 19b applied both Round 19 lists (§9o); the pre-test readied the kit and the prototype for real drivers (§9p), and Round 20's confirmation passed both reviewers on both rubrics but found three test blockers, fixed in this version (§9q); walkthrough run 5, the first on the clickable prototype, and two Round 21 proposals (Codex `301`, Opus `302`) led to Round 21 (§9r), and walkthrough run 6 to Round 22 (§9s). This version carries all of it. Full record in [`docs/ux-elevation/debate/`](ux-elevation/debate/).
> **Golden fixture v3 (dated records):** every number in the spec and the mockups comes from one fixture, computed at full precision and rounded only at display. Costs $2.8417/km (= $24.50/L ÷ 12 km/L + $0.80); the driver's two limits, tu meta (verde from $8.00/km) and tu mínimo (rojo below $6.00/km); lo del coche $1,500/semana from lunes 28 sep, allocated by calendar day to every "te quedan" total — the day, the week and the month ($214.29/día; Round 17). 2026 calendar, today = lunes 19 oct: turnos 08:00–12:30 (5 viajes → $648 after running costs; $434 on Hoy at 13:10, after the day's car share) · en pausa 12:40–14:20 · 14:20–18:00 (4 viajes → $592); offers 11:48 sin lectura · 12:10 rojo $2.20/km · 14:32 verde $9.49/km (tomada) · 14:50 amarillo $7.16/km (tomada) · 16:04 verde $11.04/km (tomada: $236 over 17.0 km) · 17:21 verde $11.40/km (tomada: $255 over 17.9 km) — the last two split out of the second turno (`today_afternoon_trips`, Round 19b), whose 4 viajes, $759 and 58.9 km are unchanged; shift 1's five trips are split out the same way for D1a (`today_morning_trips`, pre-test fixes, their second batch and Round 20: 11:20 $172 over 13.6 km → $9.81/km (26 min, 2.4 km of them to pick up: D2e's $133 sin el coche and $308/h, Round 24c), 10:41 $158 over 12.9 km → $9.41/km, 10:02 $165 over 12.4 km → $10.46/km, 9:24 $178 over 13.1 km → $10.75/km, 8:15 $158 over 12.3 km → $10.00/km; they add to shift 1's 5 viajes, $831 and 64.3 km), with its two verdes that left (`today_morning_missed`, Round 20: 9:51 $132 over 11.0 km → $9.16/km, 8:40 $118 over 9.8 km → $9.20/km), so D1a lists ten offers by 14:40; day $1,026 (9 viajes tomados, aprox.: te pagó la app $1,590 − 123.2 km × $2.8417 − lo del coche $214.29; at 14:40, 6 viajes → $548: te pagó la app $979 − 76.3 km × $2.8417 − $214.29, D3a). Last closed week 12–18 oct: te pagó la app $8,400 (reader estimate) − 675.8 km × $2.8417 = $6,480 − $1,500 = $4,980; 62 viajes; 41 h en turno (both apps); Uber 34 viajes/420 km/$5,028 aprox. ($9.13/km neto), DiDi 28 viajes/255.8 km/$3,372 aprox. ($10.34/km neto); the driver imported Uber's statement for this week: $5,062 · 34 viajes · 24 h en línea → $149/viaje antes de gastos, **source = statement**. Shifts on 19 oct: $648 + $592 after running costs (no screen shows money per shift since Round 17: shift figures before the car would not add up to the day after it). The imported Uber statement for 12–18 oct ($5,062) differs from the reader estimate ($5,028) by an adjustment the reader cannot see; the week hero stays on reader data, and E1's row "Cuadrar con tu resumen de Uber" opens the sheet that says so with the amount (E1e: "Solo Uber, antes de gastos" · "Lo que leyó Kompara $5,028" · "Lo que dice tu resumen $5,062" · "Diferencia +$34"; Round 22), the standing uses the statement, and the H5b choice ("Usar el PDF") would recompute the week on the statement payment with the known costs. October (dated, reader-only), read at Hoy's minute, 14:40 (Round 20; today counts as Tu día's $548 · 6 viajes): $11,692 after running costs and the car cost by day — the rounded weekly rows ($1,417 + $4,747 + $4,980 + $548) and the ledger ($20,429 − 1,642.0 km × $2.8417 = −$4,666 − 19 días × $214.29 = −$4,071) both foot to it, so no rounding note; 155 viajes; Uber paid $12,487, DiDi $7,942 (`today_by_app_1440`: every trip of 19 oct is an Uber trip, so today's $979 by 14:40 is Uber $979 · DiDi $0; Round 24c, Opus 322 item 7); E5's example base (× 0.94) Uber $11,738 · DiDi $7,465, faltan $2,117 to the $9,582 reference. More alternate scenarios are labelled above the phone, never inside it (Rounds 18–19b), besides E3c's mixed September: B8, a 19 oct whose only trip is the 14:32 verde offer ($113.90 − $214.29 = −$100 at 14:40); E1c, the closed week if domingo 18 had had no trips (that day −$214, the week $4,481); B9 and G4c, the 19 oct if the 12:10 rojo offer had come during navigation and drawn no chip (C11's case; the day's figures unchanged); G4b, the Semáforo screen with the battery restricted (B7's); G4d, the Semáforo screen en pausa since 12:40 (B5's, at 13:10; Round 19b); G4e, the Semáforo screen preparado on a first run (B3's, at 9:44; pre-test); and, from the pre-test fixes, G4f, preparado the next morning with Uber's last read kept (B4's, at 7:30), G4g, the Semáforo screen with DiDi's reading switched off (B6's, at 10:20), and D1b, Tu día at 14:40 in B9's world; and, from Round 20, G1b and G4h, Ajustes and Semáforo in B2's off world (martes 20 oct 8:05). Cohort CDMX·Uber·12–18 oct: 1,240 conductores, statement data, per trip only (Uber statements carry no km), $131/viaje antes de gastos → the driver's statement $149 is the 78th percentile. **Every figure carries a source (reader estimate · app statement · imported file); a rank or comparison pairs only figures of the same source and basis.** Per-app hours are not measurable by the reader when both apps run, so per-app "$/h" exists only with statement hours. Engineering must keep this true with a CI check: mock, preview and spec numbers equal engine output, and no two screens show the same period in different scopes without a label.

---

## 0. In ten lines

1. **Three tabs: Hoy · Ganancias · Comparar.** Ajustes sits behind a gear (Round 18: the walkthrough's drivers looked for their limits, costs and help under that word, not under an unlabelled person icon). The reader is a *status*, not a tab; Fiscal is a monthly review, not a tab; Historial is the week stepper, not a settings row.
2. **One hero per screen, one primary action, at most one number per row in the first viewport.** Hoy: is the semáforo working and what have I kept today. Ganancias: my week/month as a timeline. Comparar: one metric at a time.
3. **The chip tells the truth.** A fixed colour band — a solid ▲ ◆ ▼ cue, one deciding rate with "/km neto" and the position phrase against the driver's own limits ("Arriba de tu meta") — over the two labelled limits and a marker that repeats the place; where the full plate cannot fit clear of the map's markers the phrase line drops first, never the limits (the compact chip, 240 × 80, C12); $/h lives in the offer detail. A reader failure is **gris, never red**. The deciding unit never swaps. No brand strip, no K, no verdict word. **Drag only:** tap and long-press do nothing over the host; thresholds are changed in the app (Tu meta y tu mínimo, the first row of the Semáforo screen that Hoy's status row opens — two taps from Hoy since the pre-test reverted Round 19's one-tap door — or Ajustes' row, or "Cambiar tu meta y tu mínimo" from an offer's detail). The overlay editor was removed because it could only ever open during a live offer.
4. **The signature is "la regla".** The driver's two limits are two labelled lines on the chip's ink tray — "tu meta $8" above, "tu mínimo $6" below — and the trip's marker sits above both (verde ▲), between them (amarillo ◆) or below both (rojo ▼), while the fixed band above says the same place with the solid cue and the phrase; gris has no colour, no cue, no marker and dashed lines. Up means more money. The same object recurs at every scale: the mark that leads every offer row, the four-row legend that teaches the states on the demo (A1), la regla at 1:1 over the two limit controls in Tu meta y tu mínimo (G2). Where the app shows quantities it draws them to scale instead: the best bar in ink with its figure in Ganancias (the dashed car-cost line retired in Round 17: the bars are after the car, like the figure), proportional bars in Comparar and on the share card. No gauge, no traffic light, no verdict word, no coloured rounded card.
5. **The visual system is paper, ink and the raya.** One white day surface (`--paper` #FFFFFF) with ink text; Barlow for the UI and Barlow Semi Condensed for money; tab roots carry a 30sp title; the 72sp display is the day's alone (Hoy, Tu día), Ganancias sets its figure at 40sp beside its bars, Comparar leads with its finding as a 24sp sentence, and sentence heroes and headlines are 24sp (Round 17); no cards, no pills, no icon tiles, no brand strip; Kompara draws its own icons; verdict colour appears only on verdicts (the chip and the mark) and once more as the green dot of "Semáforo funcionando" when nothing needs the driver; errors and every other status are ink glyphs and words; the app follows the system theme and ships a night set; the chip is theme-independent.
6. **Money words mean one thing each.** "Te quedan / Te quedaron" is a period's money after all your costs — gasolina and desgaste per km, lo del coche by calendar day — one definition for the day, the week and the month (Round 17), with "aprox." in the label and one quiet line under the number, the count and the deduction basis, "6 viajes tomados (1 en curso), ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche ›", which opens Cómo se calculó; "Te deja / Te dejó" is what one trip or one app leaves after running costs; "neto" only as the unit on rates, defined once on A1 ("neto: ya restamos gasolina y desgaste", Round 19b); the chip and every rate never include the car; "Te pagó la app" instead of "bruto"; offers are "La tomaste · aprox." until a payment is confirmed; the overlay and its status are "el semáforo", *preparado* vs *funcionando* (and "funcionando" expires).
7. **Onboarding = demo → one disclosure → Activar → (battery only if a restriction is detected) → Hoy.** Value in 5 s, the permission while motivation is fresh (Android's question on the same screen as a tagged example picture, A3; Round 21 merged A3b into it), reader *preparado* in ~60–90 s, no account before value, no "ya está leyendo" before anything was read; the first-run primary opens the ride app to test it (B3, which opens on "Listo. Abre tu app de viajes", confirms "permiso listo" and says the costs are typical). Since Round 18 the costs are asked on Hoy the next morning (B4, typical until then) and the WhatsApp number is offered later, from Ajustes (A6–A7).
8. **Free feels complete:** reader, today, this week + 3 previous, current month, IMSS review (with the official verification route as its primary action), Tus apps, share, import. **Premium:** the breakdown of older weeks, city comparisons, the accountant's PDF. Locks mask the other side's number or the breakdown, never the driver's own total.
9. **Trial = 14 days, no card, no charge at the end, explicit start** when there is something paid to show; its terms are shown apart from any renewal language. Data eligibility is explained *before* purchase, never as a surprise after. Nobody is charged for absent data.
10. **Kill list:** Lector tab, Fiscal tab, Historial screen, Resumen de la semana, Simulador as a destination, the 3-page pitch and its "30 %", the Lector mock card, the 5-column table, "Dónde puedes ganar más", coaching consejos, streak, goal bar, "Cubierto este mes", the second consent dialog, the "$200" promise, sliders over a live offer, 55 dead strings.
11. **Kept and fixed:** import (with an honest "este archivo sí se sube" line), IMSS review (free, factual), referral (under Ajustes), share card (preview = PNG, amounts hidden by default).
12. **Scores:** today 27–31/80. The first v2 draft scored 59 (Opus) and 51 (Codex) in Round 3 — both failed it on the same defects: mockup arithmetic that didn't run through the formula, net-vs-gross comparisons, an undisclosed data flow, unvalidated paywall claims. Rounds 4–10 fixed every confirmed defect (§9b–§9e) and Round 10 passed both reviewers on the structural rubric (Opus 69, Codex 68). Round 11 then scored the same version **16/40 by both reviewers on the craft rubric** (§9g, §10): structure passed, feel did not. Round 12 scored the v3 rebuild: **Codex PASS (68/80 + 34/40); Opus NOT_YET (71/80 + 33/40)**, one point short on the signature ("it stops at Tu día"). Round 13 carried the signature into Ganancias, Comparar, Tu semáforo and the demo and fixed both lists (§9h); **both reviewers passed it on both rubrics on the same version — Codex 71/80 + 35/40, Opus 73/80 + 35/40** — and a residual pass applied their bounded lists (§9i), which both confirmed in Round 13b (Codex 71/80 + 35/40, Opus 74/80 + 36/40). The bar is a double pass (≥ 68/80 and ≥ 34/40, both reviewers, same version); glance physics (criterion 10) and time-to-value (criterion 7) stay at 8 for both reviewers until the mounted glance test (R14) and a timed install exist. **Round 14:** a blind template test (§9j) against 12 generic chips and 4 generic Hoy screens from fresh models chose the chip "la regla" (the best Uber-class rank, 0/6 template votes) and Hoy v5 (Uber-class 8.1, the best of 7); the reviewers then split — Opus PASS (74/80 + 35/40), Codex NOT_YET (71/80 + 34/40, glance physics 7). **Rounds 15–16:** with the host's fare in the crop, two blind rounds replaced the moving band with **la regla v16** — first of 11 on Uber-class (2.8), in the glance lead group (3.5 against the generic leaders' 3.2–3.3), template 1/6 — at the cost of distinctiveness (5.8 against 7.5). **Round 15** re-scored this version: **both PASS on both rubrics** — Codex 71/80 + 34/40 (glance physics back to 8), Opus 74/80 + 34/40 (signature 9 → 8; craft exactly at the bar, so any one-point loss on criteria 9–12 turns it NOT_YET) — and the Round 15b residual pass applied both lists (§9k). The final whole-product board against Uber Driver: mean Uber-class 6.5, simplicity 6.7, coherence 7.8 (§10). The mounted test (R14, on two host geometries) decides the glance. **Round 17** (debate `240`–`242` and a six-judge usability walkthrough: mean ease 5.5/7, ease against Uber 6.5/10; six of six judges hit the two meanings of "Te quedan") unified the money definition, cut the second tier, kept the 72sp display for the day, named the overlay "semáforo" everywhere and drew Kompara's own icons (§9l); its re-score split the reviewers — Opus PASS 74/80 + 34/40, Codex NOT_YET 67/80 + 34/40 (consistency 7: A1's 40dp legend rows) — while the board rose to a mean Uber-class 6.8 and simplicity 7.1 and the walkthrough's second run held at 5.5/7 (§10). **Round 18** answered both lists and the walkthrough's three weak tasks — Ajustes behind a gear, Hoy's status row as the door to Semáforo and the limits, a drawn waiting fix (B7) and negative day (B8, E1c), a shorter first run, A1's 48dp legend (§9m); its re-score passed both reviewers on both rubrics — **Codex 70/80 + 34/40, Opus 76/80 + 34/40** — while the walkthrough's third run measured mean ease 5.6/7 (ease against Uber 6.7/10; the first day 4.7, a stopped overlay 5.2, changing tu mínimo 5.3), short of the gate in §11 R16 (§10). **Round 19** drew what both reviewers and run 3's judges named — the door that opens what its line names, the Semáforo screen in its three states, an offer without a semáforo on Hoy, G2 at rest, D1 newest-first, the week's ledger, the Android hand-off, the basis line "ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche" and the rate on Hoy's offer rows (§9n); its re-score — **Opus 76/80 + 34/40 PASS; Codex 70/80 + 34/40 NOT_YET** on one craft hard fail, A3b's K tile; both NOT_YET on "comparable in use" — and the walkthrough's fourth run (≈5.4/7; runs 1–4: 5.48, 5.50, 5.60, ≈5.4, a plateau) led to **Round 19b** (§9o): D1's newest rows with their own rates, G4d en pausa, G4c's action line and one name for the cause, A3b's unboxed K, A1's "neto", basis lines without "según el lector" and G4b's clock. Opus's call after run 4: further static frames will not close the gate; the next evidence is a clickable first run with real drivers and the mounted glance test (§10, §11 R14, R16). Round 19b's confirmation passed both reviewers on both rubrics (**Codex 71/80 + 34/40, Opus 75/80 + 34/40**), and the **pre-test** pass (§9p) removed what would contaminate that driver test: one door to Semáforo from every Hoy state but the off one, Semáforo preparado drawn (G4e), Hoy at 14:40 drilling into Tu día and Cómo se calculó at 14:40 (D1a, D3a), each offer opening its own detail (D2b, D2c), one outage start (B2 "desde ayer 20:31"), C6's three lines on a 72dp tab, G4c's one action line and the prototype's live limits; its fixes (§9p) drew what those doors and outages still lacked — G4f (B4's), G4g (B6's), D1b (B9's Tu día), C13 (the shift with the permission lost, task 7's start) and G2d (the number entry); a second batch, from an independent check, drew D2d (B9's offer detail) and H6b (the next morning's shade, which task 7 reaches from C13). **Round 20** confirmed that kit on both rubrics (**Codex 71/80 + 34/40, Opus 76/80 + 34/40**) and found it not ready as written for three blockers — the answers on screen, a 21:10 month under a 14:40 clock, three disagreeing decision rules — now removed by the prototype's participant mode and facilitator window, the month read at 14:40 ($11,692), one pre-registered rule and task 9; the off state's Semáforo and Ajustes (G4h, G1b), D1a's whole list to 8:15 and "a esa hora" on the offer details came with them (§9q). **Round 21** answered walkthrough run 5, the first on the clickable prototype — mean ease **5.83/7**, off the plateau, ease against Uber 7.0/10, every answer screen reached, but changing tu mínimo and the first day at 5.00, so the gate is not met — with both Round 21 proposals (Codex `301`, Opus `302`): the limits editor in $0.50 steps, filled fields typed in place, a save that returns to its opener under a snackbar naming the new value and a dialog for an unsaved change; one permission screen with Android's question as a tagged example, its not-granted return, a stand-in for Android in the prototype and A2's own Uber y DiDi line; "Llevas" for open periods, position words for every colour word, worded period steps, "Uber reporta $34 más de lo que leímos", the car named where a number leaves it out (D2, F1), "No pudimos leer la tarifa" and B2's cause with "Que no vuelva a pasar"; the amarillo phrase and Hoy's door unchanged (§9r). **Round 21b** fixed what an independent check found before run 6 — A4b for B2's battery step, Guardar fixed in place, one snackbar rule, la regla holding the last valid pair, the ▾ that opens Elegir periodo again — and the harness now prints the snackbar for run 6 (§9r). **Walkthrough run 6** rose to 5.93/7 (tu mínimo 5.00 → 6.00, the first day 5.00 → 5.50; against Uber 7.0/10, understand 7.2/10; gate not met), with one unanimous finding, E1's reconciliation row; **Round 22** moved it to its own sheet behind "Cuadrar con tu resumen de Uber", put the per-app answer one row from the week and said "te deja por km" over Tu día's list (§9s). **Walkthrough run 7** held at 5.94/7 (against Uber 7.3/10, understand 7.3/10; gate not met), with the trip's money beside the day's in the top three for four judges in both run 6 and run 7; **Round 23** put the day after the car on every offer detail, made the amarillo phrase "Cumple tu mínimo", drew the return with the permission (B2r) and the Por app sheet (E1f), and, by both reviewers' rule, allows one more model run (run 8, judged on Opus's six readiness criteria instead of the mean), after which the evidence is real drivers (§9t). **Walkthrough run 8**, on the corrected instrument, met the walkthrough gate for the first time — 6.17/7, against Uber 7.5/10, understand 7.5/10, no task under 5.67, 54/54 answer screens — and five of Opus's six readiness criteria; it missed the one on a repeated defect, the basis of a figure without the car said only in small grey text away from it (run 7: j1, j2, j3, j5; run 8: j2, j3, j5, j6), so **Round 24** puts "sin el coche" right after every such number, on its line, and renames Semáforo's count row (§9u). **The final verdicts** pass both rubrics again — Codex 71/80 + 34/40, Opus 76/80 + 34/40, no hard fail (`321`, `322`) — and **Round 24c** applies their blockers: task 9 asks for the last finished trip, whose detail is drawn (D2e), the plan's D2 probe is neutral and task 3's success is plain; with G4's zero row gone, the first day's «Ahora no» on Hoy (B3n), the basis said once per sheet and the fixture's day all Uber (§9v). **Round 24d** applies the independent check of that version: the trip still under way at 14:40 says so on its row («En curso») and in the day's basis («6 viajes tomados (1 en curso),»), " a esa hora" follows the limit its line names, and the plan's probe runs on D2e only (§9w). **Round 24e** closes §9w's open items but the first day's Ajustes, which waits on a design decision (§9x).

## 1. What is wrong today (diagnosis)

Ground truth: the as-built inventory (27 screens, 35 surfaces, 465 strings) in [`debate/00-inventory-as-built.md`](ux-elevation/debate/00-inventory-as-built.md). Ranked by damage; both reviewers independently found every item below.

| # | Problem | Evidence (real copy / behaviour) | Why it hurts |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **A reader failure reads as "reject".** | Level rule: "A missing fare means red." Hint "Sin tarifa" is 10sp white on yellow. | The single most important pixel lies. Drivers lose good trips or stop trusting every colour. |
| 2 | **"Neto" means three different things.** | Tus costos: "ganancia neta de verdad" (after costs, but rent/insurance are collected and ignored). Fiscal: "tu neto aparecerá igual al bruto" (after commission). Inicio: "GANANCIA NETA ESTA SEMANA". | The central promise can't be audited. |
| 3 | **Setup declares success before the reader works.** | Done screen: "Kompara ya está leyendo tus ofertas" — screen capture hasn't started; Lector shows a static fake "Oferta entrante · $148". | After an alarming permission, no chip on the first offer is a 5-minute uninstall. |
| 4 | **Ten screens before value; account before value.** | Pitch ×3 ("Gana hasta 30% más por turno") → Teléfono → Código → Perfil → Divulgación → Activa → ¡Listo! → OEM (inert switch) → ¡Todo listo! → lands on the Simulator. | Motivation is spent before the accessibility cliff. |
| 5 | **Two readers, two consents, one of them untruthful.** | "Lector para DiDi · Iniciar", a second "Kompara leerá tu pantalla" dialog; the main disclosure says only "textos de la oferta", not screen images; on API 30+ the button is a no-op. | Trust gap and a Play-review risk at once. |
| 6 | **Five tabs that mirror modules, not the day.** | Inicio · Comparar · Lector · Fiscal · Ajustes; Historial is "Reached from: Ajustes only"; the Simulator is reachable from four places. | The driver can't predict where "mi semana" lives. |
| 7 | **Number grids instead of heroes.** | Inicio: up to 10 blocks; Comparar: "Métrica · Tú · Uber · DiDi · Lugar" × 6 at 12sp; Fiscal: 12 blocks, half legal text; Resumen: 5 rows + 5 cards. | Fails the 1-second glance on every data screen. |
| 8 | **Standings on sample data, shared virally.** | "Le ganas al 78% de los conductores de tu ciudad" with optional "Datos de muestra"; blended "Tú" ranked against one platform; share caption "Top 22% en CDMX 🚀". | The first WhatsApp group that compares notes discredits the brand. |
| 9 | **Coverage certified.** | "Cubierto este mes", notification "Uber: cubierto por el IMSS", then "Estas son estimaciones…" at the bottom. | The app cannot know enrollment; the headline and the footnote contradict. |
| 10 | **Paywall that blanks, dead-ends and surprises.** | Fiscal fully blurred; week summary 🔒 with no CTA; paying users see "Importa una semana para desbloquear"; footer "pendientes de revisión legal"; Ayuda promises "sin pedir tarjeta". | Hostage-taking over the driver's own data; placeholder legal copy at the moment of payment. |
| 11 | **Promises and actions that don't exist.** | "registrar viajes a mano", "activar el lector desde Ajustes", "botón de reporte", a consejo saying "…para que la app filtre…" (read-only app). | Each broken promise taxes the chip's credibility; "filtre" contradicts the Play posture in a quotable string. |
| 12 | **Three vocabularies for one verdict; jargon.** | "Conviene/Regular/No conviene" (simulator) vs "Verde/Amarillo/Rojo" (chip) vs "veredicto/semáforo/mínimo" (prose); "Umbral", "CSF", "ISR", "Tú = tu mezcla de apps". | Every new noun is a thing to learn; paragraphs get skipped. |
| 13 | **Sliders over a live 8–20 s offer; silent edits.** | Long-press sheet with two sliders that save on release; the Simulator's slider "persists the real green floor". | Fine motor control in a moving car; settings change without the driver knowing. |
| 14 | **No navigation chrome; the same data drawn four ways; surfaces disagree.** | No back button on pushed screens; bottom bar on Paywall/Import; share is "always the month card"; preview ≠ shared PNG; "Semana del 8 jun" vs "Semana del 8–14 jun". | Reads as amateur; alone rules out "Uber-class". |
| 15 | **Inert inputs, missing entry points, blank loading.** | Rent/insurance feed `dailyFixedCosts` that nothing reads; no UI to set the weekly goal; the OEM switch is wired to nothing; Inicio/Fiscal/Historial load blank. | Wasted effort and screens that look crashed on a cheap phone. |

**The 5-minute uninstall path today:** swipe three text pages → hand over the WhatsApp number → type a code → pick a city → read a legal scroll → leave for Android Accessibility → follow OEM steps → "¡Todo listo!" into a simulator → open Uber → first offer's fare is misread → **the chip turns red** next to Uber's own gross $/km badge → uninstall.

## 2. The elevated concept

**Promise (es-MX, in the driver's words):** «Antes de aceptar, veo si el viaje me deja de verdad. Al cerrar el día sé cuánto me quedó, y al cerrar la semana sé cómo voy.»
**In-product line:** **"Ve si el viaje te deja, antes de aceptarlo."**
**Promise (English, for us):** at the offer, one glance says whether the trip pays *after the driver's own costs*; afterwards the app shows what they kept and, when real data exists, how they compare. It never touches the ride app and screen content never leaves the phone.

### The driver's day

| Moment | Question | Kompara does | Surface |
|---|---|---|---|
| Before the shift | "¿Ya funciona?" | Shows real readiness: *preparado* (permission on, nothing read yet) vs *funcionando* (a real offer was read), per app. | Hoy status · Semáforo (G4) |
| **The offer (8–20 s)** — moment 1 | "¿Me conviene con mis costos?" | One stable rate + colour + shape + the position phrase in < 150 ms, or an honest "sin lectura". Never acts. | Chip |
| During the trip | — | **Nothing.** Silent while a passenger is in the car. | — |
| Between trips (parked) | "¿Cómo voy hoy?" | Today's result and the last offers, as proof the reader is reading. | Hoy |
| **End of the day** — moment 2 | "¿Cuánto me quedó?" | One quiet summary; the day, offer by offer. | Notification → Tu día |
| **Week close (Monday)** — moment 3 | "¿Fue buena semana? ¿Cómo voy contra los demás?" | The week as a timeline, one comparison, one share, the trial invitation. | Notification → Ganancias → Comparar → Compartir |
| Month | "¿Ya llegué al monto del IMSS? ¿Qué le paso a mi contador?" | A factual monthly review per app; a PDF for the accountant (Premium). | Ganancias › Mes → IMSS |

Build around **the offer, the day, the week.** Readiness is the prerequisite for all three.

### The signature

*Your two limits are two labelled lines on the plate; the trip's marker sits above them, between them or below them, and the band says where in words.* "La regla" is the one visual idea that is Kompara's: the product's central fact (net after *your* costs against *your* meta and *your* mínimo) drawn as two lines the trip is positioned against. Other reader apps also judge an offer's net against the driver's own threshold; the distinction is the form, not the arithmetic. Up means more money. Since Round 16 the colour band is fixed and reads first — a solid 30dp ▲ ◆ ▼, the rate and the position phrase ("Arriba de tu meta": a place against the driver's own limits, never a verdict word) — and the tray under it keeps the two lines with the marker; no brand strip, no traffic light, no gauge, no coloured rounded card. The chip is the signature at full size (§6); the same object carries through the app at smaller scales, and where the app shows quantities it draws them to scale instead (Round 15: six of six judges asked for comparisons drawn to scale):

- **Hoy:** the semáforo's status is words led by a glyph — a green dot when it works and nothing needs the driver ("Semáforo funcionando", the one non-verdict use of verde: "it works"), an ink status glyph otherwise (§4, Round 17: a ring with "!" when a fix waits, B7/G1/G4b · a hollow ring for preparado · a slashed ring for apagado · pause bars for en pausa) — and, since Round 18, a door into the Semáforo screen in its state, whose first row is the two limits its sub-line shows while nothing waits ("tu meta $8 · tu mínimo $6"; the pre-test reverted Round 19's door straight to the limits after walkthrough run 4); offer rows are led by the mark (§4), with the offer's time and meaning in the chip's own words under the outcome ("14:32 · Arriba de tu meta") and its net rate at the right (Round 19).
- **Tu día:** Hoy's anatomy — the day's figure, one observation led by the verde mark ("2 ofertas arriba de tu meta se fueron sin tomar"; Round 21: position words, never a colour word), offer rows led by the mark; the hours live in the quiet line, and the shift timeline is gone (Round 17: shift money would not add up to a day after the car). No invented offers (the fixture has four).
- **Ganancias · Semana:** the figure stands beside its bars (uber-5's layout, Round 17): seven day bars to scale in the quiet `--rule` fill, each day after its car share, so the bars add up to the figure, and the best day is the one ink bar with its figure above it ($1,166). **The dashed car-cost line retired in Round 17 and no longer carries the signature:** it was a patch for bars drawn before the car under a hero drawn after it (Opus R12-01), and board judges still read "the bars sum to $6,480, the hero says $4,980"; with one money definition the bars and the figure share one basis, and the car's $214 is said in words — E8's row "Cada día se restan $214 del coche", G5 and Cómo se calculó (E1, E7b, E8, N4). **Mes** draws the same grammar: four 16dp week bars on the 1dp $0 line, the best week in ink with its figure (E3, E3c, N5). The signature lives in the chip, the mark, A1's legend and G2.
- **Comparar:** the finding as one sentence with its figure inside (Round 17: "DiDi te dejó $1.21 más por km", "Te pagan más por viaje que al 78 % de los de Uber en CDMX"), over two proportional bars on one scale (`.cbar`) — "DiDi $10.34/km" over "Uber $9.13/km" on Tus apps, "Tú $149/viaje" over "Promedio $131/viaje" on Tu ciudad, "Tú $120/viaje" over "Tú · 5 – 11 oct $107/viaje" on F4 — the higher figure first at 100 %, the ink bar the higher app or "Tú", the other in `--ink-3`, so the gap reads as a length; no mark, because the semáforo glyph means a verdict, never an app or a person. The share card carries the same bars when its amounts are shown (H3), so the comparison leaves the app as the same object; with amounts hidden it draws no bars, because Tú's length against the public $131 average would give the amount away. The salario mínimo on E5 is a solid 2dp reference line with its two-word caption.
- **Tu meta y tu mínimo:** la regla itself at 1:1, replaying an offer above the two limit controls, "Tu meta · ▲ desde" and "Tu mínimo · ▼ abajo de" (Round 21: the chip's cues, never a colour word) — the lines the driver edits (typed in a filled field or stepped by $0.50) are the chip's own lines, and the band's colour, cue and phrase and the marker change as they do (G2).
- **Demo:** the legend — one column of four 48dp rows (Round 18), each state's mark, the chip's cue in its band colour and the phrase on one line — is the state selector (A1), stepped through once on first view (Round 17: the 2 × 2 grid's filled cell read as a selected pill).
- **Brand:** the K square is gone. In product the K is an unboxed Barlow Semi Condensed 700 letter in verde (the demo's wordmark, the share card, the notification icon — Round 19b: never a K tile; never on the chip; A3b, whose example of Android's Accessibility list showed it as the app's icon, was merged into A3 in Round 21). The K-raya launcher mark (a K whose arms spring from a horizontal bar) is a founder fork (§9g).

### What we take from the Uber Driver app (structure, not brand)

- **Status-first home.** Uber's home answers "am I online?" before anything else. Hoy answers "¿funciona el semáforo?" and, when the answer is no, turns into one ink cause and one big button — our "GO" (B2).
- **One hero number.** Today's result at 72sp, alone — the display step is the day's; Ganancias sets its figure beside its bars, as uber-5 does (Round 17).
- **Earnings as a timeline.** Week ↔ month ↔ day ↔ trip, with a 7-bar chart and arrows; one screen serves every week.
- **Trip-card discipline.** Every offer: time · rate · verdict · what you did. Detail one tap deeper, in a sheet.
- **Settings tucked behind one top-bar glyph** — a gear named Ajustes (Round 18; Uber's Account slot) — each row showing its current value.
- **One top-app-bar pattern** on every drill-in; full-screen tasks without the bottom bar.
- **Quiet notifications**, only for events the driver would want.

**What we do not copy:** a map (no location, no duplication of the ride app); a fake GO toggle (the real switch is Android Accessibility); an inbox/promotions feed; quests, streaks or "maneja más" pressure; any "Aceptar/Rechazar" control or label; Uber's palette or marks.

### What we cut

Lector tab · Fiscal tab · Ajustes tab · Historial screen · Resumen de la semana · Simulador as a destination (its chip preview lives in Tu meta y tu mínimo) · the 3-page pitch and the "30 %" claim · Signup Perfil · "¡Listo!" and "¡Todo listo!" screens · the second "Kompara leerá tu pantalla" dialog on API 30+ · the Lector fake offer · the 2×2 grid and its pills · the Inicio paywall tease · platform chips · consejos that coach or blame · the streak · the goal bar (replaced by a real weekly deduction) · the 5-column table and its legend · "Dónde puedes ganar más" · "Pronto:" footnotes · "Cubierto / En camino / Difícil" and every coverage verdict · the 7 legal paragraphs · the "$200 más a la semana" line · "pendientes de revisión legal" · the quick-sheet sliders · the emerald brand strip on the chip · the inert OEM switch and the second notification ask · the double AI parse and the 4-card platform picker in Importar · preview ≠ PNG · 43 + 12 dead strings, `HomeUiText.kt`, `HomeViewModel.kt`, the second verdict palette in `overlay/VerdictColors.kt` · in Round 17: Hoy's observation row, the dashed car line, the shift timeline, the best-day and best-week rows, Semana's "Tu lugar entre los de Uber en CDMX", A1's summary line and its 2 × 2 cells, the Material icon set · in Round 18: the person icon and the name "Cuenta" (now a gear and "Ajustes"), the cost question and the WhatsApp number in the first run (now B4 and Ajustes), E3d's whole-week $774, G2's 20-word helper, the word "detenido".

---

## 3. Information architecture

### Navigation

| # | Tab | Icon | One job |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Hoy** | a drawn house (`i-home`) | Is the semáforo working, what have I kept today, what happened with my offers. |
| 2 | **Ganancias** | three drawn bars on a 2dp baseline (`i-bar`) | My money over time: week ↔ month ↔ day ↔ offer. |
| 3 | **Comparar** | two drawn horizontal bars, the top one longer (`i-cmp`, the `.cbar` object; `compare_arrows` read as a transfer) | My apps against each other, or me against drivers of my city — one metric at a time. |
| — | **Ajustes** | a bare 24dp drawn gear (`i-gear`; accessible name "Ajustes"), top-right of every tab root (Round 18; "Cuenta" behind a person icon until then) | The number, plan, tu meta y tu mínimo, costs, lo del coche, the semáforo's status, notifications, invites, help, data. |

**Why Comparar and not Ajustes as the third tab:** the founder's locked principle (Inicio = individual, Comparar = comparison), Comparar is the visible paid destination, and Codex's "Tus apps" segment makes it useful before any city cohort exists. Uber's Account slot is honoured by the always-visible gear — named Ajustes since Round 18, because the walkthrough's judges did not look for settings under an unlabelled person icon. (Fork F1; both reviewers ended here.)

**Chrome rules (v3 — paper, ink and the raya)**
- Tab roots: a 72dp top bar with the 30sp root title (money face 700) at the left ("Hoy", "Ganancias", "Comparar"; on Comparar the scope **text tabs** "Tus apps | Tu ciudad" are a 16sp tab row under the title, the Ganancias grammar, each tab reserving its 600 width so switching never shifts the row) and, at the right, at most one action icon plus the settings control — a bare 24dp gear (`i-gear`, solid with its hub knocked out; never in a ring) that opens Ajustes; the share icon sits beside it where a period can be shared. **No "K Kompara" wordmark bar** in the app proper: the K (an unboxed Barlow Semi Condensed 700 letter in verde) appears only on the demo's first screen, the share card, the notification icon (unboxed; Round 19b — A3b's example of Android's list, which also showed it, was merged into A3 in Round 21).
- Share is **a share icon in the top bar** (24dp, `--ink-2`), never a button on the surface: on every Ganancias frame and on Comparar › Tu ciudad when a standing exists (F2, F3); absent on Hoy, Tus apps (F1), F4 and F5.
- Drill-ins: a 64dp top bar with ← (48dp target) and a 20sp title, at most one trailing control (IMSS del mes chooses its month with the Ganancias period stepper under the bar, `‹ Octubre · en curso ›`, not a top-bar dropdown; on Tu día the title "Tu día" stays and the day stepper sits as a cluster at the right of the same row). Full-screen tasks entered from a flow — Premium, Compartir, Importar's steps — use ✕ in the same slot; Tu plan (H2–H2d) and Corregir (H5d) keep ←. **Every H task names its flow as the 20sp top-bar title** — "Premium" (H1, H1b), "Tu plan" (H2, H2b, H2c, H2d), "Compartir" (H3), "Importar" (H4–H5e) — and the content opens with the screen's own hero or its 24/30 700 money-face headline; there are no 13sp flow labels.
- Selectors are **text tabs** (active `--ink` 600 with the 24 × 2dp underline, inactive `--ink-2`, unavailable `--ink-3`), the **period stepper** (Round 21: the period on its own 16sp line, `12 – 18 oct · semana pasada`, over one 48dp row of two worded steps, `‹ Semana anterior` · `Semana siguiente ›` — `‹ Mes anterior` · `Mes siguiente ›` on Mes and on IMSS del mes's month selector — the step that cannot move dimmed to `--ink-3` and inert, siguiente on the current period; run 5's judges hesitated over the bare ‹ ›; since Round 21b the period line ends in a small `--ink-2` ▾ and opens the Elegir periodo sheet, E6, and the build reads the period first, then the two steps, whatever the markup order, §9r) and **context selectors** that open choice sheets (on Comparar the hero's label is the selector, `.hero .label.sel`: a 16sp `--ink-2` label across a 48dp row with a 24dp caret at its right end — `DiDi y Uber · Por km · aprox. ▾` on Tus apps, `Uber · CDMX · Por viaje ▾` on every Tu ciudad state, with or without a standing, so confirming a week never moves the selector; a standing sentence is plain text, never a selector). No segmented controls, no pills, no chips, no filter capsules.
- The semáforo's state in the chrome is a **status line**: 16sp 600 words led by a 16dp status glyph (Round 17; the walkthrough could not decode three dash patterns) — `Semáforo funcionando` by a 10dp green dot with a soft halo (the one non-verdict use of verde: "it works", and only while nothing needs the driver), a working semáforo with a fix waiting by a ring with "!" that leads with the problem ("Batería restringida · aún funciona", B7, G1, G4b), `Semáforo preparado` by a hollow ring, `Semáforo apagado` by a slashed ring, `Semáforo en pausa` by two pause bars — with the time as a 400 `--ink-2` suffix ("· desde ayer 20:31", "· última lectura ayer 20:10"). **On Hoy the status is a door** (Round 18; the walkthrough could not reach the limits or a missing overlay's cause from Hoy) **into the Semáforo screen in its state, on every Hoy state but the off one** (the pre-test: Round 19 had sent the healthy door to the limits it shows, G2b, and four of six run-4 judges still named it — found neither where they looked for the limits nor where they expected the status; Opus `282`): one 48dp row — the glyph and the state words over a 15sp `--ink-2` sub-line, and a chevron. While nothing waits, the sub-line is the two limits, "tu meta $8 · tu mínimo $6", and the door opens Semáforo working (G4; B1, B8, N1; B6's partial day opens it solo Uber, G4g, since the pre-test fixes), whose first row is those limits (→ Tu meta y tu mínimo at rest, G2b: two taps from Hoy); when something waits, the head or the sub-line names it and the door opens Semáforo in that state — the battery fix (G4b, from B7), an offer drawn without a chip (G4c, from B9's "no cupo el semáforo en 1 oferta"; Round 24), the pause (G4d, from B5), preparado with the permission just granted (G4e, from B3's "✓ permiso listo") or the morning after, with its proof of life (G4f, from B4's "última lectura ayer 20:10"; the pre-test fixes). Only the off state keeps a plain line, because B2's cause and primary are the explanation; its Semáforo screen is reached from Ajustes' row in that state instead (G1b → G4h, Round 20). One glyph, one meaning, app-wide (§4): a check for a fact about data or a step done ("Semana confirmada con mi resumen", H3; "Leída", H5c; "Lectura comprobada", G4; "permiso listo" on B3's door, "Permiso listo" on A3's return — Round 21: B3's headline then opens on "Listo."; `.status.fact`), a hollow ring for waiting (H2d), a slashed ring for off or a problem (H2c, H5c's "Falta", H5e, B6's "Por ahora no leemos DiDi"). Glyphs are ink; no other hue.
- The bottom bar (80dp, a 1dp `--rule` on top, three tabs of a 24dp drawn icon over a 13sp label; active `--ink` 600, inactive labels `--ink-2` over `--ink-3` icons; the active tab carries a 3dp ink indicator on its top edge) shows on tab roots and on Tu día (the day drill-in that keeps the Hoy context, including under its sheets D2 and D3). IMSS del mes and Ajustes are drill-ins with the back arrow and no bar. **Hidden on tasks and editors:** Premium, Tu plan, Importar, Compartir, Ajustes and its editors, signup, onboarding (which carries a 2dp ink progress bar under the top bar instead).
- Sheets are the only elevated surface besides the chip and snackbars: paper, top radius 16, a 32 × 4dp handle, a 20sp title, a 35 % dim behind; a ledger inside a sheet is 48dp `label · value` rows on hairlines with the total standing on a 2dp ink rule (the sheet's raya). Notices on a surface are **ink notes** (a 2dp dashed `--ink-3` rule over the words, E7b); the one sentence that qualifies a primary — trial terms (H1), a consent (H4), a consequence (H5b) — is a 13sp ink line on the gutter 16dp above it (the footer grammar), never a band or a left-ruled notice; a tease is words on the paper plus the one primary (E2, F3) — no band, no fill; toasts are ink on paper.
- The selected week is **shared** by Ganancias and Comparar; switching tabs never loses it. Comparar is weekly only: entering it from a month shows that month's last closed week, visibly labelled.
- Re-tapping the active tab pops to its root.
- Depth: the frequent destinations (today, any day, any week or month, the comparison, the semáforo's status, tu meta y tu mínimo, costs) are within two taps of a root; optional detail (Datos guardados, Cómo se calculó, De dónde salen estos datos) sits one deeper by design.

```
Hoy ─────── Tu día at that minute ("Ver todas las ofertas de hoy" · any offer row) ── Detalle de oferta (sheet, the tapped offer's own) ── Cómo se calculó (sheet)
 └ status row (a door, every state but off) ─ Semáforo in its state (Tu meta y tu mínimo › first · its fix · per-app status
                                              · No cupo el semáforo → Tu día · ¿No ves el semáforo? → Ayuda · Pausar)
Ganancias [Semana | Mes · text tabs] ── Elegir periodo (sheet · Traer un resumen)
 ├ Semana: the quiet line → Cómo se calculó · semana · "Cuadrar con tu resumen de Uber" → sheet (Tu resumen de Uber, E1e; Traer otro resumen) · Por día → sheet · "Por app" (sub-line "DiDi te dejó $1.21 más por km") → Comparar · Tus apps (Round 22)
 │         · Por día (sheet; also any bar) → Tu día · open week: "Cada día se restan $214 del coche" → Lo del coche · share icon → Compartir
 └ Mes:    the quiet line → Cómo se calculó · Por semana (sheet; also any bar) → that week · IMSS del mes (── ¿Cómo funciona? sheet · Resumen del mes en PDF, Premium) · share icon → Compartir
Comparar [title · Tus apps | Tu ciudad · 16sp text tabs] · the hero's label selector DiDi y Uber · Por km · aprox. ▾ (Tus apps) / Uber · CDMX · Por viaje ▾ (Tu ciudad) → choice sheet
          · the finding's qualifier line → Datos (sheet: De dónde salen estos datos) · Aportar al promedio (sheet) · share icon (Tu ciudad)
Ajustes (gear) ── number row → Tu perfil (Cerrar sesión; until a number exists the row offers Entrar con WhatsApp, A6–A7) · Plan gratis → Premium / Tu plan · Tu meta y tu mínimo · Tus costos (── Combustible sheet)
               · Lo del coche (── sheet over Ajustes) · Semáforo (in its state, the off one included: G1b → G4h) · Avisos · Invita y gana · Ayuda · Tus datos (── Eliminar cuenta dialog)
Full-screen tasks: Premium · Importar · Compartir · Onboarding / Entrar (A1's "Recuperar mis datos")
Overlay: chip (drag only) · "Semáforo apagado" ink tab (C6; 5 s, non-interactive)
Notifications (H6, H6b): Se apagó el semáforo · Tu resumen de hoy está listo · Tu semana está lista · {Mes}: revisa tus registros del mes (IMSS) · Tu prueba termina
```

### Fate of every current surface

| Current surface | Fate | Where / why |
|---|---|---|
| Root LOADING | Keep, redesign | Visible skeleton of Hoy; never waits on the network. |
| Root ONBOARDING | Keep, shorten | §7. |
| Root AUTH | Merge → optional "Entrar con tu WhatsApp" | An expired session never disables the reader. |
| Wordmark top bar | Kill | Title + the gear (Ajustes). |
| 5-tab bar | Replace | Hoy · Ganancias · Comparar. |
| Pushed screens without Back | Kill pattern | Top app bar everywhere. |
| **Inicio** | Merge → **Hoy** | Today + readiness. Weekly hero → Ganancias; streak → kill; share → Ganancias; GoalBar → kill (replaced by "Lo del coche"); CostNudge → "con costos típicos" in the hero's quiet line + the one-time costs question on Hoy (B4: words on paper, one button); platform chips → Comparar › Tus apps; 2×2 grid + pills → kill; gate tease → kill; consejos ×3 → one factual observation; completeness note → Cómo se calculó / Tu día; empty state → first-run "preparado". |
| **Comparar** | Keep tab, rebuild | Tus apps / Tu ciudad, one metric; week pill → shared stepper; free hero kept, cohort-specific, real data only; 5-column table → aligned rows; "Dónde puedes ganar más", cross-link, "Pronto", single-app note → kill; 12 dead `comparar_*` strings → delete. |
| **Lector** | Merge → Hoy status + **Semáforo** drill-in (G4; renamed in Round 17) | Mock offer → kill; "Lector para DiDi" / Iniciar → kill on API 30+, a checklist row where capture consent is really needed; 4 buttons → checklist with one fix per row. |
| **Fiscal** | Merge → Ganancias › Mes › **IMSS del mes** + PDF | Tracker free and factual; coverage chips → kill; Resumen fiscal → Premium PDF "Resumen para tu contador"; explainers → 3-bullet sheet. |
| **Ajustes** | Keep the name, off the tab bar → **Ajustes** (the gear; "Cuenta" behind a person icon in Rounds 3–17) | Grouped rows with values; toggles → Avisos; debug Premium toggle stays debug-only. |
| **Tus costos** | Keep, simplified | 2 fields + maintenance + "Ya sé mi costo por km"; per-day rent/insurance → weekly "Lo del coche". |
| **Tu semáforo** | Keep, rebuilt → **Tu meta y tu mínimo** (Round 17) | Two limits (tu meta, tu mínimo), one control each, typed or stepped, la regla as the live preview (absorbs the Simulator), explicit save. |
| **Ayuda** | Keep | 5 short questions + "Escríbenos por WhatsApp"; false claims removed. |
| **Tu cuenta** | Keep → **Tu perfil** | Optional account; delete dialog + Play-subscription line. |
| **Historial** | Kill | Week stepper + Mes list. |
| **Resumen de la semana** | Merge → Ganancias · Semana | One week screen. |
| **Detalle del día** | Keep → **Tu día** | Any date, arrows; `day/{day}` finally used. |
| **Paywall** | Keep, rebuilt | Contextual title, honest trial, real legal links; + new **Tu plan**. |
| **Importar semana** | Keep, simplified | Detect → consent → parse once → review → save; share-sheet entry kept with the same consent. |
| **Invita y gana** | Keep under Ajustes | Days stated; redeem collapsed. |
| **Share card** | Keep → **Compartir** | Preview = PNG; period-correct; hero + 2 own-data facts; amounts hidden by default; no streak, no "mejor app". |
| **Simulador** | Kill as destination | Onboarding demo + the Tu meta y tu mínimo preview + Semáforo "Ver ejemplo"; the playground that persisted real settings is gone. |
| Pitch ×3 | Merge → Demo (1 screen) | |
| Signup Teléfono / Código | Keep, optional, last onboarding step | Re-offered with the trial. |
| Signup Perfil | Kill | City asked when a comparison needs it. |
| Divulgación | Keep, rewritten | Qué lee / Qué guarda / Qué hace / Tú decides; covers screen images. |
| Pantalla limitada | Kill → Hoy "Semáforo apagado" | No punitive detour, no manual-entry promise. |
| Activa (waiting) | Keep | Tries the direct route to Kompara's own toggle when the device supports it (capability-tested, never assumed from the API level); the steps cover the list fallback. |
| Activa (granted) | Merge → "Lector preparado", auto-advance | |
| OEM kit | Keep, conditional, one ask, skippable | Only when a restriction is detected; the rest under Lector › Batería. |
| ¡Todo listo! | Kill | |
| Verdict chip | Keep, redesigned | §6. |
| Quick sheet | **Kill** | The overlay editor could only open during a live offer; thresholds are edited in the app (§5.14, §5.4). |
| Reader-down banner | Merge → a bounded, non-interactive "Semáforo apagado" ink tab (C6; 5 s, once per host foreground, touches pass through) + notification + Hoy's off state | |
| E1 Lector dialog | Keep only where capture consent is actually required | API < 30 or a disabled silent lane. |
| E3 `OcrConsentActivity` | Keep; return to a visible result | |
| E4 `ShareImportActivity` | Keep; opens Importar at the consent step | |
| Notif. watchdog + OCR stopped | Merge → "El lector necesita atención" naming the cause | |
| Notif. OCR foreground | Keep where required → "Lector de Kompara activo" + "Detener" | |
| Notif. IMSS month-end | Keep, factual | |
| Notif. week close | Keep → opens that week; invites the trial | |

## 4. Shared contract (every screen inherits this)

**Vocabulary — one word per concept**

| Concept | Word in the UI | Never |
|---|---|---|
| Period result | **"Llevas / Te quedaron"** — "Llevas" for an open period (hoy / esta semana / en octubre), "Te quedaron" for a closed one (Round 21: five of six run-5 judges read "Te quedan" as money still to come) — with "· aprox." in the label + one quiet line under the number — **a period's money after all your costs** (Round 17): gasolina and desgaste per km, and lo del coche by calendar day ($1,500 ÷ 7 = $214.29 from its dated start, today included and also on days not driven; never per trip, shift or app, never for future dates). Day, week and month use this one definition, computed at full precision and rounded once, so a day carries one figure on Hoy, Tu día, the open week and the month ($548 at 14:40, $1,026 at day end), and a week's days add up to the week, a stated rounding peso aside (E1b; read at 14:40, October's weeks and ledger foot exactly, so E3d and D3b carry no note since Round 20). Results stay before taxes; "all your costs" means the costs configured — with Lo del coche unset, the quiet line reads "ya restamos gasolina y desgaste". **Every "ayer" or "ese día" money line carries its date** (Round 23: run 7's j6 checked an undated "Ayer te quedaron $1,026" against Sunday 18 oct's bar): "Ayer, lunes 19 oct, te quedaron $1,026 aprox." (B2, B2r), "Ayer, domingo 18 oct, te quedaron $284 aprox." (B4), date and amount derived together; a status line's "desde ayer 20:31" or "última lectura ayer 20:10" is not a money line | "Ganancia neta", "de verdad"; "Te quedan" on an open period (read as money still to earn); a day on a second basis; "te quedan" before the car beside "te quedan" after it; an undated "Ayer te quedaron" |
| One trip or one app | **"Te deja / Te dejó"** — what one trip or one app leaves **after running costs only** (Round 17: a trip and a period never share a verb): D2 "Te dejó · aprox." over "$114 sin el coche" ("Te deja · aprox." while the trip is under way, "Te habría dejado · aprox." for an offer not taken, D2b; Round 21 named what it leaves out in the label, "Te dejó antes de lo del coche · aprox."; Round 24 says it right after the number, the basis rule below), F1 "DiDi te dejó $1.21 más por km", the promise "Ve si el viaje te deja" | "Te quedaron" for a trip or an app |
| Rate after running costs | **"neto"** — a rate after running costs, as the unit (`$8.40/km neto`; a list's column header says it in the trip's own verb, "te deja por km", Round 22, with the basis since Round 24, "te deja por km, sin el coche"); where a screen states a rate's basis in words it says what was taken off, "Ya restamos gasolina y desgaste; lo del coche cuesta igual en las dos apps." (F1; Round 19, one sentence since Round 24b; E1f, whose column note already says the basis, keeps only "Lo del coche cuesta igual en las dos apps." since Round 24c); the offer detail's sub-line no longer says it (Round 24: "$285/h · 24 min" — under "sin el coche", "neto ·" read as a second basis) | "neto" meaning after-commission or after the car; "ya sin…" for a basis (run 3's judges read it as something missing) |
| The car | **"lo del coche"** — the driver's weekly fixed cost, taken off every "te quedan" total by calendar day; **the chip, the offer verdict, the semáforo, "te deja" and every rate never include it** — a trip is judged by what it costs to drive, and the car costs the same whether he takes it or not ($1,500 ÷ 675.8 km would turn C1's $9.49 verde into $7.27 amarillo and make drivers reject trips that pay). D2 says it right after its figure, "$114 sin el coche" (Round 24; in its label, "antes de lo del coche", Rounds 21–23), and beside it, since Round 23, gives the day that does carry the car, "Llevas hoy, con el coche: $548 ›" (Round 24's words; "ya con lo del coche" in Round 23) over "Se resta por día ($214), no por viaje" (Round 21's 13sp note, "Lo del coche ($214 al día) se resta de tu día, no de cada viaje", until then; never a per-trip share of the car, §9t); F1 says why a rate leaves it out, "…; lo del coche cuesta igual en las dos apps." (Round 24b; "Lo del coche no entra: cuesta igual en las dos apps." until then), and E1f says only that, "Lo del coche cuesta igual en las dos apps." (Round 24c); G5 states the rule, "Se resta de cada día: $214, también los días que no manejas. Nunca del semáforo." | the car inside a rate or a verdict; the −$1,500 as one Sunday's cliff |
| A figure without the car | **A figure that leaves out the car says so once, where the eye lands** (the basis rule; Round 24 put it beside the number after run 8's j2, j3, j5 and j6 named a basis said only in small grey text away from its figure; Round 24c rewrote it to what the kit does, Opus 322 defect 4): **beside the number on a single figure** — an offer detail's `$114 sin el coche` (D2–D2e; money 500 `--ink-2` at 20sp on the 32sp figure's baseline); **in the column note over a list or table of rates** — Hoy's and Tu día's "te deja por km, sin el coche" (B1, B5–B9, D1, D1a, D1b), E1f's "te dejó por km, sin el coche" over its two rates, which then carry no suffix; **on the bar label in a chart** — F1's `$10.34/km sin el coche`; the chip's "/km neto" is defined once on A1 ("neto: ya restamos gasolina y desgaste"), because the 240 × 112 plate has no room for more. **Day, week and month totals say "ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche"** on their quiet line, and a total named beside a trip says its basis in the same words, "Llevas hoy, con el coche: $548" (D2's day row). Said once means once per figure: the rates on the line under an already labelled figure (D2's "$9.49/km", "$285/h") and a difference of two rates ("DiDi te dejó $1.21 más por km") carry none; figures before every cost keep "antes de gastos" (E1e, F2–F5) | the basis said twice for one figure (a column note and a suffix, E1f before Round 24c); the basis only in a grey note away from the number; "antes de lo del coche" (Rounds 21–23); a "con coche / sin coche" tag on every total (rejected in Round 23) |
| Money the app paid | **"Te pagó la app"** | "Bruto" |
| Commission | **"Comisión de la app"** | — |
| Verdict states | **verde / amarillo / rojo / gris** (the gris chip reads "Sin lectura" or the missing input; a gris chip never carries a peso rate — a computable secondary rate waits in Detalle de oferta (two judge lenses: any figure on a gris chip reads as a verdict)) | Conviene / Regular / No conviene / veredicto / "sin color" |
| The driver's two limits | **"tu meta"** — the green limit ($8.00/km, "▲ desde" on G2), the upper line · **"tu mínimo"** — the red limit ($6.00/km, "▼ abajo de" on G2), the lower line (Round 21: the chip's cues, never a colour word — run 5's colour-blind judge) (Round 14: the blind judges read the old pair, "tu mínimo" / "tu rojo", as backwards) | umbral, "tu rojo", "verde desde", "rojo abajo de", "tu mínimo" for the green limit, "debajo de" |
| An offer's meaning | **"Arriba de tu meta"** (verde) · **"Cumple tu mínimo"** (amarillo; Round 23: one comparison, true at exactly tu mínimo, where "Entre tu meta y tu mínimo" asked for two; in a sentence lower case, "cumple tu mínimo de $6.00/km") · **"Abajo de tu mínimo"** (rojo) · **"No pudimos leer la tarifa"** (gris in the log, or the missing input; Round 21, "Faltó la tarifa" until then) — the chip's own words: the position phrase in its band (gris says the missing input in the present, "Sin lectura · falta la tarifa"), under the outcome in every offer row, in A1's legend and in Ayuda | colour words anywhere ("Verde · la tomaste", "ofertas verdes", "verde desde"; Round 21), "llega a tu mínimo", "Supera tu mínimo" (false at exactly the minimum), "Entre tu meta y tu mínimo" (Round 23) |
| The overlay and its status | **"semáforo"** — the one word for the overlay and its status (Round 17: the walkthrough met "semáforo" in settings and help but "Lector" on Hoy): Semáforo **preparado / funcionando / apagado / en pausa** (the transient C6 ink tab says "Semáforo apagado" too since Round 18: "detenido" beside "apagado" was a near-synonym, Opus R17-R10), the drill-in "Semáforo" (G4) and Ajustes' row, "Reanudar el semáforo", "Si el semáforo se apaga" (G7), "Se apagó el semáforo" (H6). **"lector"** survives only as the source of a figure, said where the figure is explained, never on a basis line (Round 19b) — "Lo que leyó Kompara" in the discrepancy sheet (E1e, Round 22; the pre-test fixes took "según el lector" out of E1d's first row, which reads as D3's) and "Lector · aprox." (H5b); Round 20 took it off the last screens that named the product's reading by it — F4's qualifier reads "Según lo que registró Kompara de tus viajes" and E3b's and E6's September "antes de Kompara" | "Lector funcionando" and every "lector" for the overlay or its status, "lector de pantalla", "OCR", "captura" (except inside the disclosure), "encendido", "detenido" |
| The limits screen | **"Tu meta y tu mínimo"** — G2's title and G1's row ("$8.00/km · $6.00/km"), reached at rest (G2b) from Semáforo's limits row — the first row of the screen Hoy's status door opens, two taps from Hoy (the pre-test reverted Round 19's one-tap door) — from Ajustes, from B3's row and from an offer's "Cambiar tu meta y tu mínimo ›" (D2; "Ajustar mis límites" until Round 18); its Guardar returns to whichever opened it under a snackbar that names the new pair — Semáforo, Ajustes and B3 already show it, the offer detail keeps its verdict at that hour (G4i, Round 21; the wording Round 21b) | "Tu semáforo" as a screen name (the word is the overlay's) |
| Offer outcome | **"La tomaste · aprox."** (counts into "Llevas hoy · aprox." as an estimate of the offer's own figures); confirmation is never per offer (see the next row) / **"Se fue sin tomar"** (never counts) / **"Se canceló"** (removed from the total; set by the driver from the row — "¿Se canceló? Quitar de mi día" — or by an itemized statement that lacks it; an aggregate statement never cancels a trip) / **"Sin confirmar"** (an outcome the reader could not establish: shown, never totalled) | "rechazada", "ganancia" for an unconfirmed offer |
| Week confirmation | **"Semana confirmada con tu resumen"** — a week-level flag set when an aggregate statement confirms the week's payment; offer rows keep "La tomaste · aprox." | "Viaje confirmado", "pago confirmado" per trip |
| Period hero | **"Llevas hoy · aprox."** as the label ("Te quedaron esa semana · aprox." on a closed period; Round 21), the figure (the 72sp display on the day only) and **one quiet line** with the count and the deduction basis, "6 viajes tomados (1 en curso), ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche ›" (→ Cómo se calculó; a trip still under way is counted and said so, Round 24d) — the reader cannot see payment, so every total is an estimate of trips taken until an import confirms | a total that reads as money paid |
| Hours | **"en turno"** (online), defined once; trip time is "tiempo del viaje" | an unlabelled "h" |
| Tense | an open period says what has come in so far, "Llevas hoy / esta semana / en octubre" (Round 21); a closed one the past, "Te quedaron esa semana"; a trip "Te dejó" ("Te deja" while under way, "Te habría dejado" for an offer not taken) | "esta semana" on a closed week; "Te quedan" on an open one |
| Estimate | **"aprox."** in the hero label, said once per screen | "aprox." repeated on every line; "estimado" |
| Wear | **"desgaste"** (electric profiles: "energía y desgaste") | "mantenimiento" as a headline |
| Initial rule | **"valor inicial"** | "lo típico de CDMX" |
| The mark | **the mark** — la regla in miniature (16 × 24dp, radius 3, 1.5dp `--ink-2` outline, two 1dp `--ink-3` lines at y 8 and 14 — tu meta and tu mínimo; verde fills y 0–8, above both, amarillo y 9–14, between them, rojo y 15–24, below both; gris = dashed outline, no fill; 18 × 28dp in the `.of` offer rows), the only leading glyph in offer rows; never in Comparar, because the semáforo glyph means a verdict, never an app or a person (Round 15). Wireframes write it `[V] [A] [R] [G]`; CSS `.mark.v/.a/.r/.g` | ✓ ! ✕ ? glyphs, circled icons, coloured dots, colour words in rows ("Verde · la tomaste") |
| La regla | **"la regla"** (design vocabulary, never a UI string): the chip's two labelled lines — upper = tu meta, lower = tu mínimo — with the trip's marker above, between or below them, under the fixed band that says the same place with a cue and a phrase. In the UI the driver meets it as the two limits by name ("tu meta $8", "tu mínimo $6") and as the meanings ("Arriba de tu meta" …), taught by A1's labelled legend, and edited in Tu meta y tu mínimo (G2) | "umbral", "carril", "zona", a proportional gauge |

**The quiet line rule (Round 14; replaces the v3 qualifier row; its basis since Round 17, its words since Round 19).** Every period hero is the one root anatomy — **label · value · quiet line**: the 16sp label carries the scope and the estimate flag once ("Llevas hoy · aprox.", "Te quedaron esa semana · aprox.", "Llevas en octubre · aprox."; "Llevas" since Round 21), the value is the day's 72sp display on Hoy and Tu día or Ganancias' 40sp figure beside its bars, and directly beneath it (under the figure row on Ganancias) the quiet line (`.hero .sub2`: 16sp `--ink-2` in a 48dp row with a trailing 24dp chevron that **opens "Cómo se calculó"**, so provenance is one tap from every hero) says the count and the deduction basis in one sentence. The basis says in plain words what was taken off (Round 19: run 3's judges read "ya sin … ni coche" as "without a car"): with Lo del coche set it is "ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche", otherwise "ya restamos gasolina y desgaste" — the words a rate's basis line uses too, because a rate never includes the car (F1's one sentence since Round 24b, "Ya restamos gasolina y desgaste; lo del coche cuesta igual en las dos apps.", which also says why the car is left out). **One line where it fits;** two lines (`.sub2.two`) when the sentence must also carry the car clause or the hours — the car clause does not fit one line at 16sp: day `6 viajes tomados (1 en curso), / ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche` (B1, B7, B9, E8 at 14:40: a trip still under way is counted and said in parentheses, Round 24d, «(1 en curso)» held whole) · with hours `9 viajes tomados en 8 h 10 min, / ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche` (D1; D1a `6 viajes tomados en 4 h 50 min (1 en curso)`, the time on shift by 14:40; B5 `5 viajes tomados en 4 h 30 min`, B6 `3 viajes tomados en 2 h 20 min`) · a negative day adds the car's daily amount as a third line, `1 viaje tomado (en curso), / ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche / ($214 por día)` (B8, Round 18; «(en curso)» since Round 24d) · week `62 viajes tomados, / ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche` (E1, E2, E7b; Round 19b drops "según el lector", which six basis lines carried for three rounds: a period names its source where two sources meet — the discrepancy sheet and F4's contrast between sources (since the pre-test fixes E1d's first row reads as D3's, "antes de gasolina, desgaste y coche") — and a statement figure keeps its own words, "según tu resumen"; the car's daily figure is said in words once, by E8's row "Cada día se restan $214 del coche", and in G5 and the week's Cómo se calculó, E1d, whose ledger carries the −$1,500) · month `155 viajes tomados, / ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche` (E3, E3c; E3d's title line, "Lo que cae en octubre, ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche") · a month with only an imported week `29 viajes según tu resumen, sin km` (E3b; no "aprox." in its label — a statement figure is exact) · before costs are confirmed, "con costos típicos" replaces the deductions clause (a rule; no frame draws it — B4 asks the cost question instead). Comparar's heroes are findings: the sentence carries the figure and the qualifier line under it carries the source, its chevron opening Datos (§5.9). Costs are never unknown: they start as labelled typical values. Where two sources disagree, the other one has its own row, stated as a fact with its amount — E1 `Cuadrar con tu resumen de Uber ›`, whose sheet (E1e) gives the basis before both figures and their difference — `Solo Uber, antes de gastos` · `Lo que leyó Kompara $5,028` · `Lo que dice tu resumen $5,062` · `Diferencia +$34` — and says the week stays on reader data (Round 22: Round 21's row with both figures on the root stopped six of six run-6 judges); on F2 the qualifier names the source ("Según los resúmenes de 1,240 conductores"); every hero's Cómo se calculó opens on the count and the gross it rests on (no first row names the reader since the pre-test fixes). "aprox." appears once per screen, in the hero label. The Round 10 scope sentences and the v3 qualifier row stand replaced wherever an older note quotes them.

**Negative figures (Round 17).** A day can be negative once the car comes off it. **Before the first trip taken today, Hoy shows no figure:** it keeps its no-trips form — B2's off state, B4's costs question — with yesterday's result as a line and "Aún no registramos viajes hoy." under it, so the car's −$214 never stands alone at 7:30 ("aún no registramos viajes" does not establish that income was zero; the share still accrues in the calculation). From the first trip the hero shows the signed result — the fixture's 14:32 trip alone would read **−$100** (113.90 − 214.29) — as a true minus (U+2212) kerned to its $, **in ink at the display step, never rojo** (rojo is a verdict colour), **never "te faltan…"**, no progress bar and no "maneja más" pressure (the goal bar stays killed); never clamped to zero and never postponed until earnings cover the car. A past day with no trips reads "Te quedaron el domingo · aprox." −$214 over "Sin viajes; lo del coche corre igual ›", and its bar draws nothing above the $0 line (its figure lives in its Por día row, "Domingo 18 / Sin viajes; lo del coche corre igual" `−$214`). **Round 18 draws both as alternate scenarios, labelled above the phone like E3c** (Opus R17-R4): **B8**, Hoy at 14:40 on a day whose only trip is the 14:32 verde offer — `−$100` at the display step, its quiet line adding "($214 por día)", the status door and the one offer row, no grey button — and **E1c**, the Por día sheet if domingo 18 had had no trips (the week $4,481 behind it, the rounded days adding to it exactly); §11 R17 tests the reading.

**The cut rule (Round 17).** A screen is one hero block, at most one visual and one list. **Between the hero block and the list, a row survives only if it states a new fact with its amount** — one neither the hero nor the visual shows (E1's "Por app" over "DiDi te dejó $1.21 más por km", which reaches a tab's screen but carries its finding, so it is no bare link; "Cada día se restan $214 del coche") — **or a state the driver must know or act on** (a reader limitation: B6's "Por ahora no leemos DiDi"; since Round 18 a waiting fix leads the status door itself, B7, and since Round 19 an offer drawn without a chip is counted on the door's sub-line, B9, so neither needs a second row). Cut on sight: a past fact presented as news (B1's "Tu mejor hora hoy: 9 a 10 h" at 14:40), a sentence that restates the visual ("Tu mejor día fue el viernes" beside its ink bar, "La mejor fue 12 – 18 oct"), a bare link to a tab ("Tu lugar entre los de Uber en CDMX") and a second door to the same destination. A named route to a breakdown is not a restatement — "Por día ›" and "Por semana ›" name the sheet every bar also opens, D1's "2 ofertas arriba de tu meta se fueron sin tomar ›" opens a filtered list, and E1's "Cuadrar con tu resumen de Uber ›" opens the reconciliation Round 22 took off the root; a drill-in may hold the full list its task needs (D1's 13 offers); the rule never forces a visual onto a screen that has none (B2).

**The display step (Round 17).** The 72/74 700 display is reserved for **the day's figure** — Hoy (B1, B5–B9), Tu día (D1, also behind D2 and D3) and their night frames (N1, N3) — so each tab is recognisable by its shape: Hoy is a status and a big number, **Ganancias a figure beside its bars**, over its rows, **Comparar a sentence over two bars**. Every other screen leads with its own object: Ganancias' figure at 40sp; Comparar's finding at 24/30; a task hero (G3, H1, H2b, H5) as a 24/30 headline over its figure at the 32/36 number step; the share card (H3) with F2's sentence.

**Ganancias' figure beside its bars (Round 17, uber-5's layout).** The 16sp label, then one figure row (`.figrow`, ≥ 88dp): the figure at 40/44 700 in the money face on the left, its baseline on the chart's $0 line, and the period's bars on the right in a 168 × 72dp plot (`.spark.mini`: the best bar in ink with its figure 4dp above it, the others `--rule`, a 1dp `--ink-3` $0 line, the day letters or week labels under it); the quiet line runs full width under the row. The bars are on the figure's basis — each day after its car share — so they add up to it: no car line, no caption column. A locked older period keeps the plot's geometry with no bars and says the lock in words inside it (E2); a period with nothing dated draws no plot and sets its figure under its label (E3b).

**Comparar's finding sentence (Round 17).** Comparar leads with what the comparison found, as one 24/30 700 sentence in the money face with its figure inside — "DiDi te dejó $1.21 más por km" (F1), "Te pagan más por viaje que al 78 % de los de Uber en CDMX" (F2, F3) — or, where there is no finding, its cause ("Aún juntamos conductores de DiDi para compararte", F4; "Falta tu resumen de Uber para ver tu lugar", F5); under it one 16sp `--ink-2` qualifier line that says what the figure rests on and opens Datos ("Manejaste cada app a distintas horas ›" since Round 21, "Según los resúmenes de 1,240 conductores ›", "Según lo que registró Kompara de tus viajes ›", "Contamos 31 viajes ›"; Round 19b: F1 and F5 drop "según el lector", which repeated the source that "aprox." and "sin confirmar" already name; Round 20: F4's drops "lector" too, with Kompara as the subject, as F2's names the drivers' statements); then the bars and the basis line. A difference is never a bare display figure: four of six board judges read F1's 72sp "$1.21" as a rate or as earnings.

**Status glyphs (Round 17).** One glyph, one meaning, app-wide — ink, 16dp, beside words, never a verdict colour (`.status.ready / .off / .paused / .fact` in `base-v3.css`): the **green dot** — the semáforo works and nothing needs the driver (the one non-verdict verde); a **ring with "!"** — it works, but a fix waits (B7, G1, G4b; problem first; drawn inline until `base-v3.css` carries it); a **hollow ring** — waiting: preparado (B3, B4, A4, G4e, G4f, DiDi on G4), a pending purchase (H2d); a **slashed ring** — off or a problem: apagado (B2, A3), an app not read (B6, DiDi on G4g, inDrive on G4), a failed charge (H2c), a missing input (H5c's "Falta"), a failed upload (H5e); **pause bars** — en pausa (B5, G4d); a **check** — a fact about data ("Lectura comprobada", "Leída", "Semana confirmada con mi resumen") or a step just done ("permiso listo" on B3's door, lowercase like every door sub-line, and "Permiso listo" on A3's 800 ms return; Round 19). They replace the solid, broken and dashed 24 × 2dp segments, which the walkthrough's judges could not decode. **The status-ring family — dot, ring, slashed ring, pause, check and ring with "!" — is the one exception to "no circled glyphs"** (Round 18, Opus R17-R9): six glyphs, 16dp, always beside their words, only in status lines, Hoy's status door and per-app rows — never in an offer row (the mark's place), a nav slot, a button or a tile; every other circled glyph stays deleted (Colour, below).
