**Drawn icons (Round 17).** Kompara draws its own icons (the `<svg>` sprite in `_head.html`; stock Material glyphs read as borrowed beside Uber's own): a 24dp box with a 20dp live area and whole-dp keylines; **object glyphs are solid geometry with 2dp knock-outs** — house, three bars on a baseline, two comparison bars, gear (Ajustes, Round 18; the person glyph retired with the name Cuenta), document, calendar, clock, IMSS building, lock, info — never outline-only (a uniform 2dp outline set is the most template icon look there is); **line glyphs** — ←, ✕, chevrons, caret, ✓, share's links — are 2dp strokes with square terminals and mitre joins, the chip's line weight; radii 0 or 3dp (the mark's), angles of 0°, 45° and 90° only; one glyph per destination, active ink and inactive `--ink-3`, never a filled/outline swap; the three tab glyphs' ink within ±10 % of each other and their mass matched to Barlow 600 at 16sp; actionable icons keep 48dp targets; judged together at actual size, day and night. Hoy is a house, Ganancias three vertical bars on a 2dp baseline, Comparar two horizontal bars with the top one longer (`compare_arrows` read as a transfer, wrong in a money app). **Reserved shapes:** the solid ▲ ◆ ▼ belong to the chip alone — no icon, disclosure or control uses them — and the mark stays off the nav (it means an offer's place against the limits); every disclosure in the app is an open 2dp chevron.

**Type.** Two faces, bundled: **money** is Barlow Semi Condensed SemiBold 600 (700 for the hero, headlines, root and section titles) with `tnum lnum` (every peso figure, rate, percentage and count that is the content of a row); **UI** is Barlow 400 and 600. Inter is retired; no weight 800 or 900 anywhere; never simulate condensing with horizontal scaling. **Steps (the Round 14 system layer and consolidation, the end of `base-v3.css`; this ladder replaces every older type rule):** root title 30/36 700 money face (tab roots) · display 72/74 700 (**the day's figure only** — Hoy and Tu día, the display step above; 56 stays only for the A5 stepper value) · figure 40/44 700 money face (`.figrow .value`: Ganancias' figure beside its bars — E1, E2, E3, E3b, E3c, E8; Round 17) · headline 24/30 700 money face (`.hero.sentence .value`, `.t-title`: sentence heroes, Comparar's finding sentences, the share card's standing, onboarding and H-task headlines, the task headlines over a number — G3, H1, H2b, H5 — G1's number, G4's status word) · section title 22/28 700 money face (`h3.sec2`: "Últimas ofertas", "Ofertas de hoy · 13", H1's "¿Y a los demás?") · number 32/36 600 (D2's net, the task figures under their headlines — G3's $2.84, H1's $149, H2b's $79, H5's $5,340 — E4/E5's per-app figures, G2's limits, the A7 code digits) · title 20/26 600 (drill-in top bars, sheet and dialog titles; A1's lockup at 20/26 700 in the money face) · offer rows (`.of`) 18/24 600 for the outcome, 15/20 for the meaning line, 16 for the time and the rate (Round 17, Codex: the 72sp figure dwarfed the 13–14sp words needed to read it) (Comparar's bar labels set name and figure at 17/600) · body 16/22 (rows, buttons at 600, the hero label, the status line, the quiet line) · small 13/18 (labels, overlines, captions, nav labels; the floor — nothing smaller). The chip keeps its own sizes (§6). Labels and section headers are sentence case ("Llevas hoy", "Turnos", "Para tu contador"); no uppercase tracking anywhere in the product. Non-breaking spaces keep `78 %`, `$1,500`, `8 h 10 min`, `12 – 18 oct` and `−$1.05/km` unbroken; no text wraps into a right gutter. **Gate:** confirm `tnum` in the shipped font files and check 13sp Barlow at 1× on a device; if either fails, UI falls back to Roboto and money to Archivo SemiCondensed 600. **Numbers.** The law is one hero plus at most one number per row in the first viewport; A root screen carries its answer and at most two supporting measures; every other count, hour, source amount or rate lives one tap away (Tu día, Cómo se calculó, De dónde salen estos datos). Lists that are the screen's content (offers, weeks, days) carry one figure per row and sit below the hero block. **Round 19 amends the root rule for the day's offer rows:** Hoy's "Últimas ofertas" shows Tu día's row whole — the mark, the outcome over the time and its meaning, and the net rate at the right (`[V] La tomaste / 14:32 · Arriba de tu meta · $9.49/km`; gris carries none) — because the walkthrough's judges asked for each offer's $/km on Hoy four rounds running (task 3), and a list that is the screen's content carries one figure per row under a hero block that keeps one number; E1 shows "62 viajes tomados", the best day's figure on its bar and the statement as one fact with its amount ("$34 más"; the standing lives in Comparar); E3 shows "155 viajes tomados" under its figure row of four week bars, the best one carrying its figure and the others one tap away (E3d); F1's provenance is its qualifier line's chevron (the trip counts live in the Datos sheet) and F4's rows carry no counts. Totals in whole pesos; `$/km` two decimals; `$/h` whole pesos; percentages rounded from full precision; tabular figures; rate precision = threshold precision; **one hero per screen and at most one number per row or element in the first viewport** (B1: hero + three offer rows, each with its one rate, gris none; E1: the figure + the best day's figure on its bar + the discrepancy's $34); never truncate a value or unit; no critical copy under 13sp; never show `$0` in place of a known value while loading.

**Colour.** Paper and ink, plus the verdict triple — and, since Round 25, **Kompara green as the brand** (Panamá, 2026-10-28: "the majority of the big black buttons should be green; otherwise it just looks like Uber"): `--brand` `#187A36` (the K's colour, `--k`) with `--on-brand` white fills every Kompara primary action ("Empezar", "Abrir ajustes", "Abrir Uber para probar", "Guardar", "Volver a dar el permiso", "Quitar restricción de batería" …), the active nav tab (icon, label and its top indicator; `#3FB565` on the night surface), the selected period's underline, a switch that is on and a field being typed into. It never fills a verdict surface — the chip's band and the row marks keep the verdict triple, always paired with ▲ ◆ ▼ and a phrase, so a green button never reads as "arriba de tu meta" — and Uber's own screens keep Uber's black ("Aceptar" on C1–C12). White on `#187A36` is 5.4:1, AA for the 16sp/600 button label. Secondary actions stay grey (`.btn.grey`), text actions ink, money ink. The tokens (implemented in [`design-system/ui_kits/android-app-v2/base-v3.css`](../design-system/ui_kits/android-app-v2/base-v3.css); the day surface values are its Round 14 system layer):

| Token | Day | Night | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--paper` | `#FFFFFF` | `#121211` | the one surface (white by day since Round 14; the warm `#F5F4EF` is retired) |
| `--paper-2` | `#F2F2F0` | `#262624` | fields, pressed, skeletons, the locked placeholder, Hoy's grey secondary, G2's stage — never a card |
| `--ink` / `--ink-2` / `--ink-3` | `#111110` / `#5C5B57` / `#8A8984` | `#F2F1EC` / `#A8A7A1` / `#74736E` | text and the primary button; ink-3 only ≥ 18sp or non-text |
| `--rule` | `#E8E7E3` | `#2C2C29` | 1dp hairlines |
| verde / amarillo / rojo | `#187A36` / `#F2B705` / `#D32F2F`, on-colours `#FFF` / `#1A1300` / `#FFF` | same | only on verdicts (the chip, the mark) — and verde once more as the "Semáforo funcionando" dot |
| `--mark-verde` / `--mark-amarillo` / `--mark-rojo` | `#2FB35A` / `#F2B705` / `#FF5C5C` | same | the chip tray's marker bar only (§6): tints of the band hue lifted for the ink tray, named tokens since Round 18 (Opus R17-R8); never on paper, never a status |
| `--chip-ink` / on | `#0E0F10` / `#EDEDEA` | same | the chip plate and C6; theme-independent |
| `--k` | verde | same | the letter K only |

**Deleted:** every slate and emerald token, info-blue, amber, the second red (`error`), `chipNeutral`, the brand strip, cards, pills, segmented controls, icon tiles, circled glyphs (the status-ring family excepted, Status glyphs above), Inter, weights 800/900, the 18-size ladder. One verdict palette (delete `overlay/VerdictColors.kt`). **Rules:** verdict colours appear only on verdicts, with one exception — verde as the dot of "Semáforo funcionando" ("it works", while nothing needs the driver), never for a fact about data ("Semana confirmada", "Leída" take the check, `.status.fact`); preparado, progress, attention and "en pausa" are **ink** (a status glyph plus words, never a hue); errors and reader problems are ink states (B2's hero is the cause as a sentence); destructive actions are ink text behind a confirmation; no fifth colour anywhere. **Surfaces:** one page per screen; the hero number sits on the surface, top-left, with its 16sp label above and the 48dp quiet line below; groups are separated by 32dp of whitespace and, where a ledger needs it, full-bleed 1dp `--rule` hairlines; rows ≥ 56dp with one label, one value or chevron, at most one figure; elevation only on sheets (top radius 16), the chip and snackbars; one ink primary per screen, 56dp, radius 10, full width, at the thumb; secondaries are ink text buttons (600, 48dp) or, under a list on a root, the grey full-width button (`.btn.grey`: 52dp, radius 10, `--paper-2`; Hoy's "Ver todas las ofertas de hoy"); selectors are **text tabs** (active `--ink` 600 with a 24 × 2dp ink underline, inactive `--ink-2`, unavailable `--ink-3`) and choice sheets, never segmented controls or pills; steppers are a 56sp value between two 56dp rings (A5) or a 32sp limit beside two 48dp rings (G2); **no pills anywhere** ("Ejemplo" is a 13sp label; status is a dot or a status glyph plus words; the settings control is a bare 24dp gear, never in a ring); **icons** are Kompara's drawn set (the drawn-icon rule above), 24dp, coloured by `fill`, never inside a container, never on a row as a tile, never emoji; gutter 20dp; rhythm 4/8/12/20/32. Interactive targets ≥ 48dp; units and billing terms ≥ 13sp. **Theme:** Kompara follows the system theme (no onboarding question; Ajustes carries a "Tema · Automático / Claro / Oscuro" row); mockups lead with day and a night set (N1–N10) proves the structure, including the amarillo and gris chips on the dark host; the chip is theme-independent — chip ink and the verdict triple keep their exact colours in both modes, and by night only the trip's band emits light.

**States, everywhere.** Loading: the final layout as a static skeleton, title kept, figures and sentences as `--paper-2` blocks cut to the real strings' widths; no loading sentence — the skeleton says it (E7). Empty: one line saying what will appear and when, phrased as absence of evidence ("Aún no registramos viajes", "No registramos ofertas este día"), never as a fact about the world; when the semáforo was off, say so in its own word ("El semáforo estuvo apagado parte del día", Tu día's partial-capture line; Hoy's off state dates the outage from its notice, "Semáforo apagado · desde ayer 20:31") and point to import. Offline: local screens work; only network blocks say "Sin conexión. Lo intentamos cuando tengas datos." + Reintentar. Error: "No pudimos cargar tus números." + Reintentar. Locked: **the driver's own totals are always visible**; what is masked is the other side's number or the breakdown, one plain line per screen (E2) or the lock in the value's own slot (F3) that promises only what that period really has, never a band, never a blur wall, **no lock on absent data**. Large fonts: content scrolls; nothing is clipped or shrunk.

**Primary action.** At most one filled button, at the thumb, labelled with a verb. Read-only screens may have none; the hero or a row is then the interaction. No Premium promotion on Hoy, the chip, recovery, permission setup or anything shown over a host app.

**Components.** Reuse `PrimaryButton` (one ink fill per screen, 56dp; text secondaries 48dp), `TextField`, `OtpInput`, `EmptyState`, `KomparaProgressBar` (2dp ink). **Rebuilt:** `PercentileBar` → `CompareBars` (the driver and the average as two proportional bars, ink and `--ink-3`). **Retired:** `Card` as a surface, `VerdictBadge` (→ the mark), `StatusPill` (→ a dot or a status glyph plus words), `ShiftLine` and the dashed `CarLine` (Round 17), the segmented control (→ text tabs), `ScopeLine` (→ the hero's quiet line), icon tiles, 🔒 glyphs. **New:** `Regla` (the chip, `.regla`, and its compact form, `.regla.compact` — the phrase line drops, the limits stay, §6) and its sibling `ReaderStoppedTab` (C6, a different object); `Mark` (`.mark.v/.a/.r/.g`, with an inner `<i>`); `OfferRow` (`.of`: mark · outcome over the time and its meaning · the net rate, on Hoy as in Tu día since Round 19; gris none); `Observation` (`.obs`: a 22dp `--ink-2` drawn glyph or a mark · one fact, with an optional quiet 14sp second line · chevron, between hairlines; stacked rows share one; only rows that pass the cut rule); `ListRow` (label · value · chevron, 56dp, at most one figure); `StepperField` (56sp value between two 56dp rings); `MaskedValue` (`•••` on `--paper-2`, never a blur); `HeroQuietLine` (`.hero .sub2`, 48dp, one line or two, `.two`; opens Cómo se calculó, or Datos on Comparar); `TextTabs` (24 × 2dp underline); `StatusLine` (16sp 600 words led by the green dot or a 16dp status glyph — ring with "!", hollow ring, slashed ring, pause bars, check; `.status.ready / .off / .paused / .fact`, Round 17; on Hoy a 48dp door row, `.row.door` — the glyph and words over a sub-line and a chevron into Semáforo in its state, whose first row is the limits (Round 18; Round 19's door to the limits reverted in the pre-test)); `FigureRow` (`.figrow`: Ganancias' 40sp figure beside a 168 × 72dp `.spark.mini` plot, Round 17; E1, E2, E3, E3c, E8); `BestBar` (`.spark i.best`: the period's best bar in ink with its figure, money 16/600, 4dp above it; the other bars `--rule`; E1, E3, E3c, E8); a reference line (`.carline`: since Round 17 only E5's salario mínimo, a solid 2dp ink line with a two-word caption in a 104dp column — the dashed car line retired with one money definition); `CompareBars` (`.cbar`: a label line — name and rate at 17/600 — over a 16dp bar, one scale, 24dp between rows; the leading row ink at 100 %, `.other` `--ink-3` under an `--ink-2` label; `.held` keeps a withheld bar's slot with a bare 24dp lock; F1–F4, and H3 with its amounts shown); `LimitControl` (G2: a limit's name with the chip's cue, "Tu meta · ▲ desde" / "Tu mínimo · ▼ abajo de", its 32sp value in a filled field — `--paper-2`, radius 8, 48dp, "$" and "/km" fixed — typed in place, and two 48dp ink rings with their step under them, "de $0.50 en $0.50" (Round 21; "de $0.10 en $0.10" since Round 19), under la regla at 1:1; both live and typeable, one five-word helper under them); the save snackbar (G4i: the kit's `.toast`, ink, 16sp 600, the check of a done fact, 4 s, no action, announced politely; 8dp above the opener's footer primary — its first footer button — or 16dp above the bottom when there is none, never over a row; Round 21, the placement Round 21b); `GreyButton` (`.btn.grey`); `StateLegend` (`.legend4`: A1's one column of four 48dp rows (Round 18) — mark, the chip's cue in its band colour and the phrase on one line; the shown state ink 600 with an ink-outlined mark, the others `--ink-2`, no cell fill; Round 17); `Icon` (the drawn set, §4); `.fade` (a 24dp paper fade above the nav where a list continues, D1/N3).

---

## 5. Screens

Format: **Purpose · Hero · Primary** → layout → states → removed → 1-second glance. Mockup frame ids in brackets. Every screen is one paper surface: the hero on the surface (the 16sp label with "aprox.", the figure — the 72sp display on the day, 40sp beside its bars on Ganancias, a 24sp finding sentence on Comparar, a headline over a 32sp figure on a task — and the 48dp quiet line that opens Cómo se calculó), hairline rows of at most one figure that pass the cut rule (§4), text tabs, the mark, status glyphs, ink notes, the 13sp footer line that qualifies a primary (H1, H4, H5b), one ink primary at the thumb. Where a frame and 120 §4's wireframe differ, the frame is described and the difference is named in one clause.

### 5.1 Hoy [B1–B9, B2r, B3n]
**Purpose:** Is the semáforo working, and what have I kept today? · **Hero:** "Llevas hoy · aprox." `$548` on the white surface (lunes 19 oct, 14:40, 6 viajes tomados: the morning shift's $648 and the 14:32 trip's $114 after running costs, less the day's $214 share of lo del coche — the figure the open week gives the same Monday, E8) · **Primary:** state-dependent; none when healthy. The Round 14 blind-test winner (Hoy v5, §9j) on the Round 17 money definition (§4); every class is in `base-v3.css`.

1. Top bar: the root title "Hoy" at 30sp (money face 700) · the bare drawn gear → Ajustes. Under it **the status row, a door into Semáforo in its state** (Round 18; one rule for every Hoy state but the off one since the pre-test): one 48dp row — `Semáforo funcionando` in 16sp 600 led by a 10dp green dot with a soft halo (the one non-verdict use of verde, "it works"; every other state led by its 16dp ink status glyph, §4: `Batería restringida · aún funciona` behind the ring with "!" when a fix waits, B7 · `Semáforo en pausa · se reanuda mañana` behind the pause bars, B5 · `Semáforo preparado` behind the hollow ring, B3, B4), a 15sp `--ink-2` sub-line and a chevron. **While nothing waits, the sub-line is the two limits, "tu meta $8 · tu mínimo $6", and the door opens Semáforo working** (G4; B1, B8; B6's partial day opens it solo Uber, G4g, since the pre-test fixes), **whose first row is those limits** (→ Tu meta y tu mínimo at rest, G2b: two taps from Hoy). Round 19 had sent this door straight to the limits (run 3's judges tapped "tu meta · tu mínimo" and expected their editor); in run 4 four of six judges named it again — found neither for the limits (task 6 averaged 5.5 taps against that one-tap route) nor where they looked for the status — so the pre-test reverted it: the status is found where it is looked for, and the limits cost one tap more (Opus `282`; the driver test logs the first tap of tasks 6 and 7). **When something waits, the door opens Semáforo in that state** and the line names what waits: the battery fix (G4b, from B7), an offer drawn without a chip, counted on the sub-line (G4c, from B9's "no cupo el semáforo en 1 oferta"), the pause (G4d, from B5), preparado with the permission just granted (G4e, drawn since the pre-test, from B3's "✓ permiso listo") or the morning after, with its proof of life (G4f, the pre-test fixes, from B4's "última lectura ayer 20:10"). Only the off state keeps a plain line without the chevron, because B2's cause and primary are the explanation (`Semáforo apagado` behind a slashed ring, the time as a 400 `--ink-2` suffix).
2. **Hero, the one root anatomy:** the 16sp label "Llevas hoy · aprox." (the estimate said once, in the label; Round 21: "Te quedan hoy" read as money still to come), `$548` at the 72sp display (money face 700; the display step is the day's alone, §4), and **the quiet line** under it (`.hero .sub2.two`: 16sp `--ink-2` in a 48dp row with a chevron) that carries the count and the deduction basis on two lines, `6 viajes tomados (1 en curso), / ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche ›` (Round 24d: the 14:32 trip is counted while still under way, and the line says so) — what was taken off, in plain words (Round 19: "ya sin … ni coche" read as "without a car") — and opens Cómo se calculó at the same moment (D3a at 14:40: $979 − $217 − $214 = $548; the pre-test — the sheet had opened on the 21:10 day's $1,026). The day already carries its share of the car ($1,500 ÷ 7), so Hoy, Tu día, the open week and the month give today one figure.
3. **No observation row** (Round 17, the cut rule, §4): "Tu mejor hora hoy: 9 a 10 h" was a past fact at 14:40 and is gone, with no forward-looking version (the fixture holds only today's hours, and a best hour to come would be advice, which Hoy never gives). The slot between the hero and the list carries **states only** — a reader limitation (B6) — and is absent when there is none, so "Últimas ofertas" follows the hero directly; a fix that waits leads the status door instead (B7), and an offer drawn without a chip is counted on its sub-line (B9), never a second row.
4. "Últimas ofertas", the 22sp section title (`h3.sec2`, money face 700), over three offer rows on one timeline (`.of`, 66dp, a hairline under each; no offer inside the 12:40–14:20 pause), **D1's row whole** (Round 19: the walkthrough's judges asked for each offer's $/km on Hoy four rounds running, so the root numbers rule now gives a list row its one figure, §4): **the mark** · the outcome in 18sp 600 over a quiet 15sp `--ink-2` line with the time and its meaning, in the chip's own words · the net rate at the right, 16sp in the money face — `[V] En curso / 14:32 · Arriba de tu meta · $9.49/km` (Round 24d: at 14:40 the 24-min trip has not ended, so the outcome says so; «La tomaste» once it has, as on D1 at 21:10), `[R] Se fue sin tomar / 12:10 · Abajo de tu mínimo · $2.20/km`, `[G] Sin lectura / 11:48 · No pudimos leer la tarifa` with no rate, because a gris offer has none; never with the car, no colour word, no chevron; each row opens its own detail over Hoy (14:32 → D2, 12:10 → D2b, 14:50 → D2c; a gris row has none, only the note "Sin lectura: no pudimos leer la tarifa"), and "Ver todas las ofertas de hoy" opens Tu día as it stands at that minute (D1a at 14:40) (§5.4). "La tomaste" is still an estimate of a trip *taken*, never paid. Under the list, the grey full-width secondary **"Ver todas las ofertas de hoy"** (`.btn.grey`: 52dp, radius 10, `--paper-2`; "Ver tu día" until Round 18 — the label now says what it opens, without a count the 14:40 day cannot know yet) opens the whole day as it stands (D1a at 14:40, the pre-test — it had opened the 21:10 day; D1 in the evening), a different place from the quiet line's ledger.
5. The bottom bar of drawn icons (§4), the active tab marked by a 3dp ink indicator on its top edge.

**Readiness rule.** *Funcionando* = at least one successful host read since the service last bound **and today**, and no newer run of repeated failures on the last usable parser (failure precedence over an earlier success); otherwise *preparado*, with "última lectura ayer 18:00" on the status line as proof of life. A partially supported app ("Por ahora no leemos DiDi", with what is missing named in the hero label, "Llevas hoy sin contar DiDi · aprox.", B6) is **not** an off state: Hoy stays "funcionando" (the green dot) for the apps it reads, with one plain, tap-inert state row in the slot under the hero and no permission toggle; the Semáforo screen (G4) carries the detail.

**States.** *Off (B2, martes 20 oct 8:05):* the status reads `Semáforo apagado · desde ayer 20:31` behind the slashed ring — the minute of H6's notice, whose tap opens this state, so the notice and Hoy tell one outage with one cause and one start (the pre-test, Opus R19b-R3: "desde las 7:50" gave it two; during the shift this outage draws nothing over the host, C13, and since the pre-test fixes the overlay's own plate, C6, tells only a stopped capture, so it no longer shares the cause) — and **the hero is the cause as a 24/30 700 sentence in the money face** — "Falta el permiso de Accesibilidad" (no terminal period, on one line, "de Accesibilidad" held together should it wrap) — with the body on three lines broken at the phrases, "Android a veces lo apaga / (por ejemplo, al ahorrar batería). / Sin él, Kompara no ve tus ofertas." (Round 21: in run 5's task 7 every judge found the fix and none the why, or how to stop it; engineering confirms that a battery restriction is what removes the permission before this copy ships); under a hairline inset to the gutter (32dp above and below), yesterday's result leads in ink with its date, "Ayer, lunes 19 oct, te quedaron $1,026 aprox." (after gasolina, desgaste and the car's day; Round 23's dated rule, §4), with today's empty state "Aún no registramos viajes hoy." under it in `--ink-2` (no figure before the first trip, and the 72sp display stays the day's, §4); **one ink primary at the thumb that names the step it opens, "Volver a dar el permiso"**, and under it, only when Android reports a battery restriction for Kompara, the 48dp text button **"Que no se vuelva a apagar"** (Round 23: it names what it prevents — run 7's j2 and j5 called "Que no vuelva a pasar" vague; → A4b, the battery step in B2's world: A3n's slashed-ring status "Semáforo apagado · aún falta el permiso", and «Abrir ajustes» and «Ahora no» both return here; Round 21, Opus `302` §5.4; A4b Round 21b) (→ a sheet with A3's three steps and the "control total" warning, then the Accessibility deep link, and nothing else: a stopped capture restarts when Kompara opens, C6, so it never reaches an off Hoy with a button — the second pre-test batch dropped its "→ the capture consent" route and its "Se detuvo la lectura" hero); it goes through Aviso if consent was never given. No red card, no red status, no dimmed hero: an off semáforo is an ink state. B2's status row is not a door — its hero carries the cause and its primary the fix — so the off state's Semáforo is reached from Ajustes (Round 20, Opus N5: the route B2 → ⚙ → Ajustes → Semáforo met "Funcionando" and "Lectura comprobada · 14:32" at 8:01): the gear opens Ajustes in this world (G1b, "Semáforo · Apagado · falta el permiso"), whose row opens Semáforo apagado (G4h), with the same primary. *Back with the permission (B2r, martes 20 oct 8:07; Round 23, an alternate labelled above the phone):* the return from Android's settings for a driver with history — task 7's recovery, A3's «Abrir ajustes» → Android (the prototype's A3s) → back with the permission (in the build read from the permission and service state; ✓ "Permiso listo" on A3 for 800 ms) → here, never the first run's B3. The status is B3's door again, the hollow ring, `Semáforo preparado` over "✓ permiso listo", its chevron into Semáforo preparado (G4e); never "funcionando", which waits for an offer read today. The sentence hero is B3's "Listo. Abre tu app de viajes" over **"En tu próxima oferta verás el semáforo."** — *próxima*, not *primera*, because this driver's offers have been read before; no example chip and no setup rows (they belong to the first run); B2's "no trips yet" form stays under the hairline, "Ayer, lunes 19 oct, te quedaron $1,026 aprox." over "Aún no registramos viajes hoy.", and the 72sp display stays the day's; the primary **"Abrir Uber para probar"** (B3's real next step) and, while Android reports a battery restriction, B2's text button "Que no se vuelva a apagar" (→ A4b; back returns here). In the prototype B2r takes B2's place in the history, so back leaves Kompara rather than showing the off state again (Codex `311` §3, Opus `312` §3: no feedback after «Abrir ajustes» was j4's and j5's top problem in run 7). *En pausa (B5, 13:10):* the status door with the pause bars, `Semáforo en pausa · se reanuda mañana` over the limits sub-line, its chevron into Semáforo en pausa (G4d, drawn since Round 19b, Opus R19-M2: B5's door opened an undrawn state; the pause is said once, by the status), then the morning's result in B1's anatomy — "Llevas hoy · aprox.", `$434` (shift 1's $648 after running costs, less the day's $214 car share, taken once as on B1), the two-line quiet line `5 viajes tomados en 4 h 30 min, / ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche ›` — then "Últimas ofertas" with the offers read before the pause, `[R] Se fue sin tomar / 12:10 · Abajo de tu mínimo · $2.20/km` and `[G] Sin lectura / 11:48 · No pudimos leer la tarifa`, each row opening Tu día; the grey button is left out, so the primary **"Reanudar el semáforo"** is the only button; no attention notifications while paused; it auto-resumes the next day. *Preparado, nothing read yet (B3, first run, 9:44):* the status door, `Semáforo preparado` behind the hollow ring over the sub-line **"✓ permiso listo"** — the check of a done fact, confirming the step just taken (Round 19: run 3 asked for the permission to be confirmed apart from the first reading), in lowercase like every Hoy door sub-line since the second pre-test batch ("tu meta $8 · tu mínimo $6", "última lectura ayer 20:10", "no cupo el semáforo en 1 oferta") — and a chevron into Semáforo in that state, preparado (G4e: "Semáforo preparado" over "Tu primera oferta lo confirma", Uber and DiDi "Esperando una oferta"; drawn in the pre-test — B3 had opened G4 working, "Lectura comprobada · hace 2 min", before any offer was read); the sentence hero "Listo. Abre tu app de viajes" (Round 21: the "it worked" on the return from Android's settings — run 5's j4 and j5 came back with no proof — never "encendido", because the first offer still confirms capture) with "En tu primera oferta verás el semáforo." under it, then **the real chip, la regla v16 in its verde state**, copied verbatim, at the gutter in a 116dp wrapper and tap-inert (▲ `$9.49` /km neto · "Arriba de tu meta" over "tu meta $8" and "tu mínimo $6", the marker above both; no $/h), with "Ejemplo" hung 12dp left of the plate on the `$9.49` baseline; three plain rows, each with one destination, say what the first numbers start from — **both limits from day one**, "Tu meta $8.00/km · tu mínimo $6.00/km / Valores iniciales ›" (→ Tu meta y tu mínimo, where "Volver a lo inicial ($8.00/km y $6.00/km)" restores both); **the costs, named as typical**, "Usamos costos típicos: $2.84 por km" with "Ajustar ›" at the right in `--ink-2` (→ the cost question, A5, which B4 asks again the next morning; Round 19, Codex R18: the first live calculation must say its costs are defaults, now that the question left the first run); and "Elegir mi ciudad ›"; the primary is the real next step, **"Abrir Uber para probar"** (it opens the host app: the walkthrough asked for a test, not a wait), and the text secondary **"Avisarme si se apaga"** asks for the notification permission once, in context (API 33+); B3 follows A3 or A4 directly (Round 18), so nothing on it assumes the costs or a number were asked. *Off on the first day (B3n, 12:30, A1's moment; Round 24c, Codex 321 §1 and Opus 322 §2: A3's and A3n's «Ahora no» gave the neutral note):* Hoy for a driver who said «Ahora no» to the permission and has nothing recorded yet — B3's two-line 48dp status row with B2's slashed ring, `Semáforo apagado` (16/22 600) over the plain `--ink-2` cause "falta el permiso", no chevron (no door: Semáforo has nothing to show before the permission); the 24sp sentence hero "Activa el semáforo" over one 16/22 `--ink-2` body, "Sin el permiso de Accesibilidad, / Kompara no ve tus ofertas."; one ink primary at the thumb, "Activar el semáforo" (→ A3, the same permission step; B4's single-button slot, 692–748); no text button, no money, no example chip, no rows, no Últimas ofertas; the gear and the nav stay. Its ⚙ has no drawn destination yet: G1 reads "Funcionando" and G1b is 20 oct's, so the prototype gives the neutral note there (§9v, still open). In the prototype it is the first day's root: «Ahora no» replaces A1–A3 with it, and a later «Ya lo activé» lands B3 in its place. *Next morning, nothing yet (B4, lunes 19 oct 7:30):* "funcionando" expired overnight, so the status door is back to `Semáforo preparado` behind the hollow ring, its proof of life on the sub-line, "última lectura ayer 20:10", and its chevron into Semáforo preparado the morning after (G4f, the pre-test fixes: "Tu primera oferta de hoy lo confirma", Uber's row keeping "Última lectura ayer 20:10" — the door had opened B3's first-run G4e, where nothing was ever read; Round 19, Opus R18-N5: the gear was the only way there); with no trip today the screen takes B2's form, not the period hero, so the car's share of the day never stands alone as a figure before the first trip: the one-time costs question is the sentence hero, "¿Manejar te cuesta unos $2.84 por km?", with the three inputs it comes from in 15sp `--ink-2`, "12 km/L · gasolina $24.50/L · desgaste $0.80/km"; under it, between hairlines, the content row **"Ajustar mis costos ›"** (→ the one cost question, A5, whose back arrow returns here and whose primary saves and returns to Hoy with the costs confirmed; Tus costos, G3, stays in Ajustes; on a root the secondary is a row, never a second button); then "Ayer, domingo 18 oct, te quedaron $284 aprox." in ink (dated since Round 23; Sunday 18 oct had 4 trips: $498.55 after running costs less $214.29, computed once at full precision — rounding twice would give $285; on a Tuesday the line reads "Ayer, lunes 19 oct, te quedaron $1,026 aprox.") with "Aún no registramos viajes hoy." under it, never "no manejaste"; the one primary **"Sí, son mis costos"** names what it confirms; costs stay labelled typical until either tap; since Round 18 this is where the costs are first asked (A5 left the first run). *Partial support (B6, 10:20):* the status door keeps the green dot on `Semáforo funcionando`, over the limits sub-line, as on B1, into Semáforo solo Uber (G4g, the pre-test fixes: G4's head with DiDi behind the slashed ring, "Por ahora no la leemos", tap-inert — the door had opened G4, where DiDi waited as if readable); the hero label names **what is missing, not what is counted** — "Llevas hoy sin contar DiDi · aprox." `$166` — over the two-line quiet line `3 viajes tomados en 2 h 20 min, / ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche ›`: the fixture gives the three Uber trips as $380 after running costs, and the day's car share comes off once, from the recorded day, never charged to one app ($380 − $214.29; "Llevas hoy en Uber $166" would charge the whole day's car to Uber), so the driver's DiDi trips could only add to it; its Cómo se calculó (not drawn) reads `3 viajes de Uber` / ya restamos gasolina y desgaste `$380` · `Lo del coche` `−$214` · `Llevas hoy sin contar DiDi` `$166`, with "DiDi no entra: por ahora no lo leemos." and no invented gross or km; the limitation takes the state slot as a row in the status grammar, the slashed ring of an app not read · "Por ahora no leemos DiDi", tap-inert, no chevron ("Uber" is never claimed as the whole day); then "Últimas ofertas" with the three Uber offers taken, each with its rate, `[V] La tomaste / 10:05 · Arriba de tu meta · $9.49/km` / `[A] La tomaste / 9:12 · Cumple tu mínimo · $7.16/km` / `[V] La tomaste / 8:36 · Arriba de tu meta · $8.62/km` (the 8:36 rate is illustrative, like the day's net; the list agrees with the count), and the grey "Ver todas las ofertas de hoy"; no primary. *A fix is waiting (B7, lunes 19 oct 14:40; Round 18, Opus R17-R3):* B1 at the same minute with one change — the phone restricts Kompara's battery, so the status door leads with the problem behind the ink ring with "!", `Batería restringida · aún funciona` (G1's Semáforo row in this state and G4b's head in the same words), over the same limits sub-line, and its chevron opens Semáforo in that state (G4b), whose primary is the fix, "Quitar restricción de batería"; the green dot is kept for when nothing needs the driver, so it never stands beside a restriction; no amber, no banner, no second button; everything else is B1's ($548, the two basis lines, the three offers with their rates, the grey button). *The negative day (B8, an alternate scenario labelled above the phone, as E3c; Round 18, Opus R17-R4):* 14:40 on a day whose only trip is the 14:32 verde offer — the root anatomy and its words, "Llevas hoy · aprox.", with `−$100` at the 72sp display, a true minus kerned to its $, **in ink, never rojo** (§4): the trip left $113.90 and the car's $214.29 comes off the calendar day; the quiet line says why on three lines, `1 viaje tomado (en curso), / ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche / ($214 por día) ›` (→ Cómo se calculó); no "te faltan" and no progress bar — the car's day is not a quota to chase; B1's status door, into Semáforo (G4); "Últimas ofertas" holds the one offer read, `[V] En curso / 14:32 · Arriba de tu meta · $9.49/km` (Round 24d), which opens Tu día, and there is no grey button, because that row is the whole list; the label above the phone, "Escenario alterno, no es pantalla · un solo viaje hoy" (Round 19, Opus R18-N9), keeps it from reading against B1 at the same minute. *An offer got no semáforo (B9, an alternate scenario labelled above the phone; Round 19 — four of six run-3 judges found "funcionando" on Hoy beside an overlay that never showed):* B1 at 14:40 if the 12:10 rojo offer ($62 → $2.20/km) had arrived while the host's navigation filled the screen, so no safe band fit and no plate was drawn (C11) — the green dot stays, because the semáforo works and nothing needs fixing, over the count on the door's sub-line, `Semáforo funcionando / no cupo el semáforo en 1 oferta ›` (Round 24: "no cupo el semáforo en 1 oferta" until run 8, where j2, j4 and j5 read "sin semáforo" as also counting the unread 11:48 offer, which does get a grey semáforo; 200 of 285dp, one line), and the door opens Semáforo in that state (G4c), whose row names the latest cause with its time ("12:10 · no cupo en la pantalla (navegación)") and what to do and where the offer went, in G8's words ("No tienes que hacer nada; quedó en Tu día"; the pre-test trimmed Round 19b's "pasa cuando la navegación llena la pantalla", which said the cause twice) and whose "¿No ves el semáforo? ›" opens Ayuda on that answer (G8), which names the cause the same way; the offer keeps its trace in "Últimas ofertas" by the suppression rule (§6), `[R] Se fue sin tomar / 12:10 · sin semáforo en pantalla · $2.20/km`, the reason in place of a meaning the driver never saw (the row says what the driver saw; the cause has one name, "no cupo en la pantalla", on Semáforo and in Ayuda — Round 19b, Opus R19-M4); everything else is B1's ($548, the basis, the 14:32 and 11:48 rows, the grey button), but its grey button and G4c's row open D1b, Tu día at 14:40 in this world, whose 12:10 row reads the same (the pre-test fixes: they had opened D1a, whose 12:10 row reads "Abajo de tu mínimo", a meaning the driver never saw), and its rows open their own details (the third batch): the 14:32 D2, the 12:10 D2d — D2b with the cause line — and the gris 11:48 the note "Sin lectura: no pudimos leer la tarifa" — "funcionando" never stands silent beside an offer the driver did not see. *Loading/error:* shared. *Locked:* never. *Night (N1, N2):* the same markup on the night tokens; the marks' fills and the green dot stay the only colour.

**Removed:** weekly hero, week row, streak, share, platform chips, 2×2 grid, gate tease, three consejos, completeness note; in v3 the status pill, the hero card, the scope sentence, the circled ✓ ✕ ? glyphs, the colour words, the per-row chevrons and the red and amber cards; in Round 14 the separate count line and the 48dp qualifier row (one quiet line now), the "Ver tu día ›" header action (now the grey button; B5 has none), the time-led one-line rows and the ink segment on a working reader (a green dot); in Round 15 the observation's chevron and tap; in Round 17 the observation row itself (a past fact at 14:40), the day's figure before the car ($762 → $548), "Lector" as the status's name, the dashed and broken segments (now status glyphs), B2's "Encender lector" (now the step it opens), B3's one-limit row and its notification ask as the primary, and B6's info glyph; in Round 18 the person icon (now the gear, Ajustes), the bare status line on B1, B5 and B6 (now the door with the limits) and the label "Ver tu día"; in Round 19 the healthy door's detour through Semáforo (it opens the limits it names), the plain status lines on B3 and B4 (now doors), Hoy's rows without a rate and the basis "ya sin … ni coche"; in the pre-test Round 19's healthy door to the limits (every door but the off one opens Semáforo again, the limits its first row) and B2's "desde las 7:50" (now "desde ayer 20:31", H6's minute); in the pre-test fixes B4's door to the first-run G4e (now G4f), B6's door to the every-app G4 (now G4g) and B9's rows into D1a (now D1b behind its grey button, and each row's own detail since the third batch). **Glance:** one dot, one number, three marks with their meanings and rates.

### 5.2 Semáforo [G4, G4b, G4c, G4d, G4e, G4f, G4g, G4h, G4i]
**Purpose:** Is the semáforo working, and fix it if not · **Hero:** the status word behind its glyph · **Primary:** only when something is wrong, for the first failing row.
Drill-in ← "Semáforo", no bottom bar, entered from Ajustes' Semáforo row and from Hoy's status door on every state but the off one (the pre-test reverted Round 19's healthy door to the limits, G2b, after walkthrough run 4; the off state is entered from Ajustes' Semáforo row in that state, G1b → G4h, Round 20, because B2's status row is not a door) — always in the state its door named (Round 17: the drivers' one word for the overlay and its status, §4). **Eight drawn states (Round 19, Opus R18-N1: G4 was drawn only in its warning state, so a judge going B1 → G4 met "Batería restringida" beside B1's "funcionando"; Round 19b drew the pause, Opus R19-M2; the pre-test drew preparado, Opus R19b-P2 and Codex R19b; the pre-test fixes drew B4's and B6's destinations; Round 20 drew the off state, Opus N5):** **G4, working** — the fixture day's state, which G1's row shows, the green dot and "Semáforo funcionando", nothing under the head and no filled primary; B1's 14:40 / 82 % (the second pre-test batch: every door into it stands at 14:40); **G4b, battery restricted** — B7's destination, the attention head and its fix (an alternate state, labelled above the phone; its status bar B7's 14:40 / 82 %, Round 19b, Opus R19-M6); **G4c, an offer got no semáforo** — B9's destination, the head still G4's green "funcionando" and the suppression row populated (labelled above the phone, because the 19 oct fixture has none; B9's 14:40 / 82 %); **G4d, en pausa** (Round 19b) — B5's destination, the pause bars in place of the green dot and the words in G4b's head-and-line anatomy, "Semáforo en pausa" over "Se reanuda mañana", so nothing wraps; the one primary undoes it, **"Reanudar el semáforo"**, so the "Pausar…" secondary is gone; the per-app rows say what was true when the pause began at 12:40 (Uber "Lectura comprobada · 12:10", the last offer read, as B5 lists it; DiDi "Sin ofertas leídas hoy"; inDrive "Aún no disponible"); no "No cupo el semáforo" row, because nothing is read while paused; B5's 13:10 / 70 % (labelled above the phone); **G4e, preparado on a first run** (the pre-test) — B3's destination only, B3's hollow ring in place of the green dot and the words in G4b's head-and-line anatomy, "Semáforo preparado" over "Tu primera oferta lo confirma" (the permission is granted; "funcionando" waits for a real offer read today); no filled primary, because nothing needs the driver, and "Pausar el semáforo hasta mañana" stays the muted secondary, because capture is on; Uber and DiDi both behind the hollow ring, "Esperando una oferta", inDrive behind the slashed ring, "Aún no disponible"; no "No cupo el semáforo" row, because no offer has been read to count; B3's 9:44 / 100 % (labelled above the phone); **G4f, preparado the next morning** (the pre-test fixes) — B4's destination: "funcionando" expired overnight, so G4e's head returns as "Semáforo preparado" over "Tu primera oferta de hoy lo confirma" (the "de hoy" separates it from a first run) and the reading history is kept — Uber behind the hollow ring with its proof of life, "Última lectura ayer 20:10", the minute B4's door names, DiDi "Esperando una oferta", inDrive "Aún no disponible"; no primary, "Pausar…" kept, no "No cupo el semáforo" row (nothing read today); B4's 7:30 / 100 % (labelled above the phone); **G4g, solo Uber** (the pre-test fixes) — B6's destination: G4's green head, "Semáforo funcionando", nothing under it and no primary, because the semáforo works for the apps it reads; DiDi's row takes the slashed ring of an app not read, its title `--ink-2`, "Por ahora no la leemos", no chevron, tap-inert like inDrive's — B6's "Por ahora no leemos DiDi" said from inside the app's row; DiDi's reading is switched off remotely when its app changes and cannot be read safely (the parser kill switch), so there is nothing to do, no permission or toggle to offer, and it comes back by itself; Uber "Lectura comprobada · 10:05" (B6's last offer, in the absolute form G4–G4d use; the third batch of pre-test fixes), inDrive "Aún no disponible", and no count row (none at zero since Round 24c); B6's 10:20 / 90 % (labelled above the phone); **G4h, apagado** (Round 20, Opus N5: B2's world had no Semáforo screen, so Ajustes' row opened G4's "Semáforo funcionando · Lectura comprobada · 14:32" at 8:01) — reached from Ajustes' Semáforo row in the off state (G1b), never from B2's status row: B2's slashed ring in place of the green dot and the words in G4b's head-and-line anatomy, "Semáforo apagado · desde ayer 20:31" (the start in `--ink-2`, the minute of H6's notice), the cause as one body line, "Falta el permiso de Accesibilidad", and its consequence in `--ink-2`, "Sin él, Kompara no ve tus ofertas." — B2's words; the one primary is B2's fix, **"Volver a dar el permiso"** (→ A3), and there is no "Pausar…", because it is already off; Uber and DiDi behind the slashed ring, "Sin permiso, no leemos ofertas", their titles `--ink-2`, no chevron and tap-inert, as G4g draws an app not read, inDrive "Aún no disponible"; no "No cupo el semáforo" row and no last read, as on G4d, because nothing is read while it is off; B2's 8:05 / 100 % (labelled above the phone). The hero, set 31dp under the top bar, is a status row whose word takes the 24/30 700 headline face — the green dot only when nothing needs the driver, otherwise its ink status glyph (§4): the ring with "!" when it works but a fix waits, the hollow ring for preparado, the slashed ring for apagado, the pause bars for en pausa — plus the one word `Funcionando` / `Preparado` / `Apagado` / `En pausa`; when something needs the driver **the problem leads** and the working state follows in `--ink-2` ("Batería restringida · aún funciona", behind the ring with "!": the state G1's Semáforo row also shows, problem first; Opus R15-07) and its consequence as one body line on a single line under it ("Tu teléfono puede apagar el semáforo"); right under that head, **the limits row** in G1's grammar, "Tu meta y tu mínimo · $8.00/km · $6.00/km ›" (→ G2b, at rest; Round 18), so the limits are one tap from every state of the screen and, since the pre-test, two taps from Hoy through its door; the R10 intro sentence is cut (the app list comes from the parser registry, never hard-coded; no blanket "todo pasa en tu teléfono", because the anonymous counters leave it). Per-app rows lead with the status glyphs in a 24dp column, so the three app names align: "Uber / Lectura comprobada · 14:32" (the check: a fact about the reading, in G4d's absolute form — the last offer read before the 14:40 doors; "hace 2 min" until the second pre-test batch, on G4, G4b and G4c alike), "DiDi / Esperando una oferta" (the hollow ring: preparado), "inDrive / Aún no disponible" (the slashed ring of an app not read, title `--ink-2`, no chevron). No check rows in the healthy state (the status word already says the permission holds; the alert is G7's switch; where capture consent is required, a "Lectura de ofertas" row appears). A detected problem is the cause beside the status word plus its body line, whose fix is the screen's **one ink primary at the thumb** (G4b: "Quitar restricción de batería" → the exact setting; only when the OS reports a restriction; this phone's steps in a sheet, one deep link per step), while the status word stays truthful. Then, in its own group, the count row **"No cupo el semáforo"** — the offers the reader parsed today but drew no chip for because no safe band fit (C11), never an unread offer, which gets the grey semáforo — **drawn only when the count is 1 or more** (Round 24c, Opus 322 §1 row 3: the zero row, «No cupo el semáforo en ninguna oferta hoy», sat on task 6's path — six of six run-8 judges reached the limits through Hoy's door → G4 and three read and commented on it — and opened with a failure that had not happened; at zero there is no row and "¿No ves el semáforo?" leads the help group with no rule above it, while Ayuda (G8) keeps the explanation, «No cupo en la pantalla (navegación, oferta alta): no tienes que hacer nada; queda en Tu día»; Round 24 had drawn the zero state as one tap-inert line, "No cupo el semáforo · ninguna oferta hoy", Rounds 19–23 as "Ofertas sin semáforo · ninguna hoy", and before them "Sin semáforo en pantalla hoy / Ninguna hoy"); with one (G4c, now the only Semáforo state that draws the row; Codex R18: the recovery after C11 had no populated counterpart) it heads the group, the count the row's figure in ink 600, "No cupo el semáforo en 1 oferta hoy" (Round 24b's one clause), **its sub-line names the latest, its time and cause** ("12:10 · no cupo en la pantalla (navegación)"), **then what to do and where it went, in G8's words**, "No tienes que hacer nada; quedó en Tu día" (Round 19b, Opus R19-M4: the task asks why and what to do; the pre-test folded Round 19b's two lines, "No tienes que hacer nada; pasa cuando la navegación llena la pantalla" and "La oferta quedó en Tu día", which said the cause twice, into this one — Opus `282`), and the row opens Tu día at that offer, logged there as "Se fue sin tomar" over "12:10 · sin semáforo en pantalla" (§5.3; the fixture day has no suppressed offer, so G4c's destination is drawn as an alternate, D1b, since the pre-test fixes — Opus R19-M7); **the cause has one name** (Round 19b: B9, G4c and G8 worded it three ways), "no cupo en la pantalla", on Semáforo and in Ayuda (G8), while the offer's own row on Hoy and in Tu día keeps what the driver saw, "sin semáforo en pantalla"; then **"¿No ves el semáforo? ›"** (Round 19, Opus R18-N4 and run 3), which opens Ayuda on that answer (G8), on every state; then one navigation row, "Qué lee Kompara ›" (the disclosure, read-only), and, on every state but G4d and G4h, the muted 48dp text secondary under the primary, **"Pausar el semáforo hasta mañana"** in `--ink-2` (really stops capture, suppresses alerts, auto-resumes next day; Kompara only). 41 words on G4, 53 on G4b, 57 on G4c, 37 on G4d, 39 on G4e, 42 on G4f, 45 on G4g, 53 on G4h (47 on G4i; Round 24 adds two words to the row).
**States:** all good (no primary; G4) · a fix waiting (G4b) · an offer drawn without a chip (G4c) · en pausa (G4d: the pause bars, "Se reanuda mañana", the primary "Reanudar el semáforo"; Round 19b) · preparado (G4e: the hollow ring, "Tu primera oferta lo confirma", no primary; the pre-test) · preparado the next morning (G4f: "Tu primera oferta de hoy lo confirma", Uber's last read kept; the pre-test fixes) · off (G4h: `Apagado` behind the slashed ring — ink, never red — "Falta el permiso de Accesibilidad" and the primary that names the step it opens, B2's "Volver a dar el permiso"; Round 20) · just after a save (G4i, Round 21: G4 a moment after Guardar on G2c, the limits row already reading "$8.00/km · $6.50/km", under the snackbar "Guardado: tu mínimo ahora es $6.50/km", 8dp above the footer's one action, «Pausar el semáforo hasta mañana», which counts as its primary (725–772dp; Round 21b), 4 s, covering no row; every opener places it by the same rule, §5.14) · partial (G4g: DiDi's row behind the slashed ring, "Por ahora no la leemos", tap-inert, while Hoy's row says "Por ahora no leemos DiDi"; the pre-test fixes) · unsupported parser · capture consent required ("Este teléfono necesita otro permiso" → Continuar) · consent cancelled ("El semáforo sigue apagado" → Intentar de nuevo) · "Revisando…" ≤ 1 s on return from settings · offline (local reader keeps working; not a failure).
**Removed:** the tab, four buttons, the "Lector para DiDi" split, the fake 220dp offer, the E1 dialog where not needed; in v3 the green ✓s, the amber "!" and the amber text button, the 24-word intro (the green dot returned in Round 14, for "funcionando" only; since Round 15 only when nothing needs the driver); in Round 15b "Funcionando" as the head of a warning (the problem leads); in Round 17 "Lector" as the screen's and the status's name and the dash-pattern segments (now status glyphs a driver can tell apart); in Round 18 the bare "0"; in Round 19 "Sin semáforo en pantalla hoy / Ninguna hoy" (now no row at zero, Round 24c — "No cupo el semáforo · ninguna oferta hoy" in Round 24, "Ofertas sin semáforo · ninguna hoy" from Round 19 to Round 23 — or the count with the latest cause) and the warning state as the screen's only drawn state (now G4, G4b and G4c, and G4d since Round 19b); in Round 19b the three wordings of the suppression cause (now "no cupo en la pantalla") and G4b's 9:41 / 100 % status bar (now its source's 14:40 / 82 %); in the pre-test the undrawn preparado state (now G4e; B3 and B4 had landed on "funcionando") and G4c's two action lines (now G8's one); in the pre-test fixes B4's landing on the first-run screen (now G4f) and B6's on the every-app working screen (now G4g); in Round 20 Ajustes' off-state route landing on "Funcionando" (now G1b → G4h). The amber "!" removed in v3 is not the ink ring with "!" (Round 17), a status glyph of the one sanctioned family (§4).

### 5.3 Tu día [D1, D1a, D1b]
**Purpose:** what happened, offer by offer · **Hero:** the day's result · **Primary:** none (rows open details).
The top row is the drill grammar: ← (back returns to Hoy, the nav stays with Hoy active), the 20sp title "Tu día" at the gutter and, as a cluster at the right of the same row, the day stepper `‹ Hoy · 19 oct ›` (a past day reads `‹ Domingo 18 oct ›` over "Te quedaron el domingo · aprox."; ‹ and › 8dp from the date, never in a gutter, the forward arrow dimmed on today) · **hero, the root anatomy:** the label "Llevas hoy · aprox." ("Te quedaron" for a past day; "Llevas" since Round 21), `$1,026` at the 72sp display (the day's step, as on Hoy; Mes opened at the same minute gives the Monday the same figure — at Hoy's 14:40, "19 oct · hoy · 6 viajes $548", E3d, Round 20), the two-line quiet line `9 viajes tomados en 8 h 10 min, / ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche ›` (→ Cómo se calculó; what was taken off in plain words, Round 19) — the hours in turno live in that line and **the shift timeline is gone** (Round 17: per-shift money, $648 + $592 after running costs, would not add up to a day after the car; the pause shows as the gap in the rows' times) · **one observation** between hairlines (`.obs`), led by the verde mark: "2 ofertas arriba de tu meta se fueron sin tomar ›" (Round 21: position words, never a colour word — run 5's j3; on two lines inside its 56dp row, "se fueron sin tomar" held together) (it opens the list filtered to those two — a distinct task, so it passes the cut rule) · the screen's one section title, "Ofertas de hoy · 13" (D1a's words with the day's count since Round 20 — D1a's ten by 14:40 plus 14:50, 16:04 and 17:21, so Tu día's list has one heading at any minute), risen to sit under the observation, with "te deja por km, sin el coche" said once as the column's 13sp `--ink-2` header on the title's baseline, at the right (154dp, one line, 39dp clear of the title; Round 22: "neto por km" read as the fare to j6 and as paid-or-left to j1 and j3, and j3 asked for "te deja por km"; Round 24 adds the basis, §4's rule) · offer rows in the `.of` anatomy Hoy now shares (Round 19) — **the mark**, the outcome (18sp 600) over a 15sp line with the time and the meaning, and the net rate (money 600, right; never with the car) — **newest first** (Round 19, Opus R18-N2 and Codex R18: at 21:10 the list began at 14:50 although the second shift ran to 18:00, and four rows over blank space did not look like 23): at 21:10 it opens on the shift's last offer and runs down the day, `[V] La tomaste / 17:21 · Arriba de tu meta · $11.40/km`, `[V] La tomaste / 16:04 · Arriba de tu meta · $11.04/km`, `[A] La tomaste / 14:50 · Cumple tu mínimo · $7.16/km`, `[V] La tomaste / 14:32 · Arriba de tu meta · $9.49/km`, `[R] Se fue sin tomar / 12:10 · Abajo de tu mínimo · $2.20/km`, `[G] Sin lectura / 11:48 · No pudimos leer la tarifa` with no figure — **under the nav bar**, the sixth row cut by the bar beneath the 24dp paper fade, so "Ofertas de hoy · 13" visibly continues toward 08:00 and the button's "todas" is kept; every rate is the fixture's — Round 19b split the two trips taken after 14:50 out of the shift's total (Opus R19-M1, Codex R19 and five of six run-4 judges: the two newest rows of the list task 3 asks for printed a placeholder; fixture `today_afternoon_trips`): 17:21 is $255 over 17.9 km → $11.40/km and 16:04 is $236 over 17.0 km → $11.04/km, both above tu meta, so both verde, and together still the shift's $491 over 34.9 km, so $1,026 holds — every drawn row with a reading prints its figure; "aprox." is said once, in the hero label, never per row · **a suppressed offer (C11) keeps its row** in the same component — the mark in its verdict colour, the outcome, the time and the net rate — with the reason "sin semáforo en pantalla" in place of the meaning on its quiet line (`[R] Se fue sin tomar / 12:10 · sin semáforo en pantalla · $2.20/km`): the mark says what the offer was, the reason that the driver never saw a chip; the 19 oct fixture has no suppressed offer, so D1 and D1a draw no such row (B9, G4c and, since the pre-test fixes, D1b draw the case as alternates; the detail a suppressed row opens is D2d, drawn in the second pre-test batch, §5.4); Hoy's door counts them ("no cupo el semáforo en 1 oferta", B9) and the Semáforo screen names the latest ("No cupo el semáforo", §5.2; Round 24) · the 24dp paper fade (`.fade`) above the nav is the scroll cue of a list that continues to 13 offers · the bottom bar stays (the day keeps the Hoy context). One day, newest first: every offer falls inside a shift, none inside a pause or an outage, and a pause is shown as a pause, not an outage; the 14:50, 14:32, 12:10 and 11:48 offers are the fixture's and the 17:21 and 16:04 rows the shift's two trips without their own figures, none invented; counts always add up ("se fue sin tomar" covers expired and passed). **At Hoy's minute (D1a; the pre-test, Opus R19b-P1 and Codex R19b: Hoy at 14:40 drilled into the 21:10 day, so one tap turned $548 into $1,026):** Tu día opens on the same clock and the same money as the Hoy it came from — the status bar 14:40 / 82 %, "Llevas hoy · aprox." `$548` over `6 viajes tomados en 4 h 50 min, / ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche ›` (B1's six trips; the time on shift as D1 counts it, 08:00–12:30 plus 14:20–14:40 — the pre-test fixes; it read shift 1's 4 h 30 min, which holds on B5 only because B5 is read inside the pause), whose chevron opens Cómo se calculó at 14:40 (D3a); the same observation, "2 ofertas arriba de tu meta se fueron sin tomar ›" (both are in the list, 9:51 and 8:40); the section title "Ofertas de hoy · 10" with "te deja por km, sin el coche" — Round 20 (Opus N6) counts every offer known by 14:40, and all ten are drawn; and those offers, newest first, B1's three in this row — `$9.49/km`, `$2.20/km` and the gris with none — then shift 1's five trips and its two verdes that left (the pre-test fixes, their second batch and Round 20; fixture `today_morning_trips` and `today_morning_missed`), `[V] La tomaste / 11:20 · Arriba de tu meta · $9.81/km` ($172 over 13.6 km), `[V] La tomaste / 10:41 · Arriba de tu meta · $9.41/km` ($158 over 12.9 km), `[V] La tomaste / 10:02 · Arriba de tu meta · $10.46/km` ($165 over 12.4 km), `[V] Se fue sin tomar / 9:51 · Arriba de tu meta · $9.16/km` ($132 over 11.0 km), `[V] La tomaste / 9:24 · Arriba de tu meta · $10.75/km` ($178 over 13.1 km), `[V] Se fue sin tomar / 8:40 · Arriba de tu meta · $9.20/km` ($118 over 9.8 km) and `[V] La tomaste / 8:15 · Arriba de tu meta · $10.00/km` ($158 over 12.3 km) — all six trips taken, the five morning fares adding to shift 1's $831 over 64.3 km, and the observation's two verdes; 14:50, 16:04 and 17:21 have not happened yet. The sixth row, 10:02 (722–788), is cut by the nav bar at 772 beneath the 24dp fade, exactly as D1 ends on its 11:48 row (the second pre-test batch: the five rows had ended 50dp above the bar on blank paper, a whole last row that read as the start of the day), and the list scrolls between the drill row and the fixed nav — 968dp of content in a 664dp window, so 304dp — and scrolled to the end the 8:15 row (682–748) sits whole above the fade and the nav at 772 (Round 20, Opus N6 and P4: the list had stopped at 10:02, so "todas las ofertas de hoy" showed four of the six trips and neither verde that left); D3a's backdrop follows, "Ofertas de hoy · 10" above the sheet and the 11:20, 10:41 and 10:02 rows under it. The money is the fixture's: shift 1's $648 after running costs, the 14:32 trip's $114, less the day's $214. **In B9's world (D1b; the pre-test fixes; an alternate labelled above the phone, as B9 and G4c):** D1a whole except the 12:10 row, which follows the suppression rule — `[R] Se fue sin tomar / 12:10 · sin semáforo en pantalla · $2.20/km`, the reason in place of a meaning the driver never saw; B9's grey button and G4c's "No cupo el semáforo" row land here (B9's rows open their own details, §5.1), so the suppressed offer has one wording on both screens, and the cause keeps its one name, "no cupo en la pantalla", on G4c and in Ayuda, never on the row; the offer was not taken in either world, so `$548`, the six trips and the observation hold; its rows are D1a's ten, down to 8:15 under "Ofertas de hoy · 10", the sixth cut by the nav bar until the list scrolls (Round 20), and its 12:10 row opens that offer's detail in this world, D2d (§5.4; the second pre-test batch). The 21:10 day (D1) stays for the evening and H6's narrative; built, Tu día is always the day as it stands at the minute it opens. Muted line only when no shift was detected: "Sin turno detectado: aún no podemos calcular tus horas."
**States:** empty past day "No registramos ofertas este día" · today empty "Aún no registramos ofertas hoy" · imported-only week: no day rows at all ("Este resumen no incluye el detalle por día") · partial capture "El semáforo estuvo apagado parte del día" with the gap shown in Cómo se calculó · offers seen but none taken: no total ("No tomaste ofertas hoy"); taken offers always total as estimates (§4 lifecycle) · locked only inside an older paid period · night (N3): the same list on the night tokens, the marks' fills unchanged.
**Removed:** five metric cards, "Mejores horas" (now a single observation), the "vistas · aceptadas · rechazadas" funnel that didn't add up; in v3 the card backgrounds, the circled glyphs, the pill filters, the scope paragraph and the per-row "aprox."; in Round 14 the "Verdes sin tomar" tab (now the observation) and the shift rows' segments; in Round 15 the date-only stepper (now "Hoy · 19 oct") and the three shift rows (now one timeline); in Round 15b the timeline's time row (four figures); in Round 17 the shift timeline and its per-shift figures (`ShiftLine`; the hours moved into the quiet line) and the day before the car ($1,240 → $1,026); in Round 19 the four rows over blank space that began at 14:50 (now newest-first from 17:21, running under the nav bar); in the pre-test the one drawn 21:10 day behind every route (Hoy at 14:40 now opens D1a); in the pre-test fixes D1a's shift-1-only hours (4 h 30 min, now 4 h 50 min on shift), its list ending at 11:48 on blank paper (now running to 10:02, cut by the nav bar) and B9's and G4c's landing on D1a's "Abajo de tu mínimo" (now D1b); in Round 20 D1a's and D1b's list stopping at 10:02 (now every offer so far, ten rows to 8:15, scrolling) and D1's "Ofertas · 23" (now "Ofertas de hoy · 23").

### 5.4 Detalle de oferta (sheet) [D2, D2b, D2c, D2d, D2e]
A sheet over the dimmed day (handle, top radius 16). Title row: **the mark**, then "Oferta de las 14:32 · La tomaste" at 20sp — the outcome lives in the title ("La tomaste" / "Se fue sin tomar" / "Se canceló" / "Sin confirmar"; week confirmation is separate context on the week hero, never an offer outcome) · the net at the 32sp number step under its 16sp label **"Te dejó · aprox."**, the figure followed on its line by **"sin el coche"** — `$114 sin el coche`, the words in the money face at 500, `--ink-2`, 20sp on the figure's baseline (93dp; a 20px line-height, so the 36dp number line does not grow), as the chip's "/km neto" follows its rate (Round 24, §4's basis rule: in run 8 j2, j3, j5 and j6 still put the trip's basis among their top three, now because it sat in small grey text above the number; Round 21 had put it in the label, "Te dejó antes de lo del coche · aprox.", after run 5's j1, j2 and j4 met $114 without the car beside a day with it); the one qualifier on the sheet; a trip leaves, a period keeps — Round 17; "Te habría dejado · aprox." over "$27 sin el coche" for an offer that was not taken, D2b; while the trip is still under way it reads **"Te deja · aprox."** — the 14:32 trip lasts 24 min, so at Hoy's 14:40 it has not ended, and "Te dejó" comes once it has, as over D1 at 21:10; "La tomaste" and the day's "viajes tomados" count accepted offers, which is why Hoy's 14:40 total already includes the trip, as an estimate — Round 20b, Opus `295`; since Round 24d the day says it too: at 14:40 the 14:32 row reads «En curso» on Hoy and Tu día and the basis «6 viajes tomados (1 en curso),») · the why, in one body line from the rule in force at that moment, the deciding rate in money 600 against the limit it was judged by, in the money face at 500: "$9.49/km · arriba de tu meta de $8.00/km" (the limits saved with the offer — those in force when it arrived, never repainted with a later pair; once the driver has changed the limit the line names since, the line adds "a esa hora" after it (Round 24d: D2 and D2e name tu meta, so a mínimo save leaves them as drawn; D2b–D2d name tu mínimo), "$2.20/km · abajo de tu mínimo de $6.00/km a esa hora" (Round 20, Opus N7: after a save on G2b the sheets read $6.00 beside a door reading $6.50); on D2 the longer line still fits (348 of 353dp), on D2b, D2c (since Round 23's line) and D2d it wraps to a second line, which the prototype absorbs by first shrinking the paper above the actions, to no less than 7dp, then opening the sheet one line higher; "No conservamos tus límites de ese día" if they were not saved; for gris, "No pudimos leer la tarifa"), with "$285/h · 24 min" as its 13sp sub (since Round 16 the offer's $/h lives here, not on the chip; the trip time said once; Round 24 drops its "neto ·", which under "sin el coche" read as a second basis, and run 8's j1 needed a second read of "Neto") · a two-row hairline ledger that adds up to the net, one figure on the label's baseline and the arithmetic or caveat as the 13sp sub-label: Tarifa de la oferta / el pago final puede cambiar `$148` · Gasolina y desgaste / 12.0 km (2.0 para recoger) × $2.84 `−$34`, then, 16dp down on its own hairline where a driver would look for the car, **the day in one body-size row with a chevron, "Llevas hoy, con el coche: $548"** (Round 24: the trip's basis and the day's in the same words, "sin" against "con"; "Llevas hoy, ya con lo del coche" in Round 23) (the figure in money 600; `$1,026` over the 21:10 day) over the 13sp `--ink-2` sub-line **"Se resta por día ($214), no por viaje"**, opening that day's Cómo se calculó (D3a at 14:40, D3 at 21:10) — Hoy's own figure, so no new number and no new basis, and the trip's verb and the day's sit side by side, each with its basis (Round 23, Opus `312` §2: the trip's money against the day's was a top-three defect for four judges in run 6 and again in run 7; a per-trip share of the car was rejected, §9t; until then one 13sp line, "Lo del coche ($214 al día) se resta de tu día, no de cada viaje.", Round 21) (the trip is judged on running costs only, §4); on a closed day the row would say the past with its date, "Ese día, lunes 19 oct, te quedaron, con el coche: $X" (not drawn) · the sheet opens 12dp under the dimmed day hero's rule, so the hero stays visible above it and no line behind it is sliced · the ledger starts 8dp under the hero's sub-line (the ledger row's own 12dp padding keeps 20dp of air above "Tarifa de la oferta"; the pre-test fixes: at 16dp the content ran 8dp past the sheet and left 16dp under the last action), so D2 and D2c held 560dp of content in their 560dp sheet and D2b 504 in 504, each over the kit's 24dp bottom inset (Round 23: with the 57dp day row, its title on one line at 260 of 315dp, D2 and D2c hold 628dp and open at the top of the dimmed hero's basis line — 224 over Tu día, 208 over Hoy in the prototype, where 224 would cut the $548 — and D2b and D2d hold 572dp on the hero's rule, 280; the content still fills each sheet over the 24dp inset, the actions at 660–828 or 716–828) · after a 16dp gap (29dp of paper since Round 23, 11 on D2d), three row actions at the thumb at the row weight (400): **"Cambiar tu meta y tu mínimo ›"** → Tu meta y tu mínimo at rest (G2b; the only one that navigates and carries a chevron; Round 18: the row names what it changes, in the words the chip and Semáforo use — "Ajustar mis límites" until then) · "Avisar que se leyó mal" (WhatsApp to support with time + derived numbers only) · **"¿Se canceló? Quitar de mi día"** in ink-2 (§4's "¿Se canceló el viaje? Quitar de mi día" is the same action): the row becomes "Se canceló", leaves the total, with an undo snackbar; only itemized evidence cancels a trip, an aggregate statement reconciles totals. No verdict pill, no nested table, no paragraph repeating the rate; the colour lives only in the mark. Nothing about the passenger is stored, so nothing is shown. **Each offer row opens its own detail** (the pre-test; Codex R19b: every row of Tu día had opened the 14:32 verde sheet), in this anatomy over the same dimmed day: **D2b, the 12:10 rojo** — "Oferta de las 12:10 · Se fue sin tomar", "Te habría dejado · aprox." `$27 sin el coche` (nothing was taken, so the conditional), "$2.20/km · abajo de tu mínimo de $6.00/km" over "$54/h · 30 min"; the ledger Tarifa de la oferta / lo que ofrecía la app `$62` (no trip, so no final payment to warn about) · Gasolina y desgaste / 12.3 km (5.2 para recoger) × $2.84 `−$35`, then the same day row; two row actions, "Cambiar tu meta y tu mínimo ›" and "Avisar que se leyó mal" — "¿Se canceló?" leaves, because there was no trip to cancel, so the sheet is one row shorter and opens on the hero's rule (280; 12dp under the observation's rule until Round 23); **D2c, the 14:50 amarillo** — D2's sheet whole at D2's height, "Oferta de las 14:50 · La tomaste", "Te dejó · aprox." `$86 sin el coche`, "$7.16/km · cumple tu mínimo de $6.00/km" (Round 23: the chip's yellow phrase in lower case with the limit it cleared in money 500, D2's grammar; until then "entre tu meta y tu mínimo", which named both limits and printed neither figure) over "$191/h · 27 min", the ledger `$120` (el pago final puede cambiar) · `−$34` (12.0 km, 2.0 para recoger), and D2's three row actions. Every figure is the fixture's offer, as C3's and C2's cards read ($62 − 12.3 km × $2.8417 = $27.05; $120 − 12.0 km × $2.8417 = $85.90); behind both is the 21:10 day, because at 14:40 the 14:50 offer had not arrived. **D2d, the 12:10 offer in B9's world** (the second pre-test batch; an alternate labelled above the phone, as B9, D1b and G4c — D1b's 12:10 row, "12:10 · sin semáforo en pantalla", had no detail of its own, so it opened D2b over the 21:10 day as if the driver had seen its rojo): D2b's sheet whole, the comparison "$2.20/km · abajo de tu mínimo de $6.00/km" kept, because the detail is history and the offer was below tu mínimo anyway, with "$54/h · 30 min" still its 13sp sub right under it, then one added 13sp `--ink-2` line under the sub, **"Sin semáforo: no cupo en la pantalla (navegación)"**, which says the driver never saw that verdict on the road — "sin semáforo" first, then the cause in the one name G4c and Ayuda (G8) give it, on one line (275 of 353dp; the third batch of pre-test fixes moved it under the sub and gave it that name; Round 20 dropped the second "pantalla", Opus's nit, while D1b's row behind keeps "sin semáforo en pantalla"); the same ledger ($62 − $35 = $27), the same two row actions and no "¿Se canceló?"; behind it D1b as it stands at 14:40, the dimmed $548 and the observation whole, the 12:10 row under the sheet. The added line makes the content 18dp taller, so the sheet opens flush under the dimmed observation's rule (336, 516dp) instead of 12dp under it, and the gap above the actions is 10dp instead of 16 — the 24dp bottom inset kept, the actions at D2b's place (716–828), nothing behind sliced (re-measured with the line under the sub: 336–852); since Round 23 the day row at 14:40 ("Llevas hoy, con el coche: $548", → D3a; Round 24's words) makes it 572dp on the hero's rule (280), 11dp of paper above the actions; the rojo mark stays the sheet's only colour. **D2e, the 11:20 trip** (Round 24c, Codex 321 task 9 and Opus 322 item 7: at 14:40 the 14:32 trip is still under way, so task 9's «último viaje terminado» is 11:20, whose row opened only the note): D2's sheet over D1a at 14:40 / 82 %, at D2's height (224–852, content 628dp in 628dp, the day row 574–631, the actions 660–828, the 24dp inset), the verde mark and "Oferta de las 11:20 · La tomaste" (one line, 298 of 353dp); "Te dejó · aprox." over `$133 sin el coche` (162dp); "$9.81/km · arriba de tu meta de $8.00/km" (275 of 353dp) over "$308/h · 26 min"; the ledger `$172` (el pago final puede cambiar) · `−$39` (13.6 km, 2.4 para recoger, × $2.84; one line, 185dp); the day row "Llevas hoy, con el coche: $548 ›" (→ D3a) over "Se resta por día ($214), no por viaje"; D2's three row actions. Every figure is the fixture's 11:20 trip, an Uber trip like every trip of 19 oct: $172 − 13.6 km × $2.8417 = $133.35 → $133 (−$38.65 → −$39), $9.81/km, $308/h over 26 min. D1b opens the same sheet (the one row that differs there, 12:10, lies under it). **The trip under way (prototype):** from 14:32 to 14:56 D2's label reads "Te deja · aprox." and its title "Oferta de las 14:32 · en curso" (278dp; Round 24c), drawn at runtime beside the label swap: the kit's longer "· La tomaste · en curso" needs 362 of the title's 325dp, wraps, and costs 4dp of the 24dp inset (part-d's measure).

### 5.5 Cómo se calculó (sheet)
[D3, D3a, D3b, E1d] One tap from every hero's quiet line; a ledger on a sheet, learned once, with the total under the sum line — one four-line ledger for the day, the week and the month (Round 19 drew the week's, E1d). **The fuel row names the distance only** (Round 19, Opus R18-N7: D3b printed "× $2.8417", four-decimal pesos in user copy): its 13sp sub-label is the period's km, and the cost per km lives in Tus costos (G3), where it is set; D2's single trip alone shows its arithmetic whole ("12.0 km (2.0 para recoger) × $2.84"). **Day (D3, "Cómo se calculó · hoy", over Tu día):** a four-line ledger that foots on screen — `9 viajes tomados` / antes de gasolina, desgaste y coche → `$1,590` · `Gasolina y desgaste` / 123.2 km → `−$350` · `Lo del coche` / $1,500 ÷ 7 días → `−$214` (Round 17: the car is a row here, so the day's total is the one Ganancias gives the same Monday) · the total `Llevas hoy · aprox.` → `$1,026` on the rule · one 13sp line, "El pago final lo confirma tu resumen de la app. Antes de impuestos." (an outage adds "el semáforo estuvo apagado de … a …: no registramos viajes") · the sheet is sized to its ledger and opens flush under the first offer row's rule, so the dimmed hero, the observation, "Ofertas de hoy · 13" and the newest row (17:21) stay whole behind it · no button: the sheet ends there and the scrim or the handle dismisses it. **Day at Hoy's minute (D3a; the pre-test, Opus R19b-P1):** the day's quiet line at 14:40 — on B1 and on D1a — opens D3's four lines with the 14:40 figures, over D1a: `6 viajes tomados` / antes de gasolina, desgaste y coche → `$979` (shift 1's $831 plus the 14:32 fare of $148) · `Gasolina y desgaste` / 76.3 km → `−$217` (64.3 + 12.0) · `Lo del coche` / $1,500 ÷ 7 días → `−$214` (the day's whole share at any hour: the car is charged by the day, not by the hour) · the total `Llevas hoy · aprox.` → `$548`; the rows foot exactly ($979 − $217 − $214), so the total carries no rounding note; D3's 13sp note stays; the sheet opens flush under the 14:32 row's rule at D3's height, so nothing behind it is sliced (since the pre-test fixes the dimmed quiet line behind reads "6 viajes tomados en 4 h 50 min,"; since Round 20 the title "Ofertas de hoy · 10" stays whole above the sheet, and D1a's 11:20, 10:41 and 10:02 rows sit under it). The sum a driver checks is the $548 on the screen they tapped. **Month (D3b, "Cómo se calculó · octubre", over Ganancias › Mes):** **D3's four lines with the month's figures** (Round 18, Opus R17-R1: the two sheets opened with one label on two bases), so the ledger is learned once, read at Hoy's minute, 14:40, with E3 behind it (Round 20, Opus N2 and Codex: the month had been the 21:10 day under a 14:40 clock, so a detour in task 4 met "hoy · 9 viajes $1,026" beside Hoy's $548) — `155 viajes tomados` / antes de gasolina, desgaste y coche → `$20,429` · `Gasolina y desgaste` / 1,642.0 km → `−$4,666` · `Lo del coche` / $1,500 ÷ 7 × 19 días → `−$4,071` (the weekly figure over its days, not the rounded $214.29 × 19, Codex R17; today included, so today counts as Tu día's $548) · the total `Llevas en octubre · aprox.` → `$11,692`, over the 13sp sub-line **"Del 1 de octubre hasta hoy a las 14:40"** (Round 20: the rows foot exactly, $20,429 − $4,666 − $4,071, so the rounding note left and its slot dates the sum, which keeps the sheet's height — new copy, §9q; a month whose lines miss the total by a peso still says so on the total, as E1b does for the week — Round 15b, Opus R15-04: the screen that shows the math must not leave a peso unexplained) · the sheet ends at its total above the 24dp bottom inset (Round 17: "Ver cada semana ›" left, because the weeks have their own row on the root, "Por semana ›"); behind it is E3 with its figure beside its bars, and the sheet opens flush under the Por semana row's rule, covering the IMSS row whole, so nothing behind it is sliced; no button (per-app payments live on E3's IMSS row, not here). **Week (E1d, "Cómo se calculó · semana", over Ganancias › Semana; Round 19 — run 3's judges found no screen that broke the week down):** D3's four lines with the week's figures — `62 viajes tomados` / Uber $5,028 · DiDi $3,372 → `$8,400` (Round 22: each app's part, so the $5,028 that E1e reconciles is visibly a part of this week — j1, j3 and j6 could not place it against $8,400; until then D3's sub-label, as on D3a and D3b; Round 19b dropped "según el lector" from the quiet line that opens the sheet and the pre-test fixes from this row, so the week's source lives where two sources meet — the discrepancy sheet, E1e, and F4's contrast between sources) · `Gasolina y desgaste` / 675.8 km → `−$1,920` · `Lo del coche` / $1,500 por semana → `−$1,500` (the same $1,500 the seven bars share at $214.29 a day) · the total `Te quedaron esa semana · aprox.` → `$4,980`; the rows foot exactly ($8,400 − $1,920 − $1,500), so the total carries no rounding note; D3's 13sp note, "El pago final lo confirma tu resumen de la app. Antes de impuestos."; behind it is E1 as it stands, and the sheet opens flush under the tapped quiet line's rule, covering E1's three rows whole;
