the open week (E8) opens the same ledger for its days so far; the statement discrepancy has its own sheet, Tu resumen de Uber (E1e, §5.6), from E1's row "Cuadrar con tu resumen de Uber". **Partial support (B6, not drawn):** `3 viajes de Uber` / ya restamos gasolina y desgaste → `$380` · `Lo del coche` → `−$214` · `Llevas hoy sin contar DiDi` → `$166`, with "DiDi no entra: por ahora no lo leemos." **Tu ciudad:** the driver's net and the basis used for the standing. Rules that live here and never on a root: "Horas = tiempo en turno, no con pasajero"; "Con costos típicos" while costs are unconfirmed. Never locked. No card, no brand-green button.

### 5.6 Ganancias · Semana [E1, E1b, E1c, E1d, E1e, E1f, E2, E8]
**Purpose:** my week as a timeline · **Hero:** "Te quedaron esa semana" as a figure beside its bars · **Primary:** none at the root.
Top bar "Ganancias" · **share icon in the top bar** · the gear (→ Ajustes) · **text tabs** Semana | Mes · the period on its own line, `12 – 18 oct · semana pasada`, over one 48dp row of two worded steps, `‹ Semana anterior` · `Semana siguiente ›` (Round 21: five of six run-5 judges hesitated over the bare ‹ ›; the block grows from 48 to 70dp, so everything under it moves down 14dp; the current week reads "en curso" with "Semana siguiente" dimmed to `--ink-3` and inert; the dates line ends in a small `--ink-2` ▾, `12 – 18 oct · semana pasada ▾`, and opens the Elegir periodo sheet, E6, Round 21b, §9r) · the 16sp label "Te quedaron esa semana · aprox." over **the figure row** (`.figrow`, Round 17, uber-5's layout, §4): `$4,980` at the 40sp figure step on the left, its baseline on the chart's $0 line, and on the right the week's seven day bars in a 168 × 72dp plot (`.spark.mini`) — **each bar a day after its car share** ($1,500 ÷ 7 = $214.29 by calendar day), at 51 63 60 71 100 58 24 % of the viernes $1,166, in the quiet `--rule` fill except **the best day, the one ink bar, named above it at 13sp `--ink-2`** (`vie $1,166`, its Por día row; Round 21: the bold figure read as the week's total, j2, j4, j5), over a 1dp `--ink-3` $0 line and the day letters; the bars are on the figure's basis and add up to it (the days' rounded figures sum to $4,979; the week, rounded once, is $4,980, and E1b says so), so **there is no car line and no caption column**; only `height:N%` is per bar; no future bars, losses on the $0 line, unknown days as gaps · under the row, full width, the two-line quiet line `62 viajes tomados, / ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche ›` (what was taken off in plain words, Round 19; the source is named once, in the first row of the sheet it opens, Round 19b; → Cómo se calculó · semana, E1d, the week's four lines with the −$1,500) · three observation rows (`.obs`, each led by its drawn `--ink-2` glyph; 445–501, 501–557 and 557–613dp, the last 159dp above the nav, so the screen does not scroll): **"Cuadrar con tu resumen de Uber ›"** (the document), one plain line with no figure → **Tu resumen de Uber (E1e)**, where the Uber-only reconciliation is said with its basis (Round 22: Round 21's "Uber reporta $34 más de lo que leímos" over "Leímos $5,028 · tu resumen dice $5,062" put three figures with no common basis under $4,980, and all six run-6 judges stumbled on it; the statement is still named, never silently substituted, and the figure stays on reader data); **"Por día ›"** (the calendar) → the **Por día sheet (E1b)**, which any bar also opens; and **"Por app ›"** (the Comparar glyph) over the 13sp sub-line "DiDi te dejó $1.21 más por km", F1's finding computed, → **the Por app sheet (E1f)**, on Ganancias (Round 23; it opened Comparar · Tus apps, F1, in Round 22, after j2, j5 and j6 had looked for the split here before finding Comparar and task 5 fell from 6.50 to 5.67 — and in run 7 five of six judges were surprised by the silent tab switch, top three for j2 and j6, and j2 came back on the current week) · the bottom bar. **No buttons on the surface:** share is the icon; import lives in E1e's "Traer otro resumen" and in E6's "Traer un resumen". **E1e, "Tu resumen de Uber · 12 – 18 oct"** (Round 22; a sheet over E1 as E1d is, opening at 445dp flush under the quiet line's rule and covering the three rows whole, the tapped one too, so its title repeats its subject): one 13sp `--ink-2` line that gives the basis before any figure, "Solo Uber, antes de gastos"; a two-row ledger, each figure on its label's baseline, `Lo que leyó Kompara` `$5,028` (the Uber part of E1d's first row) and `Lo que dice tu resumen` `$5,062`; under the 2dp sum line, `Diferencia` `+$34` at 600, a plain signed amount with no verdict and no colour; the 13sp note "El total de la semana ($4,980) usa lo que leyó Kompara. El pago final es el de Uber." (its second sentence held on one line); then, set apart by 24dp of whitespace and no rule, "Traer otro resumen ›" → Importar (H4 → H5 / H5b), ending at 828dp over a 24dp inset; no button (the handle and the dim dismiss). **E1f, "Por app · 12 – 18 oct"** (Round 23, Opus `312` §4; a sheet over E1 as E1d and E1e are, at 445dp flush under the quiet line's rule, covering the three rows whole, the tapped one too; the nav stays on Ganancias): the finding at 16sp ink, "DiDi te dejó $1.21 más por km" (the row's sub-line and F1's sentence, so the tap lands on what it promised); the column header, **"te dejó por km, sin el coche"** (13sp `--ink-2`, right-aligned over the rates, one line, 151dp, 8dp above the first rule; Round 24c, Opus 322 defect 4: Hoy's note in the past tense of the week's title, the basis said once for the table); two 56dp rows, the higher app first as on F1, `DiDi · 28 viajes` `$10.34/km` and `Uber · 34 viajes` `$9.13/km`, the rates without a suffix (Round 24 had put "sin el coche" after each rate and kept a two-line basis under them, so the sheet said the basis three times — rubric 12) — F1's rates, each app's payments less gasolina y desgaste over its own km ($3,372 over 255.8 km, $5,028 over 420 km, E1d's split), their difference the finding's $1.21; the 56dp row "Manejaste cada app a distintas horas ›" (→ Datos, as on F1); then one 13sp `--ink-2` line, **"Lo del coche cuesta igual en las dos apps."** (Round 24c: "Ya restamos gasolina y desgaste;" cut, since the column note says the basis; the 18dp it freed went to the gap above the column header, 31 → 49dp, so the sheet still opens at 445 with the basis at 786–828 and the 24dp inset), said beside the rates because the $4,980 behind the sheet does take off the car (run 7's j6 met F1's line as a contradiction of Ganancias); ending at 828dp over the 24dp inset; no bars and no button (the handle and the dim dismiss); closing it (scrim, handle or back) returns to E1 on the same week. Comparar · Tus apps (F1) is unchanged, one tab away. Codex `311` §4's alternative, the row relabelled «Ver por app en Comparar ›» keeping the week and the back destination, is not drawn: the sheet answers where three run-6 judges looked. Cut in Round 17 under the cut rule (§4): "Tu mejor día fue el viernes" (the ink bar and its figure already say it) and "Tu lugar entre los de Uber en CDMX" (a bare link to a tab; the standing lives in Comparar). **E1b, "Por día · 12 – 18 oct"** (the sheet rises to just under the day letters, so the figure and every bar stay whole above it and its edge crosses no text): seven ledger rows, each a day after its car share and each opening Tu día (Lunes 12 · 8 viajes `$594` · Martes 13 · 9 viajes `$736` · Miércoles 14 · 9 viajes `$695` · Jueves 15 · 10 viajes `$827` · Viernes 16 · 14 viajes `$1,166` · Sábado 17 · 8 viajes `$677` · Domingo 18 · 4 viajes `$284`), then the one total on the 2dp rule, `Te quedaron` / "Redondeado una vez · los días suman $4,979" → `$4,980`; no button (the handle and the dim dismiss). The lead "Antes de lo del coche" and the `Semana $6,480` / `Lo del coche −$1,500` rows are gone: the days already carry the car, so they add up to the week. The root never lists amounts, but every breakdown is one tap away. **E1c, a day without trips (an alternate scenario labelled above the phone; Round 18, Opus R17-R4):** E1b's sheet over E1 if domingo 18 had had no trips — the car's share still comes off that calendar day, so its row reads "Domingo 18" over the 13sp sub-line "Sin viajes; lo del coche corre igual" with `−$214` in ink (a U+2212 minus; never rojo, never clamped to zero, never "te faltan"); behind the sheet E1's figure row reads `$4,481` (the six driven days less that $214), viernes still the one ink bar with `$1,166`, and domingo's slot draws nothing above the $0 line; the other six rows are E1b's, and the rounded days add to $4,481 exactly, so the total, `Te quedaron` `$4,481`, carries no rounding note.
**States:** *current week (E8, "19 – 25 oct · en curso", lunes 14:40):* the label "Llevas esta semana · aprox." over E1's figure row — `$548` at 40sp, the figure Hoy gives today (every day carries its car share, so week and month never show a day two ways) — beside the week's seven slots: lunes, the only day yet and so the best so far, is the ink bar at 100 % (the open week scales to its own best day, so bars never jump against last week); its figure is the hero's, so the bar carries none; the six days to come have no shape at all — empty slots over the 1dp $0 line, their letters `--ink-3`, never a box with a height; today is marked in the axis (L in ink 600) · under the row, the two-line quiet line `6 viajes tomados (1 en curso), / ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche ›` (→ E1d's ledger for the days so far; «(1 en curso)» since Round 24d) · one observation row with the daily amount, **"Cada día se restan $214 del coche ›"** (→ Lo del coche, G5; the car's figure said once, in words: a new fact with its amount) · no standing and no compare row until the week closes and is confirmed · *loading (E7):* the same structure — the label, the figure row with its plot, $0 line and day letters, the quiet line's two lines, the two observation rows with their glyphs and Por día's own words — with only the figures and sentences as square `--paper-2` blocks cut to the measured widths of the real strings ($4,980 → 111 × 28dp on the digits' cap height at 40sp, each quiet line 232dp, the discrepancy 282dp) and seven equal `--paper-2` placeholder bars at 40 %; no best-bar figure and no chevrons while there is nothing to scale or open, no loading sentence (the skeleton says it), the share glyph `--ink-3` until there is something to share; tabs and stepper usable, never a word about the network · *offline (E7b):* E1 unchanged, because everything on Ganancias is local (the figure, the bars with the ink best day, the quiet line, the discrepancy and Por día), and one **ink note** under the rows says so — the dashed rule over the cause, "Sin conexión" (16sp 400), and one 13sp line, "Todo lo de esta pantalla está en tu teléfono."; no retry button, because nothing here failed (Round 17: the city row the note used to stand in for left E1) · *Elegir periodo (E6, the "Elegir semana" sheet):* weeks newest first under 13sp month overlines, each month one lined group ("Octubre": "19 – 25 oct · en curso", "12 – 18 oct" in ink with a check, "5 – 11 oct", "28 sep – 4 oct"; "Septiembre · antes de Kompara" — the period before the app, named by its group header in the same words as E3b's stepper ("antes del lector" until Round 20, which took "lector" off the screen copy) — holding "14 – 20 sep / Importada · sin detalle por día", the same words as E3b), weeks older than the free window would read "total siempre visible", no lock glyph, and the last row "Traer un resumen ›", set apart by whitespace; the root behind is E1 with its figure beside its bars, the scrim over the status bar too · empty week ("Esta semana aún no registramos viajes", empty bars, stepper works) · imported-only week (tag "Importada"; **no invented daily rows**: "Este resumen no incluye el detalle por día"; rates whose denominator the import lacks are withheld) · partial coverage notice · first week (< 2 days: bars for available days only) · *older than the free window (E2, "5 – 11 oct · hace 4 semanas" as seen on lunes 2 nov, the first Monday it leaves the this-week-plus-three window):* **the week's own total stays visible** (`$4,747` in E1's figure row under "Te quedaron esa semana · aprox.", with E1's quiet line `60 viajes tomados, / ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche ›` under the row); only the breakdown (bars, days, offers) is locked, and the lock is said in words, not drawn: the plot keeps E1's geometry (168dp, the 1dp $0 line and the day letters, so nothing moves between weeks) but holds no bars — no placeholder shape, never a blur or ghost rows; inside the plot, "Por día · Premium" (16sp 600, the name of E1's row) and, in 13sp `--ink-2`, "Gratis esta semana y las 3 anteriores." (counted from 2 nov, 5 – 11 oct is the fifth week back, so it is the first one locked) — which promises only what an old week really has (recorded days and offers, not standings or best hours; the billing facts, "sin tarjeta" included, live on H2b); no band; the trial is the one primary, "Probar 14 días gratis" · *night (N4):* E1 by token — the quiet bars `--rule` (#2C2C29), the best bar and its figure `--ink` (#F2F1EC); no accent to re-tune.
**Removed:** Historial, Resumen de la semana, Inicio's grid, Comparar's week pill, the goal bar; in v3 the segmented control, the scope sentence, the car-cost row, the accent Comparar card, the "Compartir tu semana" and "Trae tu resumen" footer buttons, the emerald and slate bars, the "$•••" rows; in Round 13 the "antes del coche" tag, the dashed future-day outlines and the 2dp baseline under the week bars; in the residual pass the "(−$1,500)" in the closed week's qualifier; in Round 14 the separate count line and the source-led qualifier row (the quiet line now carries count, source and basis) and the plain rows (now observation rows with glyphs); in Round 15 the bars split on a solid car line and its two-word caption; in Round 17 the dashed car line, its 120dp caption column and the bars before the car (the bars are after the car, like the figure), the 72sp hero (the figure sits beside its bars at 40sp), "Tu mejor día fue el viernes", "Tu lugar entre los de Uber en CDMX" and E7b's offline city line, E1b's lead "Antes de lo del coche" and its `Semana $6,480` / `Lo del coche −$1,500` rows, and E7b's Reintentar; in Round 19 "ya sin … ni coche" (read as "without a car") and a quiet line whose chevron had no week ledger to open. **Glance:** the week's number and its best day.

### 5.7 Ganancias · Mes [E3, E3b]
**Rounding:** the month hero is the unrounded sum of its dated records rounded once; the weekly rows are rounded individually and may miss it by a peso, which Cómo se calculó then states — read at Hoy's minute, October's rows foot exactly, $11,692 both ways, so no screen of the month carries a rounding note (Round 20). **One minute:** the open month is read at the minute it is opened — on the fixture day Hoy's 14:40, the status bar 14:40 · 82 % as on B1 and E8 — so today counts as Tu día's $548 · 6 viajes on E3, E3d and D3b (Round 20, Opus N2 and Codex: the month had been the 21:10 day under a 14:40 clock, three taps from task 4's start). **Above the fold (E3):** `Octubre · en curso ▾` (Round 21b: the ▾ opens the period picker, whose month list is not drawn) over `‹ Mes anterior` · `Mes siguiente ›` (Round 21; without the year, like every period label in Ganancias; "Mes siguiente" dimmed) · the label "Llevas en octubre · aprox." over Semana's figure row (Round 17): `$11,692` at 40sp beside four 16dp week bars (28 95 100 11 %, Semana's bar width) on the 1dp `--ink-3` $0 line, in Semana's grammar — the best week, 12 – 18 oct, is the one ink bar, named above it at 13sp `--ink-2` with its week, `12 – 18 $4,980` (its Por semana row; Round 21b, as E1's "vie $1,166", so it is not read as the month's answer), the other three the quiet `--rule` fill; today is marked in the axis, never in a fill; labelled with October day numbers, "1 – 4 · 5 – 11 · 12 – 18 · hoy" ("hoy" in ink 600; the month lives in the stepper); no scope tag · under the row, the two-line quiet line `155 viajes tomados, / ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche ›` (→ D3b; "en curso" is said by the stepper) · two observation rows (a calendar and the IMSS building, drawn): **"Por semana ›"** → the **Por semana sheet (E3d)**, which any bar also opens (Round 17: "La mejor fue 12 – 18 oct" restated the ink bar), and **"IMSS del mes ›"** with no amounts (→ §5.8) · the **share icon in the top bar**; no "Compartir tu mes" button on the surface · the bottom bar. **E3d, "Por semana · octubre":** "Lo que cae en octubre, ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche" (Round 19: the line said only "ya sin lo del coche", which left out gasolina and desgaste) · the ledger reads oldest first, like the bars behind it, and every row is a term of the sum: `1 – 4 oct · 27 viajes` / "4 de 7 días del coche" `$1,417` (the sub-line says the row's share of the car and nothing more, so the row holds one figure — Round 18, Opus R17-R2: the whole week's figure was a second number on the row and understated a week the reader saw for four days) · `5 – 11 oct · 60 viajes` `$4,747` · `12 – 18 oct · 62 viajes` `$4,980` · `19 oct · hoy · 6 viajes` / "1 día del coche" `$548` (Tu día at 14:40); figures on the title baseline of two-line rows; the two scopes are both named, never interchanged · the total `Llevas en octubre` → `$11,692`, one line with no note (the rounded rows add to it exactly: $1,417 + $4,747 + $4,980 + $548), the only row on the 2dp rule; each row → that week; a tap on any bar opens the same sheet. The month sheet D3b is D3's four lines with the month's figures — what the trips paid by 14:40 ($20,429), gasolina y desgaste over the month's 1,642.0 km (−$4,666), the car cost by day (−$4,071, 19 days) and the total ($11,692), which foots, dated in one 13sp line, "Del 1 de octubre hasta hoy a las 14:40" (§5.5). **Mixed month (E3c, an ALTERNATE FIXTURE labelled above the phone as page text, never inside it — the reader active from 21 sep):** dated reader weeks give the figure, in E3's figure row ("Te quedaron en septiembre · aprox." `$7,170` · the two-line quiet line `75 viajes tomados, ya restamos gasolina, desgaste y coche ›`), the undated import is an observation row, "14 – 20 sep · importada / Te pagó $5,340 · no entra en el total ›" (14 – 20 sep is named in the row and under its slot, never in the hero); the stepper reads "Septiembre"; the two reader weeks are Mes bars at E3's 16dp width on the 1dp $0 line — the best, 21 – 27 sep, in ink, named in the same 13sp `--ink-2` label with its week (`21 – 27 $5,723`, Round 21b), 28 – 30 sep in the quiet `--rule` fill — and the import's slot has no bar at all — the $0 line itself turns dashed under it (`--ink-3`, 6/4: the chip's gris grammar applied to the line), so nothing with a height or an outline stands where no dated amount exists; then "Por semana ›" and "IMSS de septiembre ›". **Undated-import-only month (E3b, septiembre):** the stepper reads "Septiembre · antes de Kompara" (Round 20; "antes del lector" until then; its back arrow dimmed: nothing exists before it); the hero is the payment with its source, scoped by its label so the figure never claims the month — "Te pagó Uber del 14 al 20 sep", `$5,340` at Ganancias' 40sp step under its label (no bars to stand beside; the 72sp display is the day's), the quiet line `29 viajes según tu resumen, sin km ›` (→ Cómo se calculó, where the missing "te quedó" is explained; no "aprox." in the label because a statement figure is exact) — no explanatory line on the root; the week and the IMSS entry are observation rows, "14 – 20 sep · importada / Sin detalle por día ›" and "IMSS de septiembre ›" (which places the payment in the month, never an IMSS outcome); no bars, honest empty space; the car cost of the reader-less days (28 – 30 sep, $643, not deducted: sin km no hay neto) is stated in Cómo se calculó, not on the root. **Allocation rule (stated in Cómo se calculó):** income and running costs are summed by **date** (never pro-rated); only the recurring car cost is allocated by calendar day ($214.29/día) — to every day, today and the open week included, on Hoy and Tu día as on Semana and Mes (Round 17) — so no screen shows a day on a second basis and a week is the sum of its days (the open week says the daily figure once, in E8's row "Cada día se restan $214 del coche"). **An undated imported week is never distributed across months**: it is a week, shown as "importada · sin detalle por día" (E3b, E6). A week **wholly inside one month** counts toward that month's *payment* totals (and the IMSS payments line) without inventing daily rows; its net is withheld if km are missing, and the month hero keeps the reader weeks' total and the import is named by its own row, "14 – 20 sep · importada · Te pagó $5,340 · no entra en el total" (E3c). A **boundary-crossing** undated week stays unallocated and is named in the hero. It never mixes scopes silently. Older months: total visible, the breakdown locked in words inside an empty plot, as on E2. Night (N5): E3 at the same 14:40 · 82 % (the dark theme is a system setting, not the hour), its figure row by token — the quiet bars `--rule`, the best week and its figure `--ink` — at the 16dp bar width on the 1dp $0 line, today in the axis.

### 5.8 IMSS del mes [E4]
**Purpose:** what each app paid this month, the dated reference as information, and the official way to check coverage · **Hero:** per-app payments · **Primary:** **"Consultar mi vigencia en el IMSS"** (free, opens the IMSS site).
**Launch state (ships first, E4):** drill-in ← "IMSS del mes", no bottom bar; the month is chosen with the Ganancias period stepper under the bar (`Octubre · en curso` over `‹ Mes anterior` · `Mes siguiente ›`, "siguiente" dimmed; Round 21), not a top-bar dropdown · the 13sp label "Lo que te pagó cada app · aprox." · two rows with the figure in the money face at the 32sp number step (the one step shared with the chip and the code digits), the app name on the figure's baseline — "Uber `$12,487`", "DiDi `$7,942`" (October to 14:40, the month's minute: today's $979 is all Uber, $979 · DiDi $0, fixture `today_by_app_1440`; Round 20, the split since Round 24c, Opus 322 item 7: C1–C3 are Uber offers and G4 reads «DiDi · Esperando una oferta») — **no reference amount and no threshold line** · rows "¿Cómo funciona? ›" (the 3-bullet sheet: "Lo que te pagó cada app es nuestra estimación con tus registros. El monto que cuenta para el IMSS y el monto mínimo los define la ley, no Kompara, así que aquí no te decimos si ya te toca: consúltalo directo."; on E4 the only link shown is imss.gob.mx/vigencia — the DOF note and the CONASAMI table appear only after B-038 verifies them, served from config; the same links then go in the store listing and privacy notice) and "Resumen en PDF / Premium · no es constancia ni asesoría ›" (the subordinate accountant's row; the non-advice line is its sub-line) — the payments and these rows are two groups parted by one hairline and 32dp of air, as on G6, never an empty band between two rules · under the PDF row, one 13sp `--ink-2` line that says once who confirms what, **"El IMSS confirma tu vigencia. Kompara no lo representa."** (wording to be confirmed by B-038) · the one primary at the thumb. The month view (E3) shows this screen as one row without amounts.
**After the legal gate (E5, marked by the 13sp status line "Ejemplo · regla pendiente · te pagó × 0.94", a label, never a pill):** the title "Para el IMSS cuentan" over one column per app — a 13sp app name over its 32sp figure, both on the left edge of the bar below (`$11,738`, `$7,465`; Round 24c) — and two `--ink-3` bars on the $0 line with a 24dp gap: the **computed statutory base** per app (the payment, which for reader estimates contains no tips and for statements has tips removed, times the rule's factor), each base said once; the 2026 salario mínimo is the system's reference line — a solid **2dp ink line** (81.6 % of the Uber scale, 52dp up the 64dp bar area; the DiDi bar 64 %) runs into a 104dp caption column between its two words, "Salario mínimo" above and "$9,582" below (never the bare word "mínimo", which is tu mínimo); the $0 line steps down to 1dp `--ink-3`; Uber crosses the line, DiDi stays below it and its gap is the one axis label, under its own bar, "faltan $2,117" ("faltan" only on that base; no second ledger of the same two figures; the payments themselves stay on E4). The sources (DOF 5777156, CONASAMI 2026 — the minimum's amount, source and year printed and linked: CONASAMI, tabla de salarios mínimos 2026, conasami.gob.mx) live in ¿Cómo funciona?; the same single 13sp line as E4 under the PDF row, "El IMSS confirma tu vigencia. Kompara no lo representa."; the same stepper, two rows and primary as E4; still no verdict, no colour. The factor and the tip treatment are placeholders labelled Ejemplo until the legal gate defines the rule; internal ticket ids never reach drivers. Store listing and privacy notice carry the same non-representation statement.
**Rules:** one name everywhere ("IMSS del mes"); the reference amount **and the official citations (DOF note, IMSS and CONASAMI links)** are **served from remote config with their year and source**, never hard-coded (the strings in E4/E5 are the launch values, verified in the B-038 legal check), and must be verified before release (the 2026 figure above is the STPS/CONASAMI general monthly reference and is itself a launch-check item); never "Cubierto", "Ya te toca", "debe inscribirte" as a certainty; **until legal (B-038) confirms the income basis, the screen shows payments and the official route with no bars, no "faltan" and no threshold notification**; withholding (ISR/IVA) estimates and the word "retenciones" do not appear anywhere until the rates and base are validated — the PDF lists recorded pay, costs and result regardless. Free. "Sirve para pedir un crédito" is gone; so are the emerald progress cards, the month chip and the seven-line paragraph.

### 5.9 Comparar [F1–F5]
**Purpose:** one weekly comparison question · **Hero:** one supported difference or one cohort-specific standing · **Primary:** none; "Probar 14 días gratis" when Tu ciudad is locked; "Confirmar con mi resumen" when the week is not confirmed; share is the top-bar icon when a standing exists.
The top bar carries the title "Comparar" (the gear at the right; the share icon joins it on Tu ciudad with a standing) and, under it, the scope **text tabs** "Tus apps | Tu ciudad" as a 16sp tab row — the Ganancias grammar (each tab reserves its 600 width, so switching never shifts the row or its underline) · the shared week stepper, `12 – 18 oct · semana pasada` over `‹ Semana anterior` · `Semana siguiente ›` (Round 21), labelled as on Ganancias (Round 21b gave Ganancias' period labels the ▾ that opens E6; Comparar's have none yet, §9r) · **the hero on every state:** its label **is the selector** (`.hero .label.sel`: a 16sp `--ink-2` label across a 48dp row, a 24dp caret at its right end) — `DiDi y Uber · Por km · aprox.` on Tus apps; `Uber · CDMX · Por viaje` (F2, F3, F5) / `DiDi · CDMX · Por viaje` (F4) on Tu ciudad — one grammar on every state, so confirming a week (F5 → F2) never moves it; scope and metric are never a band of their own, and a standing sentence is plain, with no dotted words. It opens one choice sheet: on Tus apps the metrics Por km · Por viaje · Por hora, one at a time, that order everywhere (an unavailable metric is `--ink-3` with its reason under it — no second tab row); on Tu ciudad app, city and metric (no silent default — the city is proposed from the number, visibly, and confirmed; changing it never changes any saved threshold). Under the label, **the finding as one 24/30 700 sentence** in the money face with its figure inside (Round 17, §4: the 72sp display is the day's, and four of six board judges read F1's display "$1.21" as a rate or as earnings) — the difference on Tus apps, the standing on Tu ciudad, or, where there is none, its cause (F4, F5); then the qualifier line (`.sub2`, 16sp `--ink-2`), which leads with the source and whose chevron opens **Datos** — the De dónde salen estos datos sheet: week, app or cohort, metric definition, source, trip counts and km, exclusions; on Tu ciudad the cohort size as a population fact, the waiting-time caveat "no cuenta la espera entre viajes", mean vs median. Provenance is the qualifier's chevron, never a separate row on the surface · **one comparison object on every state**, 24dp under the hero and **drawn to scale** (`.cbar`; Round 15 — the judges said a row on a reference line "does not compare"): two horizontal bars on one scale, 24dp apart, each 16dp, square at the left baseline with a 4dp rounded tip, under a label line — the name and the rate at 17/600, the rate in the money face ending at its bar's tip; the higher figure comes first and sets 100 %; the ink bar is the higher app on Tus apps and "Tú" on Tu ciudad, the other is drawn in `--ink-3` under an `--ink-2` label line, so the gap reads as a length; the pair is one image to a screen reader ("Por km: DiDi $10.34, Uber $9.13"); no mark and no colour, because the semáforo glyph means a verdict, never an app or a person · **one basis line** on every state: 13sp `--ink-2`, 20dp under the bars and 12dp above the rows; it names the basis only — "Ya restamos gasolina y desgaste" on Tus apps (Round 19: what was subtracted, in plain words; "Ya sin gasolina ni desgaste" read as something missing), "Antes de gastos" on every Tu ciudad state — because the source and the count are in the qualifier · "Promedio" is the arithmetic mean of the cohort's per-trip pay ($131/viaje for CDMX · Uber · 12 – 18 oct; the median is $126 and is never shown as "promedio"); the fixture carries the sum and the count that derive it, and the deciles that derive the standing · the contribution opt-in is a row, "Aportar al promedio ›", the one row on every Tu ciudad state, whose sheet carries the default-off switch and its eligibility sentence ("Sumar mis totales, sin mi nombre, al promedio de CDMX · Solo cuentan tus semanas confirmadas con resumen; las estimaciones del lector no se suman") on every surface where it appears (F2–F5, G6).
- **Metrics, denominators and sources.** Por km · Por viaje · Por hora, in that order everywhere (one order, one vocabulary). A rank, a comparison or a "disponible" promise may only pair figures of the **same source** (reader estimate · app statement · imported file) **and the same basis**. The city cohort is statement data, per trip, before expenses, so: a **standing exists only for a week the driver confirmed with the app's own statement** (free and paid alike; otherwise F5); per-app "por hora" is unavailable unless statement hours exist on **both** sides (the reader cannot split online hours between apps that run at once; the cohort may lack hours too), and the reason names the side that is missing — Tus apps: "falta tu resumen de DiDi (el de Uber ya está)"; Tu ciudad: "aún no hay promedio por hora de Uber en CDMX" — generated from the availability registry (driver side × cohort side); **"por km" is unavailable for Uber in Tu ciudad** because Uber statements carry no km. Tus apps uses the driver's own reader data on both rows and says so once.
- **Tus apps (F1, free inside the window):** the selector `DiDi y Uber · Por km · aprox.` (the estimate flag said once, in the label), the finding **"DiDi te dejó $1.21 más por km"** ("te dejó": an app's word, after running costs only, never the car), and the qualifier "Manejaste cada app a distintas horas ›" (the R10 two-sentence caveat in plain words, saying who drove when since Round 21 — five of six run-5 judges found "A distintas horas en cada app" unclear or skipped it; the label's "aprox." names the source, so Round 19b drops "según el lector"; → Datos). Then the bars: "DiDi `$10.34/km` sin el coche" in ink at 100 % and "Uber `$9.13/km` sin el coche" in `--ink-3` at 88 % (Round 24, §4's basis rule: the words after each rate at 17sp 400 `--ink-2`, F5's "· sin confirmar" style, one line each, 165 and 156dp; the chart's accessible name "Por km, sin el coche: DiDi $10.34, Uber $9.13"), so $1.21 reads as a length (trip counts and km live in Datos); the basis on two lines, "Ya restamos gasolina y desgaste." / "Lo del coche no entra: cuesta igual en las dos apps." (Round 21, j6: a "net" rate without the car needs its reason where it is read); no rows, so the root carries three figures. Distance definitions and coverage must match before a difference is shown (an imported week without km shows no $/km). The choice sheet lists Por hora in `--ink-3` with "falta tu resumen de DiDi". With one connected app Comparar opens on Tu ciudad and "Tus apps" is the unavailable tab (`--ink-3`), whose tap opens "Conecta DiDi para comparar tus apps". 37 words.
- **Tu ciudad (F2, Premium): strictly one basis, one denominator, one source.** The city population reports pay before expenses per trip from statements, so the driver's side is their statement per trip ($5,062 ÷ 34 = $149). Under the selector `Uber · CDMX · Por viaje`, the standing is one sentence with its figure inside, **"Te pagan más por viaje que al 78 % de los de Uber en CDMX"** (metric, rank and cohort, said once, so it stands alone on H3), and the qualifier "Según los resúmenes de 1,240 conductores ›" (the source and who is counted; → Datos) — then F1's bars: "Tú `$149/viaje`" in ink at 100 % and "Promedio `$131/viaje`", the city mean, in `--ink-3` at 88 % (the `PercentileBar` is gone; so are the people-dots and any duplicate "Top X %"; "Tú" is always the ink bar, so below the average the sentence reads "Te pagan menos por viaje que al …" and Promedio comes first at 100 %); basis line "Antes de gastos"; then "Aportar al promedio ›". The sentence says what is measured (a per-trip rank is not overall superiority) and is said once. The week figure on E1 names its own source too (its quiet line and the discrepancy row), so a reader total and a statement standing are never confused. If no matching basis exists, no standing is shown. Never a blended "Tú" against one platform's cohort; never "todos los conductores"; "Promedio" never "conductor típico"; no daily/hourly population claims. 44 words, the selector included.
**States:** one app only ("Esa semana solo registraste Uber") · no personal data · **no valid cohort (F4, DiDi · CDMX · Por viaje):** the scope is the selector and the cause is the sentence under it, "Aún juntamos conductores de DiDi / para compararte", whose qualifier "Según lo que registró Kompara de tus viajes ›" (→ Datos; the source first, with Kompara as the subject as F2's names the drivers' statements — Round 20 took "lector" off it: the figures below are the driver's own recorded trips) says what the figures below rest on — no paywall, no standing, no share of a standing, no sample data (the R10 line "No usamos datos de muestra para calificarte" is a rule, no longer drawn); the free self-benchmark fills the slot as F2's bars with the previous week second: "Tú `$120/viaje`" (the stepper's week) in ink at 100 % and "Tú · 5 – 11 oct `$107/viaje`" in `--ink-3` at 89 % (dates, not "semana anterior", which would sit beside the stepper's "semana pasada"), and "Diferencia `+12 %`" 24dp under the bars, the figure in the money face at the right edge (ink, not a verdict colour), computed from the figures printed beside it ($120 ÷ $107; Round 15b, Opus R15-04 — full precision, $120.43 vs $106.90, gave +13 %, which the screen's own figures do not support); basis line "Antes de gastos"; then "Aportar al promedio ›" (viewing never requires contributing) · metric unavailable ("Falta distancia para comparar por km" — choose another, never auto-substitute) · synthetic data only behind a 13sp "Ejemplo" label, never personalised · **locked (F3, free):** the selector, the standing, the stepper and the driver's own row stay visible; only the Promedio value is withheld — its bar is not drawn, because a length would give the figure away, but keeps its 16dp slot, so nothing below moves between F2 and F3, and a **bare 24dp lock** in `--ink-3` stands where its rate would end on the label line, optically flush with the right edge — never a boxed glyph, never a blur, never a red or amber lock; F2's basis line and row stay ("Aportar al promedio ›" — a chevron row is navigation, not a second primary) and the quiet line opens the same Datos sheet; no band — the lock and the one primary say what the trial adds; the one primary "Probar 14 días gratis" (47 words: the selector's four take it past the 45-word target) · **week not confirmed by a statement, free or paid (F5, shown on 5 – 11 oct):** the selector `Uber · CDMX · Por viaje` and the sentence "Falta tu resumen de Uber / para ver tu lugar", whose qualifier "Contamos 31 viajes ›" (→ Datos) is the one figure's basis ($5,000 ÷ 31; Round 19b: the Tú row's "sin confirmar" already names the source); then F2's two label lines without bars, since one figure has nothing to be scaled against: "Tú · sin confirmar" (the qualifier in `--ink-2` 400) · `$161/viaje`, and 16dp under it "Promedio", whose value slot says when the average arrives, "al confirmar tu semana" in `--ink-3` — never a peso figure, a lock or a paywall; no mark (the semáforo glyph means a verdict); basis line "Antes de gastos"; the row "Aportar al promedio ›"; the one primary **"Confirmar con mi resumen"** (→ Importar; "de Uber" is said by the sentence; never a purchase prompt; a subscriber sees the same screen and other paid capabilities stay usable); the R10 four-sentence explanation ("Tu lugar sale de lo que Uber te pagó según su resumen, no de lo que leyó el lector…") is cut to that one line, the rule standing in the bullets above · entered from a month: that month's last closed week, labelled · offline: last benchmark with its week · error: personal rows stay · night (N6, N7): ink on dark paper from top to bottom; the leading bar is light ink, the other `--ink-3`.
**Removed:** the 5-column table, the legend, "Tú/Lugar", "Dónde puedes ganar más", the "Pronto" footnote, standings on sample data; in v3 the segmented control, the metric pills, the two 48dp selector pills, the accent standing card, the 20-person bar, the "$•••" mask, the provenance sentence on the surface, the second footer button and the separate context band; in the residual pass the dotted selector words inside F2/F3's standing sentence; in Round 14 the 20sp standing sentence (now label · 72sp · quiet line), the Datos rows ("Datos ›", "Datos · 1,240 conductores ›", "Datos · solo tus viajes ›": provenance is the hero's chevron) and the source on F5's row (now its quiet line); in Round 15 the compared row on a reference line, the ink and gris marks and F5's dashed line (now proportional bars, or two label lines while one figure waits); in Round 17 the 72sp display figure and its two quiet lines (now the finding sentence and its qualifier) and the count on F2's basis line (now in the qualifier).

### 5.10 Compartir [H3]
✕ "Compartir" in the top bar (the H header grammar), the 13sp label "Así se verá" naming the preview, no bottom bar · the preview **is** the exported PNG (one renderer), for the period you came from, shown as the artefact on a full-bleed `--paper-2` viewer strip (a backdrop, not a card): a 4:5 **paper share card** (radius 16, a 16dp text inset) — the one card-like object in the product, because it is the image — with the K in verde on the wordmark's baseline and the period on its first line ("K Kompara · 12 – 18 oct"), then F2's finding in the first person and drawn the same way — the 24/30 700 sentence **"Me pagan más por viaje que al 78 % de los de Uber en CDMX"** (the figure unbreakable) over the 16sp `--ink-2` line "Según los resúmenes de 1,240 conductores" (no chevron: an image has nothing to tap), 80dp under the first line — so the image that travels carries the qualified claim (Round 17: the 72sp display stays the day's). With "Ocultar montos y horas" on (the state drawn) the card draws **no bars** (Round 15b, Opus R15-09): the bars are proportional and F2 shows everyone the $131 average, so Tú's length (Promedio is 0.879 of it) would give away $149 per trip, while a percentage gives away no amount; the switch off adds F2's bars with their figures — "Tú `$149/viaje`" in ink at 100 % and "Promedio `$131/viaje`" in `--ink-3` at 88 % — so the comparison leaves the app as the same object it is in Comparar (the standing says what is measured, carries metric, app, period and basis, and exists only for a statement-confirmed week on a real cohort); F2's basis line closes the claim as the one 13sp fine-print line, "Antes de gastos" (the count is in the line above; app and city are said by the sentence); the card's inset is 16dp all round, so the image stays 4:5; two facts from the **same statement**, one group at a 32dp pitch on the card's foot, 77dp under the basis line — "Viajes `34`" and the status line "Semana confirmada con mi resumen" led by the check (`.status.fact`: a fact about the data, never the semáforo's green dot) (with amounts shown, "Horas en línea 24 h" joins them); "kompara.mx" ends the card's first line; without a standing the card leads with "62 viajes esa semana"; a reader estimate is never mixed with a statement standing · one row with the switch **"Ocultar montos y horas"**, default ON · the primary **"Compartir en WhatsApp"** and the text button "Más opciones". No streak, no "mejor app", no standing without data, nothing about passengers; no emerald gradient, no K square, no stat tiles — the only colour on the screen is the K. Free for free periods.

### 5.11 Importar [H4–H5e]

detection decides the platform, so there is nothing to pick) · the consent sentence as the 13sp ink line directly above the primary (the footer grammar H1 and H5b share; no left-ruled notice, no band): "Tu archivo se sube a Kompara solo para leer tus números." — it names the one thing uploaded and makes no deletion promise until a verified retention job exists (the account requirement and the free total are stated in §8, not on this screen) · the one primary "Elegir archivos" (multi-select) · then "Leyendo tu archivo" with real events (subiendo · leyendo · armando), one parse · **"Revisa tu semana" (H5):** the already-parsed result on the surface — the 24/30 headline "Te pagó la app", `$5,340` under it at the 32/36 number step and one 16sp `--ink-2` qualifier, "14 – 20 sep · PDF de Uber" (period then source; Round 17: a task hero is a headline over a number) — then the ledger: Viajes `29` · Horas en línea `26 h` · Comisión `24 %` · Te quedaron `—` with the reason as the row's 13sp sub-line, "faltan los kilómetros" (missing fields stay missing, never zero; **without distance the net is withheld too**, not only the per-km rate); the 13sp line **"Se guarda en septiembre como una semana, sin repartir por día."** (Round 19, Codex R17–R18: it said "Se suma a septiembre", while E3c keeps this gross import out of the month's net total, "no entra en el total"); the row "Corregir lo leído ›" between two rules (→ H5d); the one decision, primary **Guardar** — ✕ is the way out, no "Descartar" · **reconciliation (H5b)**, when the week already has reader data: the hero sentence "Esa semana ya tiene datos" (Round 18: shorter), the 13sp context "28 sep – 4 oct · Uber", a two-column hairline ledger (the one in the kit, because the comparison is the point) with each column's coverage as the second line of its header (8dp of air above its hairline), "Lector · aprox. / 1 – 4 oct" | "PDF / 7 días" — Viajes `14` | `20` · Te pagó la app `$1,480` | `$2,210` · Kilómetros `150.6` | `—` (the statement carries no km; its hours are not needed by the merged week, so no hours row; reader hours never per app) — then the merged week under the 13sp lead "Así quedaría tu semana", built like D3's Cómo se calculó: "Gasolina y desgaste" / "de 14 viajes" `−$428` (the row names its coverage, so the partial cost never reads as the week's; the km are already in the ledger above) and the ledger's total, "Te quedaron" `—` on the 2dp ink rule at 600 with "faltan los km de 6 viajes" under it; under it one quiet 13sp line, **"Lo del coche se resta de la semana, no de una app."** (Round 19, Opus R18-N6: Round 18's "Lo del coche −$1,500" row set the whole week's car against this Uber-only statement, against B6's rule; the car is the week's cost, never charged to one app's statement); 77 words, the one frame over the ≤ 70 rule (the two-column exception, the consequence, the coverage and the car's scope); the cross-month consequence is the 13sp ink line directly above the primary, **"El PDF queda en la semana; octubre conserva lo leído del 1 al 4."**; one decision, the primary **"Usar el PDF"** — ✕ leaves the reader's week untouched, so there is no "Quedarme con lo del lector" secondary · toast "Listo, tu semana ya está en Ganancias" → that week.
**Rules:** requires an account (not payment); reconciliation is by app, period and field with **matching coverage for numerator and denominator** — a confirmed full-week payment is never paired with partial reader km to produce a "Te quedaron": the net is withheld and the known costs are shown separately (the H5b preview above; the gap is named, "faltan los km de 6 viajes"); reader hours never appear per app in a reconciliation; retries are idempotent; imported weeks never invent daily rows or offers; the review has a "Corregir" route for a misread field — **H5d, ← "Importar"**, the headline "Corregir lo leído" opening the content over its field group; no intro sentence — the field being corrected is the lead: the three read fields as editable values 16dp apart (Viajes `29` · Te pagó la app `$5,340`, focused, the read figure intact with the caret at its end, a 2dp ink underline and the hint under that field only, "Escribe “Tus ganancias” tal como aparece en el PDF." — "se leyó $5,340" joins the label only once the typed value differs · Horas en línea `26 h`, its "h" a fixed 16sp `--ink-2` suffix outside the editable value), the numeric keypad up, no field and no disclaimer for data the statement does not carry (km), primary "Guardar corrección", text "Cancelar"; recovery states keep the retained result as plain rows with a state word instead of a glyph and offer one fitting primary, never a save — **H5c** (missing second capture): the hero sentence "Falta la captura del Tablero", "DiDi · 5 – 11 oct", rows "Ganancias / Te pagó la app $3,100 · 29 viajes → Leída" (the check: a fact about the data, never the green dot) and "Tablero / Horas en línea y kilómetros → Falta" (the slashed ring), their marks in a 74dp status column anchored to the gutter, the persistence line "No sumamos la semana a tu historial hasta que revises y guardes." (never "nada se guarda", which would deny that an uploaded file was stored), primary "Agregar la captura", text "Revisar lo leído"; **H5e** (failed upload): the hero sentence "No se pudo subir tu archivo", the cause as a status line led by the slashed ring (a problem, as on H2c), "Sin conexión o el servidor no respondió", and the muted line "Tu archivo sigue en tu teléfono.", the retained file as a row with its size as the value, "Uber · PDF semanal · 14 – 20 sep → 1.2 MB" (the retry repeats the same action under the consent already given on H4, so nothing restates it), primary "Reintentar", text "Elegir otro archivo" (the unreadable-file variant swaps the headline, "No pudimos leer este archivo", and makes "Elegir otro archivo" the primary); the saved review stays reachable even when the week is outside the free window. **Removed:** the 4-card picker, the double parse, the 900 ms fake timer, "Extrayendo datos con IA…", the "¡Semana importada!" screen, the unverified "lo borramos al terminar"; in v3 the amber privacy card, the icon tiles, the green check and amber mark, the receipt card; in Round 13 the 13sp flow label, H4's left-ruled notice and H5b's context clause about the reader's days (the column headers carry the coverage).

### 5.12 Premium and Tu plan [H1, H1b, H2, H2b, H2c, H2d]
the bar names the flow (the H header grammar): ✕ "Premium" on H1 and H1b, ← "Tu plan" on H2, H2b, H2c and H2d; no bottom bar · **Premium (H1):** the hero is the driver's own figure **on the basis and source Premium can answer** — the 24/30 headline "Por viaje, Uber te pagó" over `$149` at the 32/36 number step with "antes de gastos" in 16sp `--ink-2` on its baseline (Round 17: a task hero is a headline over a number; never "te pagaron" about an estimate; no data: the headline names the city question alone) — and "¿Y a los demás?", the 22sp section title, is the hook · then one plain benefit list built from **what purchase actually reveals for this driver** (never something already free, such as the standing sentence): three body lines, no rows, nothing to tap — "El promedio de tu ciudad", "El detalle de cada semana", "Tu semana y tu mes en PDF" (the three things the free app withholds on F3, E2 and E4; no "retenciones") · **trial eligible:** the terms are the 13sp ink line directly above the primary (the footer grammar: the commitment is read with its terms), "14 días gratis, sin tarjeta. Después, **$79** al mes." (the price in UI 600 — a price inside running text never takes the money face), and the primary **"Empezar mi prueba"**; the page's air sits between the list and the footer · **not eligible:** the real Play price in the same place and the primary **"Suscribirme por $79 al mes"** + "Se renueva cada mes hasta que canceles en Google Play" · the 13sp legal line "Términos · Privacidad" (working links; restore lives on H2, H2c and H2d, not here). Trial terms and renewal terms never share a screen; no icon tiles, no cards, no green slab. Starts only on this explicit tap and only when a paid result exists to show.
**States:** no account → **H1b** (✕ "Premium"), the title is the task, "Confirma tu WhatsApp", and the rule is two plain lines, "Una prueba por conductor." / "Después eliges cuándo empieza." ("sin tarjeta" is not repeated one tap after H1; eligibility is known only once the number is verified, so OTP completion returns to H1 in its true state and does not start the clock), the same open-underline field as A6 ("Número de WhatsApp · +52 55 1234 5678", focused, the keypad up), nothing under the field, primary "Mandar código", text "Ahora no" · price loading · trial eligible / active / used · **Tu plan (H2, ← "Tu plan" in the bar; a subscriber never sees the sales page as their account screen):** the access end date as the one heading, "Premium hasta el lunes 9 de noviembre", its components as one muted line, "Prueba gratis hasta el 2 de noviembre y 7 días más por invitar.", two open rows — "Suscribirme ahora / $79 al mes ›" (the only figure besides the dates) and "Restaurar compra ›" — and the if-nothing line "Si no haces nada, tu semáforo, tu día, tu semana y tu IMSS siguen gratis."; no primary; a subscriber's Tu plan carries "Administrar suscripción" instead of the subscribe row · **Suscribirme (H2b, ← "Tu plan" in the bar — the price hero and the primary say what the screen does):** the trade-off is the 24/30 headline, "Google Play cobra hoy", and the price sits under it at the 32/36 number step, `$79` with "al mes" at 16sp `--ink-2` (Round 17); renewal and cancellation are one 16sp `--ink-2` sentence, "Se renueva cada mes. Cancelas en Google Play cuando quieras; sigues hasta el fin del mes pagado."; the banked days are one sentence in the same treatment, its one bold lead in ink on the fact the driver could lose, in the Codex wording, "**Tus 21 días gratis quedan guardados.** Se usan al terminar tu acceso pagado y, si aplica, el periodo de gracia." (Play bills from purchase; the server appends the unused promotional days after the paid period and, if it applies, the grace period — never a deferred first charge); the primary **"Suscribirme por $79 al mes"**, the text "Mejor avísame el 9 de noviembre", and one 13sp line "Restaurar compra · Términos · Privacidad"; no weekly equivalent, no failed-charge paragraph; the one billing screen allowed to exceed the word budget, back through legal B-038 · **a failed renewal charge (H2c)** is the heading "Tu pago necesita atención" under ← "Tu plan", never a suspension: the cause as a status line led by the slashed ring (off or a problem, the same mark as a stopped semáforo), "Google Play no pudo cobrar el mes", then the two phases of continued access as a two-row ledger under the 13sp overline "Tu acceso sigue hasta" — "Periodo de gracia → 26 de noviembre", "Después, 21 días gratis → 17 de diciembre" (grace first, banked days after; the last row is the end date, so no if-nothing line repeats it) — the row "Restaurar compra ›" closing the group, and the one primary **"Arreglarlo en Google Play"** · **purchase pending (H2d):** the heading "Pago en proceso" under ← "Tu plan", the pending confirmation as a status line led by the hollow ring (the preparado mark: waiting, never H2c's failure mark), "Google Play está confirmando tu pago", the one-row ledger under the same overline "Tu acceso sigue hasta" — "Tus días gratis → 9 de noviembre" — the row "Restaurar compra ›" (the "I already paid" case), the 13sp footnote "Si no se confirma, no se cobra nada y conservas lo que ya tenías."; no primary, because there is nothing to do (a driver without prior access reads "Premium se activa cuando se confirme") · cancelled (back to H1 silently) · Play unavailable · offline with a valid cached entitlement (no downgrade from a fetch failure). Cancelling returns to the exact originating week or comparison.
