**the figure opens selected** — "6.50" alone, not the fixed "$" or "/km", on a highlight of ink at 14 % (≈ 56 × 38dp; no new colour), no caret — so the first key replaces it; under the field, across the card, the 13sp `--ink-2` helper "Menor que tu meta ($8.00/km)", which takes the place of "Toca la cifra para escribirla." and grows the focused card from 92 to 112dp; the OS numeric keypad is up and the screen resizes above it as Android does — Guardar, the one primary, stays directly above the keypad (508–564dp), "Volver a lo inicial" waits for the keypad to close, and the content scrolls 56dp so the focused card ends on the footer's edge and la regla stays whole, redrawing as the driver types — while the typed value crosses the other limit (a mínimo at or over tu meta, a meta at or under tu mínimo) or the field is empty, it holds the last valid pair, never an impossible one (Round 21b); "6.5", "6,50" and "$6.50" read the same, up to $99.99; the rings stay live and step from the typed value; tu mínimo must be below tu meta, and equal is refused; **the error waits for Guardar**, never live while typing: Guardar on a value at or above tu meta keeps the editor and the keypad, and the helper becomes **"Tu mínimo debe ser menor que tu meta"**, ink 600 behind G4b's ring with "!", never red; tu meta's field mirrors it, "Mayor que tu mínimo ($6.00/km)" and **"Tu meta debe ser mayor que tu mínimo"**; an empty field leaves Guardar inert; the system back first closes the keypad, keeping the typed value unsaved, and a second back asks (G2e). **G2e, "¿Guardar el cambio?"** (Round 21, Opus `302` §1): back — the top bar's or the system's — on an edited screen opens the kit's dialog over G2c (`.dim` + `.dialog`, as G6b and G9), the 20sp question over the change itself, "Tu mínimo: $6.50/km" (both changed: "Tu meta: $8.50/km" over "Tu mínimo: $6.50/km"), and two 16sp ink 600 text buttons with 48dp targets, "No guardar" · "Guardar" — Guardar saves and returns under the snackbar, No guardar closes with the saved pair untouched and no snackbar, the scrim keeps the editor; with nothing changed, back closes without asking; the build centres the dialog (the frame draws it in the free band, 580–709dp, so nothing under the scrim is cut). **G4i, just after saving** (§5.2): the opener with its line already updated ("Tu meta y tu mínimo · $8.00/km · $6.50/km" on Semáforo) under the kit's `.toast` as a snackbar — ink, 16sp 600 paper text led by the 16dp check of a done fact, 4 s, no action, announced politely, placed by one rule on every opener (Round 21b; at one fixed 709–755dp it had covered B3's «Avisarme si se apaga», the offer detail's «Avisar que se leyó mal» and Ajustes' «Tus datos»): 8dp above the opener's footer primary — its first footer button, so Semáforo's «Pausar el semáforo hasta mañana» (725–772dp) and B3's «Abrir Uber para probar» — or 16dp above the bottom when there is none (Ajustes), and moved up to the first gap that clears every row by 8dp where that spot would cover one (the offer detail's sheet: above its rows) — **"Guardado: tu mínimo ahora es $6.50/km"**, or "Guardado: tu meta ahora es $8.50/km", "Guardado: tu meta $8.50/km · tu mínimo $6.50/km", "Guardado: volviste a $8.00/km y $6.00/km"; Hoy's door sub-line follows, "tu meta $8.00 · tu mínimo $6.50" (both limits take two decimals once either has cents). **In the prototype the limits are live** (the pre-test; Round 21): G2b shows the saved pair; each ring steps $0.50 and snaps to the grid; a tap on a field focuses it as G2d draws it, with G2d's keypad, the figure selected and then a caret; the first real change moves to G2 (tu meta) or G2c (tu mínimo) and recolours the preview against the edited pair (verde at or above tu meta, amarillo between, rojo below tu mínimo); Guardar refuses a crossing with the field's error and otherwise saves, returns to the opener — Semáforo, Ajustes or B3, already showing the pair, or an offer detail — and shows G4i's snackbar for 4 s by the rule above; the revert is live whenever the editor's pair differs from $8.00 / $6.00 and inert at it; la regla, rings included, holds the last valid pair while a typed figure crosses or is empty; «Por hora» gives the standard "no está en esta prueba" note; B3's example chip repaints from the saved pair, its labels in Hoy's short form ("$8" while both limits are whole, "$8.50" once either has cents) and its lines 12dp past the wider label, so limits of $10 or more no longer crowd it or the editor's regla (Round 21b); back with a change opens G2e; every "tu meta · tu mínimo" line — Hoy's door, Semáforo's limits row, Ajustes, B3's row — shows the saved pair, while the offer details keep the limits that judged each offer and add "a esa hora" after the one they name once it has changed (Round 20, §5.4; Round 24d); "Volver a lo inicial" puts $8.00 / $6.00 in the editor and saves nothing; starting task 6 resets the limits to the fixture's $8.00 / $6.00 (Round 21b: a save made in task 8 had carried into it; a save otherwise lasts the session); G2d, G2e and G4i are drawn live over the editor and its opener, not as screens of their own. Words: G2b 60 (Round 21b, the revert held at rest), G2 62, G2c 60, G2d 53, G2e 60 (69 with the dialog), G4i 45.
**Removed:** the 55-word intro, forced-caps label, two sliders, decorative badges, the long city name, "la otra cifra", the Simulator screen and its playground, the overlay quick sheet; in v3 the sample offer card, the ✓ badge, the stepper card, the segmented control and the outlined "Ejemplo" pill; in Round 13 the "Más ajustes" row; in Round 14 the chip widened to the page with the limits hung on its lane and the one shared stepper (each limit now has its own control under la regla); in Round 15 the active-card outline, the dimmed rings and the $0.50 step (both limits live, $0.10 printed); in Round 16 the $/h on the stage; in Round 15b the mixed "$9.50" / "$6" tray; in Round 17 the name "Tu semáforo" and the step-only controls; in Round 18 the 20-word helper; in Round 19 the unstated step (each ring now says it) and the mid-edit state as the only one drawn; in the pre-test fixes a tap that opened an undrawn keyboard (now G2d).

### 5.15 Tus costos and Lo del coche [G3]
← "Tus costos" · **a task hero:** the 24/30 headline "Manejar te cuesta" with the per-km cost under it at the 32/36 number step, one run, `$2.84 por km` (the unit 16sp 400 `--ink-2` on its baseline, the stepper's unit style; Round 17: the 72sp display is the day's; live; runs through the real formula: $24.50 ÷ 12 + $0.80; the 12 km sample lives on A5) · the setting row **"Combustible / Gasolina ›"** — the fuel type is a stored property of the car that changes the formula, so it opens a choice sheet (Gasolina · Eléctrico), as G9 does for the city, not view tabs · three plain rows that are the formula, each showing its value and saving on edit through its own sheet (where "Guardar" and "Aplica desde hoy" live — the page carries no footer and no idle primary): "Rendimiento / 12 km por litro" ("típico" until the B4 tap), "Precio de la gasolina / $24.50 por litro" ("referencia" until then; served per city once a city is chosen, national until then), "Desgaste / $0.80 por km" · the row "Escribe tu costo por km ›" (direct entry, the formula's exit). **Lo del coche is not drawn here** — it is not in the $2.84 — so G1's row is its one door and **its sheet (G5) opens over Ajustes:** "Lo del coche" / "Renta, crédito o seguro", two `--paper-2` fields (radius 8, the limits editor's, so the kit has one field radius — Round 21b; 56dp, the 13sp label inside, the 20sp value in the money face), "Cada semana · $1,500" and "Se resta desde · lunes 28 sep" (a dated start that precedes every week it is deducted from), the rule as the driver's question answered in two short sentences, "Se resta de cada día: $214, también los días que no manejas." / "Nunca del semáforo." (Round 17, one money definition: the $214 comes off every calendar day, so the day, the week and the month all carry it — never a trip or a rate), the sheet's primary "Guardar" and the muted text "Quitar" under it (cost changes take an effective date; when km/L changes the app offers "¿Aplicar también a tus viajes anteriores?" because an efficiency correction was always true, a price change was not). While costs are typical, every affected hero's quiet line says "con costos típicos" and the offer detail says so; once confirmed (B4 "Sí, son mis costos"), "confirmado" is said once, on Ajustes ("confirmados hoy").
**States:** validation "Revisa este número" (km/L outside 4–40) · EV values restored on edit; electric profiles say "energía y desgaste" everywhere · saving · error. Typical values are **labelled estimates** ("típico", "referencia"), never silent personal facts. **Removed:** seven equal fields, per-day rent/insurance that nothing read, "ganancia neta de verdad", "mantenimiento" as a headline; in v3 the accent hero card, the two underlined fields, the segmented control, the three-paragraph sheet; in Round 13 the fuel tabs (now the Combustible row) and the Lo del coche group on this screen (now only on G1).

### 5.16 Avisos
[G7] ← "Avisos" · when the system has notifications off, the block is the hero sentence, "Tu teléfono los tiene apagados" (the 24/30 700 headline), with its one-line consequence "Sin ellos, Kompara no puede avisarte" (the reader's failure is said once, by the first switch), and the fix is the one primary at the thumb, "Activar avisos en el teléfono" (→ Android's notification settings); the switches show the saved choices with only the switch dimmed (the titles `--ink-2`, "Sin montos" at full contrast so the privacy promise can still be read) · five switch rows (ink switch on, `--paper-2` with an `--ink-3` ring off), each a real notification the driver would want, titles that name the moment or the thing, never an imperative: "Si el semáforo se apaga" · "Tu resumen de hoy / Sin montos" (the one sub-line kept, as the privacy promise) · "Tu semana, los lunes" · "Cuando toque traer tu resumen" · "Tu mes, para el IMSS" (off in the frame). With notifications allowed, the hero and primary disappear and the switches stand alone, live. The "recordatorio semanal para compartir" is gone; so are the amber card and the tonal button.

### 5.17 Tu perfil · Entrar con WhatsApp
[G9] ← "Tu perfil" · three rows that show their value, no underlines: "Tu WhatsApp / Para cambiarlo, escríbenos" → `+52 55 1234 5678` read-only · "Tu nombre / Opcional" · "Tu ciudad / CDMX y Edomex · por tu número" (accented names; the row says where the value came from and opens the city sheet — CDMX y Edomex · Guadalajara · Monterrey · Puebla) · the city-change confirm as the kit's **system dialog** over the dimmed sheet: "¿Cambiar a Monterrey?" / "Te comparamos con Monterrey desde esta semana. Tu meta sigue en $8.00/km; allá se sugiere $7.70/km." ("Monterrey" said twice, not three times) with two equal text actions, "Cancelar" and **"Cambiar"** (a city change never changes any saved threshold; if the driver never edited the semáforo, the app *offers* the new city's initial value with a preview, and only applies it on confirm) · the muted 48dp text button "Cerrar sesión" in the footer (confirm: "Tu configuración y tus totales quedan en tu cuenta.") · deletion lives only on Tus datos (G6), not here. No primary and no "Guardar cambios" button is drawn: the number is read-only and the city applies on "Cambiar". Deletion also cancels pending uploads, revokes the contribution opt-in, and is available from outside the app (kompara.mx/borrar, named in the privacy notice, per Play's account-deletion requirement); success and error states are explicit. Reader state and local history survive logout and session expiry. No red anywhere. "Entrar con tu WhatsApp" = Teléfono → Código, two screens on the A6/A7 layout (one field, one primary; six cells, the active one the ink underline).

### 5.18 Invita y gana
No frame of its own in v3; re-drawn on the §4 rules from its R10 content. "Tu código" as the hero on the surface (the code in the money face) · the one line "Por cada conductor que entre con tu código, **los dos ganan 7 días de Premium**" (number from the backend, always stated; G1's row says "7 días por amigo") · the one primary "Compartir por WhatsApp" and the text "Copiar código" · a stats line · a collapsed row "¿Te invitaron? Escribe su código ›". Under Ajustes only; never in the core loop; no card, no tile.

### 5.19 Ayuda
[G8] ← "Ayuda" · five short questions as hairline rows, single-open accordion (drawn with "¿Por qué no me sale el semáforo?" open — the walkthrough's hardest task finds its answer first, and since Round 19 Semáforo's "¿No ves el semáforo? ›" on G4 and G4b–G4h opens Ayuda on that answer), 24dp expand chevrons (down = closed, up = open) as the only glyphs, the answer in body `--ink-2`: "¿Por qué no me sale el semáforo?" (four causes, one line each, each with its fix: "Falta el permiso de Accesibilidad: Ajustes › Semáforo › Volver a dar el permiso." · "La batería lo apagó: quita la restricción." · "No cupo en la pantalla (navegación, oferta alta): no tienes que hacer nada; queda en Tu día." (Round 19b: G4c's name for the cause, with its action) · "Lo pausaste: Reanudar el semáforo en Hoy.") · "¿Qué significan los colores?" ("Verde: arriba de tu meta. Amarillo: cumple tu mínimo. Rojo: abajo de tu mínimo. Gris: faltó un dato, como la tarifa (te decimos cuál)." — the chip's own words; the R10 answer's last sentence about the number is cut) · "¿Qué lee Kompara?" · "¿Me pueden bloquear Uber o DiDi?" · "¿Cuánto cuesta?" · the one primary at the thumb, "Escríbenos por WhatsApp" (ops dependency: a support number). No "botón de reporte", no "pestaña Lector".

### 5.20 Tus datos [G6, G6b]
← "Tus datos" · the sentence hero "Tus viajes se calculan en este teléfono" (the 24/30 700 headline: where the maths happens) · the one fact that cannot be tapped as its 16sp `--ink-2` sub-line, "Tu pantalla se lee sin guardarse" (A2: the image is read and erased at once), not a dead row · the first group is what leaves: the navigation row **"Sale del teléfono / Cuenta y lo encendido ›"** → the A2 long form (the WhatsApp number, the totals and summaries G6b deletes, an uploaded file, a read report, and whatever is switched on — so the claim covers the account as well as the opt-ins), and under it two switch rows a driver can explain in one sentence — **"Enviar conteos de lectura / Sin cifras de tus viajes"** (the anonymous read-count telemetry, named as A2's "Manda conteos de lectura" names it: default on, disclosed, can be turned off — the one flow that leaves without a choice per item, covered by "lo encendido" because it is on) and **"Aportar al promedio"** (Comparar's row by its own label; default off; viewing comparisons never requires it; its eligibility sentence in the sheet); subs of five words or fewer, no full stops · three rows: "Datos guardados ›" · "Borrar datos del teléfono ›" · "Eliminar cuenta / También en kompara.mx/borrar ›" (the only entry to deletion in the app) → **the confirm (G6b), a system dialog over Tus datos:** "¿Eliminar tu cuenta?" at 20sp · "Se borran tu cuenta, totales y resúmenes. Tu historial queda en este teléfono." at 16sp (the app knows this driver is on Plan gratis, so no Premium date is claimed; the Google Play sentence, "cancélala en Google Play; borrar la cuenta no la cancela", is appended only when a subscription or trial exists) · two equal 16sp text actions, "Cancelar" and "Eliminar" on the right (the destructive action is never the filled primary, never red; the out-of-app route stays on the row that opened the dialog); afterwards a "Cuenta eliminada" screen, or the recoverable error "No pudimos eliminarla. Inténtalo otra vez.", never inside the confirm. Deleting cancels pending uploads and revokes the opt-in. No blanket "nothing leaves" claim that ignores WhatsApp auth, billing, telemetry, a chosen import or the opt-in contribution; no amber card, no paragraph blocks.

---

## 6. The offer moment (the real product) [C1–C13]

**Laws.** It informs; it never acts (enforced by `verifyReadOnlyCapture`). One second: where the trip sits against the driver's two lines — said by the cue, the phrase and the marker — is the decision; the number is the magnitude. Never cover the host's controls, its fare or Aceptar. Never pretend: a failure is grey, not red. Silent during the trip: the chip exists only while an offer card is on screen.

**Anatomy — "la regla" v16** (the Round 16 blind-test winner, lineup 8, §9j; the component is the Round 16 block at the end of `base-v3.css`, its exact markup per state in `design-system/ui_kits/android-app-v2/_regla-markup.html`, copied verbatim — a frame adds only an inline position; the compact form, C12, adds the `compact` class to the same markup). One reading order in every state: a fixed verdict band on top, the ink tray with the driver's two limits under it. Where the trip sits is said three ways in one place — the cue's shape, the position phrase and the marker against the two lines — and colour reinforces them. The band never moves; the number never leaves it.

| Element | Spec |
|---|---|
| Plate | 240 × 112, radius 14 on every corner, fill `--chip-ink #0E0F10`, shadow `0 10 24 rgba(0,0,0,.24), 0 2 4 rgba(0,0,0,.2)`; one component for every chip in the kit (C1–C5, C7–C10, C12's compact form, the A1/B3/G2 previews, N8–N10). Theme-independent; on a dark host the day shadow vanishes, so a 1dp 12 % white keyline and a deeper shadow (`0 10 24 rgba(0,0,0,.5)`) hold its edge (`.night .regla`, N8–N10). |
| The band | Fixed: 56dp, full width, flush to the plate's top, the same slot and size in every state. A solid 30dp position cue (▲ ◆ ▼) at the left (13dp inset); the deciding rate `$9.49` at 30sp 600 — under the host's 36sp fare — with its unit on the same baseline, "/km neto" (14sp 500, the UI face); under them the **position phrase** at 17sp 600, "Arriba de tu meta" / "Cumple tu mínimo" / "Abajo de tu mínimo" (Round 23: "Entre tu meta y tu mínimo" until then; "cumple" stays true at exactly tu mínimo, §9t): where the trip sits against the driver's own limits, never a verdict word. **No $/h:** the other rate lives in the offer detail (D2). |
| The tray | The ink under the band: the two limits as labelled lines, **"tu meta $8"** (upper) and **"tu mínimo $6"** (lower), Barlow Semi Condensed 600 at 15sp, labels at 72 % of the on-colour and values at full, each on a 2dp line at 75 % white from a common x past the longer label to 42dp short of the edge (x 104; 131 for per-hour limits, C5; 123 when the limits carry cents, G2 — when either limit has cents both carry them, "tu meta $9.50" · "tu mínimo $6.00"); and the **marker**, a 22 × 5dp bar (radius 2) in the state's marker tint, 18dp from the right edge — above both lines (verde, `--mark-verde` #2FB35A), centred between them (amarillo, `--mark-amarillo` #F2B705) or below both (rojo, `--mark-rojo` #FF5C5C, set 1dp lower in Round 18 for even gaps) — the chip's own third line and the same object as the row mark's filled slot (Round 17 marker test, §9j and `180`: 0/6 judges read the bar as a control, 6/6 the white triangle it replaced); the tints are the band hues lifted for the ink tray and are named tokens since Round 18 (Opus R17-R8). Categorical, never proportional to the money. |
| Verde | the band `#187A36`, white text, cue ▲, the marker bar above both lines in `--mark-verde`, "Arriba de tu meta". |
| Amarillo | the band `#F2B705`, text `#1A1300`, cue ◆, the marker bar between the lines in `--mark-amarillo`, "Cumple tu mínimo" (Round 23). |
| Rojo | the band `#D32F2F`, white text, cue ▼, the marker bar below both lines in `--mark-rojo`, "Abajo de tu mínimo"; negative amounts identical (`−$1.05` /km neto, a U+2212 minus kerned to its $; the rate keeps its 30sp and ends at x 192 of 240, C7), same plate. |
| Gris | **no colour, no cue, no marker:** the band takes a neutral `#2A2C2F`; the missing input (24sp 600) over the reason in the present tense (17sp 500, 72 %) on the rate's and the phrase's rows, at the band's 14dp inset, flush with the limit labels — `Sin lectura` / `falta la tarifa`; both lines dimmed (labels 50 %) and dashed 5 on / 4 off, their values kept. A per-hour driver's limits read `tu meta $200/h` · `tu mínimo $150/h` (`.rg-ph`, Ejemplo, C5). Other inputs: `Falta tiempo`, `Falta distancia`, `Sin confirmar`, `Leyendo oferta`; reason `sin cálculo` (the chip is the semáforo, so it never says "sin semáforo"; that phrase is Tu día's reason for an offer drawn without a chip); Tu día logs what failed, in the past (`Sin lectura · No pudimos leer la tarifa`; Round 21, "faltó la tarifa" until then). Never a peso figure for the trip on gris. |
| Compact (C12) | `.regla.compact`: **the phrase line drops, the limits stay** — 240 × 80 (312 × 104 at 1.3×): a 38dp band with the 24dp cue and the rate at 26sp with "/km neto" (the phrase stays in the accessible name) over a 42dp tray with both labelled limits at 14sp and the marker bar at 22 × 4dp; gris puts its headline and reason on one line. The band alone was rejected in Round 15b: without the limits it is the category's coloured rectangle with a number and a glyph (Codex). Drawn whenever the full plate cannot fit — no slot clear of the map's markers, or a band shorter than 144dp (Placement, below); its bottom edge keeps the 16dp foot (C12). **Suppression only below 112dp** (80 + 32), when even the compact chip does not fit (C11). |
| Semáforo apagado (C6) | a different object: a 240 × 72 ink tab, radius 14, the chip's shadow, no lines, no band, no colour, its foot on the regla's (457–529 on the standard host); three lines, each ending on a word — "Semáforo apagado" (22sp 600, money face; Round 18: the tab and Hoy's off state, B2, share one status word — "Semáforo detenido" in Round 17, Opus R17-R10) over "Dejamos de leer tus ofertas" and "Abre Kompara al estacionarte" (13sp): the state, what stopped and the fix — opening Kompara restarts the reading, so it asks for that once the car is parked (the pre-test fixes: the middle line had read "Falta el permiso de Accesibilidad", a cause this plate can never show, because with the permission gone nothing can draw over the host — that outage is C13; the new line is 151dp of ink in the 212dp column; Round 19b gave the tab a cause; the pre-test grew the plate 16dp upward with its foot kept at 529, so the text clears the edge by 11dp above, 10.5 below and 14.5 at the left — the 56dp plate had left 4.5 above and 1 below — and no line ends on "·"; Opus R19b-R1); 5 s, once per host foreground, touches pass through, not draggable. This outage posts its own notice too, "Se apagó el semáforo" over "Dejamos de leer tus ofertas. Abre Kompara al estacionarte." (one per unresolved incident, naming its real cause), so C6's status bar carries C13's K after the clock, in the status bar's ink (the second pre-test batch). |
| Spoken label | the band is one image whose accessible name **starts with the position phrase**, so a screen reader hears the place before the figure: "Arriba de tu meta: 9.49 pesos netos por km" · "Cumple tu mínimo: 7.16 pesos netos por km" · "Abajo de tu mínimo: menos 1.05 pesos netos por km"; gris names its input and reason, "Sin lectura: falta la tarifa", never a phrase; the two limits are real text read after the band (the compact chip keeps them); the cue and the marker are decorative. `check-fixture.py` asserts each chip's phrase against its state. |

- **Position is said three ways in one place:** the cue's solid 30dp silhouette, the phrase, and the marker above, between or below the two lines (none on gris; on the compact chip the phrase line hides and the cue and the marker carry it) — meant to be colour-blind-safe and readable at arm's length without colour (hypotheses until R15 and R14). In greyscale the four states are four distinct patterns — three shapes, three phrases, three marker places, and gris with none of them and dashed lines — and gris should never read as rojo (R14). Colour reinforces the position; the phrase names a place against the driver's own limits, never a verdict.
- **One deciding rate,** always in the driver's chosen unit, always right of the cue, at 30sp — under the host's 36sp fare, so the chip never out-shouts the host (Codex R14); "/km neto" marks it as after running costs and is meant to separate it from the host's gross $/km (a hypothesis for the comprehension pass and R14). While costs are still typical, the detail says so; the chip never says "de verdad".
- **No second rate.** $/h left the chip in Round 16 — six of six judges asked to demote it, and Opus R14 counted four figures on the plate at the decision moment; it lives in Detalle de oferta with the trip time ("$285/h · 24 min", D2). Residual test R3 checks whether drivers miss it at the offer.
- **No brand strip, no K, no verdict word, no coloured rounded card.** The cue is a shape and the phrase a place; "Conviene" / "No conviene" read as orders and never appear.
- Text uses each zone's on-colour (`#FFFFFF` on verde and rojo, `#1A1300` on amarillo, `#EDEDEA` on chip ink); nothing under 13sp.

**State contract** (tu meta $8.00/km, tu mínimo $6.00/km):

| State | The band, or the gris words | The tray |
|---|---|---|
| Valid green ($148, 12.0 km, 24 min) | ▲ `$9.49/km` neto · "Arriba de tu meta" | the marker bar above both lines; verde |
| Valid yellow ($120, 12.0 km, 27 min) | ◆ `$7.16/km` neto · "Cumple tu mínimo" | the marker bar between the lines; amarillo |
| Valid red ($62, 12.3 km, 30 min) | ▼ `$2.20/km` neto · "Abajo de tu mínimo" | the marker bar below both lines; rojo |
| Negative (C7: $22 · 12.3 km · 30 min) | ▼ `−$1.05/km` neto · "Abajo de tu mínimo" | the marker bar below both lines; rojo; never clamped to zero |
| Missing fare | `Sin lectura` / `falta la tarifa` (live chip; Tu día logs `No pudimos leer la tarifa`) | **gris:** no colour, no cue, no marker; dashed lines |
| Missing distance (incl. the pickup leg when costs need it) | `Falta distancia` / `sin cálculo` | gris, in either unit — costs can't be computed |
| Hour selected, time missing | `Falta tiempo` / `sin semáforo` (no rate: a figure on a gris chip reads as a verdict; the computable km rate waits in Detalle de oferta, where it keeps its full qualifier) | gris; the dashed lines carry the per-hour limits (`tu meta $200/h` · `tu mínimo $150/h`, C5); `$9.49` never appears on C5 |
| Km selected, time missing, km inputs complete | the per-km band as usual (the chip carries no $/h) | that state's marker; the missing time is recorded in the detail |
| Costs | — | never unknown: costs start as labelled typical values |
| Payment basis unclear | `Sin confirmar` / `sin cálculo` | gris; never subtract an assumed commission |
| New offer still parsing | `Leyendo oferta` | gris; previous figures cleared immediately |
| Unrecognised layout | `Sin lectura` / `revisa la oferta` | gris; no vibration, no flashing |
| No offer / parser disabled / unsupported | hidden | the Semáforo screen (G4) explains the limitation |
| Capture stopped, service alive (C6) | `Semáforo apagado` / `Dejamos de leer tus ofertas` / `Abre Kompara al estacionarte` | a different object (above); **5 s, once per host foreground, touches pass through**; not draggable; its own notice, "Se apagó el semáforo" · "Dejamos de leer tus ofertas. Abre Kompara al estacionarte.", the K after the clock |
| Accessibility permission lost (C13) | nothing: no plate, no tab — without the service nothing can draw over the host | the host as it is, Kompara's K small icon waiting in the status bar (H6's "Se apagó el semáforo"; the shade it opens the next morning is H6b); Hoy's off state (B2) names the cause once Kompara is opened |

"Unknown" is a data state, not a fourth verdict. **Engine changes required before this appearance ships:** remove "missing fare → red"; remove the zero-cost fallback when marginal cost is unavailable; remove the automatic unit substitution; keep `distinctUntilChanged`.

**Placement, growth and motion.** The plate's bottom edge sits **16dp above the host's offer card**, 12dp from the right edge (top = card top − plate height − 16: on the standard 393 × 852dp host the card's rendered top is 545, so the plate fills 417–529 — a mock reference, not a production coordinate; since Round 15b every placement number in C1–C12 is measured from the card as drawn and from the markers' outer bounds, never from an assumed top), so the band reads beside the offer it judges and never covers the fare, Aceptar, the countdown, the ≡ / En línea / ⌕ row or a navigation banner. The safe band is the host's own: from the bottom of its top chrome (the control row or a navigation banner) to the top of its offer card, measured from the host's bounds after layout, never assumed. **Map-marker exclusions:** inside the safe band the plate also keeps clear of the host's pickup pin, the destination, the driver's puck and, during navigation, **the next manoeuvre on the active route** (the turn the banner announces, C9; Opus R15-02), with **16dp to each marker's outer bounds — a pin's outer stroke included** (Codex R15, Opus R15-03; C1's map pans so the pin's stroke ends 16.5dp above the plate) — taking the first slot, in this order, that clears all of them and stays 16dp under the top chrome: (1) the foot slot, right 12; (2) the same height at left 12; (3) higher on the right, only as far as the markers need; (4) higher on the left, the same way (C8, C9). **When no slot holds the full plate, the phrase line drops first, never the limits:** the compact chip — 240 × 80 — tries the same slots in the same order (C12); only when the compact chip cannot clear the markers either is the last resort the foot slot over the marker — **the verdict beats a pin**: a marker is never a reason to suppress the chip. Positions are stored **per host app and orientation** and revalidated on every layout change (tall offer C8, nav banner C9, font scale C10, an Uber-proportioned card C12). **Suppression:** when the band between the top chrome and the card's top is shorter than the plate + 32dp — **144dp** for the 112dp plate (the 16dp foot plus 16dp clear of the top chrome) — the compact chip (80 + 32 = **112dp**) takes the slot instead; only when even that does not fit is no chip drawn (C11) rather than covering a control; the offer is still logged in Tu día in its own row — the mark in its verdict colour, the outcome, the time and the net rate, with the reason "sin semáforo en pantalla" in place of the meaning (`[R] Se fue sin tomar / 12:10 · sin semáforo en pantalla · $2.20/km`, §5.3), so the log never implies the driver saw a chip; Hoy's status door counts them on its sub-line ("no cupo el semáforo en 1 oferta", B9) and the Semáforo screen names the latest with its time and cause ("No cupo el semáforo · 1 oferta hoy", G4c, §5.2; Round 19, renamed in Round 24) — the cause in one name, "no cupo en la pantalla", with what to do and where the offer went, "No tienes que hacer nada; quedó en Tu día" (G4c, in G8's words; Round 19b, trimmed to one line in the pre-test). **Growth — the 1.3× font rule:** every size on the plate scales with the system font and the plate grows with it — at 1.3× it is **312 × 146** (145.6: band 72.8, cue 39dp, rate 39sp, phrase 22sp, limit labels 19.5sp; 312 = 240 × 1.3; since Round 23 the longest phrase, "Abajo de tu mínimo", ends 75.6dp inside the band at 22.1sp, C10) — and placement and suppression are rechecked after any growth; never truncate, ellipsize, shrink or swap a figure; money, sign and unit never separate. **Motion:** appear ≤ 150 ms after the parse — a fade with a 16dp rise over 120 ms on a decelerate curve (reduced motion: a 100 ms fade only); leave 500 ms after the same offer's card leaves, over 160 ms; a re-parse to a different state is an **atomic swap** — colour, cue, rate, phrase and marker change in place, no crossfade, no travel; a new offer or host change clears stale values immediately. No pulse, count-up, countdown, beep, haptic or celebration. The touch region is the plate minus the system's back-gesture inset, so a drag never fires Back and Back never fires a drag. **Colour-blind cue:** the cue's silhouette, the phrase and the marker's place — a deuteranope who sees verde and rojo as similar olives should still read ▲ against ▼ and "Arriba" against "Abajo" (R15 renders it; R14 tests it on a mount).

**Placement frames (C8–C12, the same 393 × 852dp host; every number measured from the card as drawn and from the markers' outer bounds).** *C8 · tall offer:* the 14:32 verde offer on the host's tall card (a sheet handle, the fare with the host's own gross rate under it — `$12.33/km` = $148 ÷ 12 km, before costs — the two rows, the passenger and payment lines, Aceptar and the host's Rechazar), its top at y 316 from content, not padding; the foot slot (316 − 16 − 112 = 188, right 12) would cover the pickup pin (y 275.5–292.5 with its stroke), so would its twin at left 12, and higher on the right meets the destination square (x 321–339, y 147–165), so the plate rises on the left only as far as the pin needs — left 12, x 12–252 × y 147.5–259.5: 19.5dp under the 80–128 control row, 16dp above the pin's outer stroke, 69dp left of the destination, 56.5dp above the card; the chip never repeats the host's gross figure, and the two never share a position or a face — meant to keep Kompara's net apart from the host's gross, which R14 must confirm. *C9 · navigation banner:* at 14:50 the driver is still on the 14:32 trip when the amarillo offer arrives (◆ `$7.16/km` neto · "Cumple tu mínimo"); the host's 96dp turn banner at y 84–180 (left onto Av. Insurgentes Sur · "2 min · 1.2 km") replaces the control row and the map re-frames under it — the puck (x 77–115 × y 489–527), that left turn on the route (x 196.5–224.5 × y 482–511.5) and the destination (y 371–389). The next manoeuvre is an exclusion: the foot slot, right 12 (y 417–529), would cover the turn, and at left 12 the plate covers the puck, so it takes slot (3), higher on the right past the destination — x 141–381 × y 243–355: 63dp under the banner, 16dp above the destination, 26dp right of the puck, 127dp above the turn; whether the ink plate groups with the host's dark banner is a mounted-test item (R14). *C10 · 1.3× font:* the 14:32 offer at system font 1.3×: the plate is **312 × 145.6** by the font rule; the host scales too (fare 47sp, rows 19sp, a 64dp Aceptar, the pickup address on two lines), so its card top rises to **469**, and the plate stays 16dp above it — 469 − 145.6 − 16 = **307.4**, filling 307.4–453 (Codex and Opus R15: placed from the measured card top, not an assumed 473) — while the host re-frames its map so the pickup dot's outer stroke (y 273.5–290.5) clears the plate by 16.9dp. *C11 · no safe band:* the 12:10 rojo offer ($62) arriving during navigation on the tall card — banner to 180, card from 260 — leaves 80dp where the full plate needs 112 + 32 = 144 and even the compact chip needs 80 + 32 = 112: **no chip is drawn** (never over the banner, the fare or Aceptar, never shrunk to fit); the offer is still parsed and logged in Tu día as above (the 19 oct fixture has no suppressed offer, so D1 draws no such row); suppression is per layout — when the banner leaves, the band is recomputed and the chip returns above the card. *C12 · Uber-proportioned offer:* the 14:32 offer on a card that starts at y 342 (40 % of 852, as on Uber's own offer screen, uber-2), the driver's puck centred above it — its ring x 159–233 × y 153–227, its halo to y 251 — and no pins, as in the reference. The full plate finds no slot: the foot slot (342 − 16 − 112 = 214, right 12, y 214–326) runs 13dp into the ring, so does its twin at left 12, and clearing the ring from above (bottom 153 − 16 = 137, top 25) would sit on the 80–128 control row (top ≥ 144). So the phrase line drops first: the compact chip (▲ `$9.49` /km neto over both labelled limits and the marker, 240 × 80) takes the foot slot, right 12, y 246–326 — 19dp under the ring, over the halo's soft edge, 16dp above the card; at 1.3× (104dp) it finds no slot here. On Uber this is the common case, not C1 (Opus R15: every blind lineup used C1's crop), so the mounted test runs on both geometries (R14).

**Gestures.** **Drag only.** Drag moves the chip (snaps to the nearer edge; a drag never fires anything else). **Tap does nothing** — the parked tap-to-expand failure modes (accidental toggles while dragging, window relayout, low payoff) stand; the "why" lives in Tu día › Detalle de oferta, and the phrase and the marker against the driver's own two lines explain themselves. **Long-press does nothing either.** The overlay threshold editor is **removed**: the chip exists only while an offer is on screen, so an editor over the host could only ever open during a live, ticking offer — "parked only" was impossible to honour. Thresholds are adjusted in Tu meta y tu mínimo (§5.14), reached from Hoy's status door through Semáforo, from Ajustes or from an offer's detail ("Cambiar tu meta y tu mínimo"). This also deletes one interactive surface over someone else's app.

**Recovery.** One unreadable offer: gris, no notification. Repeated failures: the app is marked on the Semáforo screen. Capture stopped while the service lives: the bounded "Semáforo apagado" tab (C6; 5 s, once per host foreground, non-interactive), whose lower lines say what stopped and the fix, "Dejamos de leer tus ofertas" / "Abre Kompara al estacionarte" — opening Kompara restarts the reading (since the pre-test fixes C6 no longer names the permission, a cause it can never truthfully show); it posts its own notice, "Se apagó el semáforo" over "Dejamos de leer tus ofertas. Abre Kompara al estacionarte.", the K after the clock in C6's status bar, and the notice's tap, like opening Kompara, lands on a working Hoy (B1; the second pre-test batch). Accessibility permission lost (the service dead): nothing can draw over the host, so during the shift the driver sees the host with no semáforo and Kompara's K waiting in the status bar (C13), and the watchdog notification **"Se apagó el semáforo" over "Falta el permiso de Accesibilidad. Abre Kompara al estacionarte." (H6, 20:31; B2's hero names the same cause, and B2 dates the outage from this minute, "desde ayer 20:31")** — still first in the shade the next morning, unchanged and not restamped (H6b, 20 oct 8:01, pulled down over C13: the screen task 7 reaches; the second pre-test batch) (one per unresolved incident, deduplicated, naming the real cause — revoked permission · stopped capture · unsupported version — each with its own recovery route; suppressed while "en pausa") + Hoy's off state (B2, an ink state); the notice's tap, like opening Kompara from the host, lands on B2, whose one primary, "Volver a dar el permiso", explains the next step (A3's steps, and only those) *before* any system prompt. Parser kill-switched: B6's words, "Por ahora no leemos DiDi" on Hoy's row and "Por ahora no la leemos" on Semáforo's DiDi row (G4g), with nothing for the driver to do and no "ya lo estamos arreglando"; the reading comes back by itself. Where Android requires an ongoing capture notification: "Semáforo de Kompara activo" with a real "Detener". No greeting overlay before the shift (A/B candidate only; readiness lives in Hoy and the Semáforo screen).

**After the trip and at the end of the day.** After each trip: nothing — the ledger records silently; no toast, praise or judgment. Day summary (optional, channel "Resúmenes"): "Tu resumen de hoy está listo · 9 viajes · 8 h 10 min en turno" (H6: one duration, in D1's form; data fragments carry no closing period) — **no money in any notification** (the phone is mounted and unlocked while driving), never "turno cerrado" as a certainty, and summaries are deferred while a ride app is in the foreground **or an offer or trip is known to be active** (a navigation app can be in front during a trip). Lock-screen versions carry titles only (`setPublicVersion`).

---

## 7. Onboarding [A1–A7, A3s, A3n, A4b]

**Target: one ask per screen; the permission right after the demo, while motivation is fresh; 3 Kompara screens before Hoy on a stock phone (A1 → A2 → A3 → B3), 4 with a detected battery restriction (A4); value on screen 1 in ~5 s; reader *preparado* in ~60–90 s; no account before value.** Round 18 shortened the run (the first day was the walkthrough's weakest task, ease 4.2): the cost question is asked on Hoy the next morning (B4 → A5, typical costs labelled until then) and the WhatsApp number is offered later, from Ajustes or once after the first week (A6 → A7). Progress is a continuous thin bar over **the common run's two steps** (no "n/5"; Round 19, Opus R18-N8: the four-step bar stopped at 75 % on a stock phone and never completed): A2 at 1 of 2, so it fills half, A3 at 2 of 2, full; A4, an extra step shown only when a battery restriction is detected, draws no bar and keeps its 2dp slot, as do A3s (the prototype's stand-in for Android, never built) and A5–A7, outside the run; A3n, A3's own not-granted return, keeps its full bar (Round 21 merged A3b, the picture of Android's list, into A3). Back on every step but the first (the arrow's glyph on the 20dp gutter).

| # | Screen | The single ask | Content |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Demo** | "Empezar" | The lockup "Kompara" at 20/26 700 in the money face (the verde K is its initial; one step quieter than the 24/30 headline) and the headline **"Antes de aceptar, ve cuánto te deja."** on A2–A7's title y (Round 19: run 3's judges read "ve si te deja" as "see if it lets you"; the headline now says how much an offer leaves). 8dp lower, the label **"Ejemplo del semáforo"** (13sp `--ink-2` at the gutter), which introduces once the word every later screen uses (Round 19: "semáforo" was never introduced) and keeps the demo from reading as a live reading (Round 17: the 60-character summary line above the demo is cut; the sample's figures live in the card and the chip), and under it a second 13sp `--ink-2` line, **"neto: ya restamos gasolina y desgaste"**, the one definition of the chip's "neto" (Round 19b, Opus R19 and runs 3–4: "neto" was never defined), paid for by the headline gap (16 → 8dp) and the slice (316 → 306dp); the slice is a 306dp crop of **C1's host** with no host chrome (the control row is cropped away to pay for the legend's height; Round 18 took 24dp more of map to pay for the 48dp rows) — the same SVG map and C1's Viaje card whole (the countdown ring, the "Viaje" capsule, `$148.00` and one 15sp row with **the sample's basis, "12 km en total (2 para recoger) · 24 min"**, so $9.49/km has its distance — Round 18, Codex R17; floating 14dp above the slice's bottom edge), framed by a full-bleed hairline on both cut edges, which cut only map — with the **real** chip, la regla v16 in its verde state, over the slice's map, placed by C1's rule 16dp above the card, showing a verdict that is true under the initial rule (▲ `$9.49` /km neto · "Arriba de tu meta", its marker above tu meta $8; $285/h lives in D2); the pickup dot sits down the driver's street, 36.5dp left of the plate instead of under it (the route leaves at the top; the puck and the avenue label stay whole under the card), so the first frame a driver sees teaches the marker-preserving placement, not the last resort (Codex R15, Opus R15-05). Flush under the slice, **the legend is the state selector** (`.legend4`): **one column of four 48dp rows, each on one line** (Round 18: back on the 48dp target pitch — four adjacent choices need non-overlapping 48dp targets, Codex R17; Round 17: five of six board judges read the 2 × 2 grid as a picker or as ragged — "Entre tu meta y tu mínimo" wrapped, and the shown state's filled cell read as a selected pill) — each state's mark on the 20dp gutter, then **the chip's cue in its band colour** (13dp ▲ ◆ ▼; gris none, its slot kept), then its phrase in the chip's words at 16/22 — "Arriba de tu meta", "Cumple tu mínimo" (Round 23), "Abajo de tu mínimo", "Sin lectura" — so the legend teaches the chip's own shapes as well as the row mark; the shown state in ink 600 with an ink-outlined mark, the others `--ink-2` with `--ink-3` marks (gris dashed), and no cell fill (the text-tab grammar); a tap swaps the chip, the selection and the host card — fare and basis row — to that state's offer (amarillo C2's $120.00, rojo C3's $62.00, gris C4's unread fare), so a plate never sits over another state's offer. A static frame cannot move, so the motion is specified: on first view the demo steps once through the four states (≈1.5 s each, atomic swaps of chip, selection and sample), then rests on verde; no auto-step under reduce-motion; taps still select (a tap ends the run). The sample is tap-inert except the legend. Demo changes never touch real settings. One primary, "Empezar" → A2; the text secondary **"Recuperar mis datos"** brings back a returning driver's costs, limits and totals by WhatsApp number and code (A6–A7's fields) — Round 19: it says what it does instead of promising an account to sign in to, which does not exist. |
| 2 | **Aviso** (Play prominent disclosure) | "Acepto" | Title "Lo que hace Kompara", sub "Para mostrarte lo que deja una oferta.", then **four short lines, each led by its bold lead — Lee · Guarda · Manda · Uber y DiDi — 75 words in all** (Round 18 cut about 100 words to 59 in three lines, the first day being the walkthrough's weakest task; Round 21 gave the risk its own line, a deliberate deviation from the ≤ 60 budget, because it is a required honest disclosure; every disclosed flow is still named), separated by hairlines: **Lee** "cada oferta con el permiso de accesibilidad de Android: toma tarifa, distancia y tiempo de una captura de pantalla y la borra." (the accessibility service, the per-offer screen capture, what it reads and its deletion; the app list from the parser registry) · **Guarda** "tus cifras en este teléfono, nunca imágenes ni datos del pasajero." · **Manda** "conteos de lectura (apágalos en Tus datos) y, si tú eliges, totales, tu aporte al promedio, archivos o reportes." What Kompara does with the permission is said again on A3, right under Android's question. **Acepto** / "No acepto". One disclosure truthfully covers both capture lanes, retained derived figures and the optional data flows; back through legal B-038 (Tus datos › "Sale del teléfono" opens the long form). 75 words, over the ≤ 60 budget by design; the four lines end 116dp above the footer and scroll at large fonts. |

The bar is full: A3 is the common run's second and last step. Help lives under Ajustes › Ayuda. Also opened from B2's "Que no se vuelva a apagar" ("Que no vuelva a pasar" until Round 23; Round 21), shown only when that restriction is detected; from there it is **A4b** (Round 21b): the same title, instruction and buttons on B2's 8:05, labelled above the phone "Escenario alterno · desde Hoy apagado (B2)", with A3n's slashed-ring status "Semáforo apagado · aún falta el permiso" in place of "Semáforo preparado", and «Abrir ajustes» and «Ahora no» both return to B2, where the permission is still missing (27 words since Round 23's steps). B2r's "Que no se vuelva a apagar" opens A4b too (back returns to B2r), where its status line, written for the missing permission, no longer fits (§9t, still open). |
| 5 | **¿Cuánto rinde tu coche?** (outside the first run since Round 18) | one stepper | Opened from Hoy's next-morning question (B4's "Ajustar mis costos ›"), so it draws no onboarding bar (its 2dp slot kept) and its clock reads B4's 7:30; the back arrow returns to B4 unchanged, and the primary saves and returns to Hoy with the costs confirmed. `[−] 12 km/L [+]` (the 56sp "12" centred between the two rings, the unit on its baseline) preselected with "Típico de un sedán"; under a full-bleed hairline the two reference lines "Gasolina $24.50 por litro" and "Desgaste $0.80 por km"; live "Manejar te cuesta $2.84 por km" (the one 600 line) and the sample "Viaje del ejemplo: unos $34" (A1's 12 km offer). One primary whose label says what it does with the untouched value: **"Usar 12 km/L"** (becomes "Continuar" once edited), at the same y as on every A screen; the footer's text secondary "Ya sé mi costo por km". Costs stay visibly "típicos" until confirmed here or with B4's "Sí, son mis costos". |
| 6 | **Tu número** (optional; outside the first run since Round 18) | "Mandar código" | Offered from Ajustes (its first row, "Entrar con WhatsApp") or once after the first week (A1's "Recuperar mis datos" uses the same two screens to bring a returning driver's data back, Round 19); no onboarding bar (its 2dp slot kept); the back arrow returns to where it was offered. "Guarda tus costos y tu meta con tu WhatsApp" (one word per concept: "semáforo" names only the overlay and its status, on A3–A4; the limit is "tu meta") · "También tus totales por semana." · one field, "Tu número" (+52 prefix), drawn focused with the numeric keypad up and the primary riding above it · one line of trust, "No lo mostramos a otros conductores." · skipping (the first-week offer alone adds a text "Ahora no"; elsewhere ←) keeps the free reader working for good. No trial promise here (H1 makes the offer when it is relevant). The account is required only for the trial, import and backup, and is offered again at the first week close. Backup is promised only when the server actually restores. |
| 7 | **Código** | six digits | "Escribe el código" · "Te lo mandamos por WhatsApp al +52 55 1234 5678." · six cells at the 32/36 number step on A6's field idiom (the active cell with A6's ink caret), the keypad up, no progress bar · auto-submit on the 6th digit; "Cambiar número" · "Reenviar en 30 s" on one baseline; network failure → "Reintentar" + "Seguir sin cuenta por ahora". Success returns to where it was offered (in Ajustes the number becomes the first row), never to a simulator; no bar (its 2dp slot kept). |

**City** is never applied silently. Until the driver chooses one, the semáforo uses a labelled national *valor inicial* ($8.00/km) and fuel price ("referencia"); when a number exists, the city is **proposed visibly** ("Tu ciudad · CDMX y Edomex · por tu número", G9) and confirmed the first time a comparison needs it or from Hoy's first-run line "Elegir mi ciudad". Choosing a city changes the comparison cohort and the labelled *reference* values (fuel price) but never any saved threshold; if the driver never edited the semáforo, the app *offers* the city's initial value with a preview and applies it only on confirm. **Notification permission** is asked once, on Hoy, while the driver waits for the first offer. **Decline path** ("No acepto"): Hoy with "Semáforo apagado"; "Volver a dar el permiso" goes through Aviso; no "Pantalla limitada", no manual-entry promise. **Returning user:** A1's "Recuperar mis datos" → number and code (A6–A7's fields; 2 screens) → if the reader isn't on this phone, steps 2–4.

**Versus GigU (phone → WhatsApp code → name/platform):** same OTP, no card; we drop the name and the platform pick (the reader detects the apps); we ask for identity *last*, because our differentiator is a visible verdict and we show it before asking for anything.

---

## 8. Monetization
**Trial timing (judge panel, growth lens):** the 14-day trial is offered only once a paid result exists for this driver — a week that has left the free window, or a statement-confirmed week whose city average is available. Before that, H1 shows the benefits with "Te avisamos cuando haya algo que ver" instead of the CTA, so the 14 days never run on an empty product; the fixture driver qualifies (confirmed 12 – 18 oct week, city average available).


| Job | **Gratis, always** | **Premium** |
|---|---|---|
| The offer | Reader, chip, semáforo, costs, recovery, demo | — |
| Your day | Hoy, Tu día (with its one observation), offer detail, day summary | — |
| Your week and month | This week + the 3 previous **calendar** weeks; the current-month summary (independently free so calendar edges never create holes); days chart; Tus apps; share; **the totals of every older period** | The breakdown (days, offers, bars) of older weeks and months |
| How you compare | One valid, cohort-specific standing sentence on the matching basis; Tus apps inside the window; the self-benchmark when no cohort exists | Tu ciudad detail per metric |
| IMSS and papers | The monthly review per app and the official verification route (the primary action) | The accountant's PDF |
| Import | Voluntary, reviewed, account required | — |
| Data controls, help | Free | — |

**Order of evaluation, every time:** does the result exist → does it meet the data requirement (verification, cohort size) → does the driver have access. Nobody is charged for absent data; verification is explained before purchase; a subscriber lacking verified data sees "Verificar mis datos", not a second paywall; positive entitlement and verification keep their cached state through transient network errors.

**Locks** live only on Comparar › Tu ciudad detail, older periods and the PDF — never on Hoy, the chip, recovery or setup, and on a tab root only as the masked other-side number (F3) or the locked breakdown of an older period (E2), with the driver's own totals always visible. **Tease:** the driver's own value visible, the other side masked, one plain line per screen naming the concrete benefit ("Compara lo que te dejó cada app" · "Ve tu semana frente a los conductores de tu ciudad" · "Revisa todas tus semanas" · "Prepara tu resumen del mes"); any fictional values sit in a card labelled "Ejemplo".

**Trial.** 14 days, a real promotional entitlement (not a Play subscription trial), tied to the verified WhatsApp number, **no card, no charge at the end** ("Sin tarjeta. No habrá ningún cobro al terminar."). **Explicit start only**, and only when a paid result exists to show; OTP completion does not start the clock; signup copy never promises an automatic trial. The **first** eligible week-close notification *invites* it ("Tu primera semana está lista · … si quieres, probar Premium 14 días gratis") but never consumes it; later weeks (such as 12 – 18 oct in the fixture, whose first eligible week was 5 – 11 oct) just say "Tu semana está lista · Toca para verla". The trial's terms and the subscription's renewal terms never share a screen: the trial card stands alone; "Se renueva cada mes hasta que canceles en Google Play" appears only with the subscribe offer (Tu plan or the not-eligible paywall). Tu plan shows "Termina el {fecha}" and what happens if the driver does nothing; one reminder near the end; after: "Terminó tu prueba. Tu semáforo sigue gratis." **Price:** the real localized Play price; $79/mes shown as "≈ $18 a la semana" is the hypothesis to test ($59/$79/$99, weekly SKU).

---

## 9. Decision record — the 16 forks

| # | Fork | Final call | Why (and who held what) |
|---|---|---|---|
| F1 | Third tab | **Comparar**, Ajustes behind the gear (Cuenta behind a person icon until Round 18) | Founder's individual/comparison split; the visible paid surface; Tus apps makes it useful from day one. Codex held; Opus changed. |
| F2 | Chip cue & vocabulary | **Glyph + one deciding rate + "neto" + a small second rate; verde/amarillo/rojo/gris** | A shape is read before words and needs no colour; "Conviene" reads as an order and was retired by design-principles. Codex changed to the glyph; Opus held. Second rate kept pending R3. |
| F3 | Chip tap | **Does nothing; long-press does nothing; the overlay editor is removed** | The parked implementation evidence (accidental triggers, relayout, low payoff) stands for tap; Round 3 showed the overlay editor could only open during a live offer ("parked only" impossible), so it went too. "Why" lives in Tu día; thresholds in Tu semáforo. |
| F4 | Missing input | **Never swap the unit; gris; show a computable rate only as uncoloured information** | Missing distance invalidates costs in both units. Codex held; Opus changed. |
| F5 | Import | **Keep, with consent on every route** | The on-device rule is about screen content; a chosen PDF is a different act. Opus held; Codex changed. |
| F6 | IMSS | **Free monthly review, factual, no coverage/entitlement verdicts; PDF Premium; legal gate on the income basis** | The job is uniquely Mexican; the *assertion* was the defect. Both changed toward the middle. |
| F7 | Signup | **Optional, asked once as the last onboarding step, re-offered with the trial; required for trial/import/backup** | The free reader must never depend on WhatsApp or the network; asking at peak motivation feeds the cohorts. Opus changed to optional; Codex changed to "ask in onboarding". City is *proposed visibly* from the number and confirmed, never applied silently (Round 3 showed silent CDMX defaults everywhere). |
| F8 | Hoy content | **Status + hero + last 3 offers; no week row** (Round 17: the observation left Hoy — its slot carries states only, §4 cut rule) | The last offers are the best readiness proof and the reason to open the app; the week has a tab. Converged; Round 17 cut "Tu mejor hora hoy" as a past fact presented as news (both reviewers, `241`, `242`). |
| F9 | Insights | **One strictly factual observation, no coaching, no blame** (on Tu día since Round 17) | "Se fue sin tomar" covers expired and passed; an expired offer is not a bad decision. Converged. |
| F10 | Goal / streak / referral / share grid | **Goal and streak killed; referral under Ajustes; share = hero + 2 own facts** | Rent is an expense, not a goal; streaks pressure; referral is the WhatsApp-native growth engine and already built. |
| F11 | Comparison structure | **Tus apps / Tu ciudad, one metric at a time, aligned rows, provenance sheet, strict like-for-like; cohort-specific standing only on real data** | 10+ numbers on one screen fail the rubric; blended vs one platform is dishonest. Codex's structure + Opus's standing hook. |
| F12 | Trial start | **Explicit only, when a paid result exists; week close invites** | A notification can't consume a one-time benefit for an absent driver. Codex held. |
| F13 | Free window | **This week + 3 previous calendar weeks + the current month** | Two weeks makes a free month look broken; calendar rule from Codex; four weeks from Opus. |
| F14 | Fixed costs | **One optional weekly "Lo del coche", allocated by calendar day ($1,500 ÷ 7 = $214.29) and taken off every "te quedan" total — day, week and month alike — never off the chip, a trip, "te deja" or a rate** (amended in Round 17) | Matches how renters pay; never inert fields. Opus's figure, Codex's "must be a real deduction". **Round 17 reversed the Round 7 call ("the car is weekly, never on the day"):** the fixture already allocated the car by day in Mes and on the open week, so the same Monday carried $762 on Hoy, $1,240 on Tu día and $1,026 on Mes, and a weekly-only rule cannot give a sane open week (−$738 on Monday, or a $1,500 Sunday cliff); both boards asked for one definition, both reviewers agreed with changes (`241`, `242`), and six of six walkthrough judges stumbled on it (§9l). The chip stays on running costs: the car would turn C1's $9.49 verde into $7.27 amarillo for a cost that does not depend on the trip. |
| F15 | Hero labels | **"Te quedan" + scope line; "neto" only as the rate unit** | Scope must always be visible; the chip stays short. Codex's labels, Opus's brevity. |
| F16 | Greeting overlay | **Not in v2; the failure pill is bounded** | Readiness belongs in Hoy and the Semáforo screen; a running service is not proof the next layout parses. Codex held; A/B candidate (R4). For consistency the C6 pill — "Semáforo apagado" since Round 18 ("Lector detenido" until Round 17, "Semáforo detenido" in Round 17) — is 5 s, once per host foreground, non-interactive. |

|---|---|---|
| 1 | Example arithmetic didn't run through the formula (a verde chip at $7.20/km under an $8.00 floor; a "yellow" offer that was red by its own inputs; D2 that couldn't reproduce its headline) | One golden fixture; every figure recomputed; CI check planned. |
| 2 | Tu ciudad compared the driver's net to the city's gross and ranked on it; the share card broadcast it | Strict one-basis rule: gross vs gross, standing on that basis; the share card names metric, app and period. |
| 3 | The disclosure never said derived totals leave the phone or feed city averages; no opt-in | "Qué guarda / Qué sale del teléfono" blocks; explicit contribution opt-in (default off) in Tu ciudad and Tus datos; Tus datos lists every flow. |

| 6 | inDrive claimed as read; the disclosure listed no apps | App list from the parser registry; "inDrive · Aún no disponible". |
| 7 | "Funcionando" never expired; "en pausa" had no state | Decay rule (since last bind, today); "En pausa" with Reanudar and alert suppression. |
| 8 | Overlay editor "parked only" but only reachable during a live offer; Tu semáforo auto-saved | Overlay editor removed; Tu semáforo has Guardar and a discard confirm. |
| 9 | Old weeks masked the driver's own total | Totals always visible; breakdown locked. |
| 10 | IMSS used a 2025 constant in a 2026 product; "pasaste el monto" asserted; PDF outranked the official route | Dated, config-served reference; "estimado" everywhere; "Consultar mi vigencia en el IMSS" is the primary; no bars until legal confirms the basis. |
| 11 | Trial copy implied an automatic trial; "sin tarjeta" sat beside renewal terms | Explicit start; trial card separate from renewal language; Tu plan added. |
| 12 | CDMX defaults applied silently | Labelled national initial value; city proposed visibly and confirmed. |
| 13 | Accepted offers read as earned money | "La tomaste · aprox." / "Semana confirmada con tu resumen" / "Sin confirmar" lifecycle; no money in notifications. |
| 14 | Emoji as icons; 40dp targets; 11–12px units and legal; unscrollable bodies; stale km/L cost on Cuenta; "lo típico"; "mantenimiento"; "de verdad"; "Gris" vs "gris" | Vector icons; ≥ 48dp; ≥ 13sp; scrollable content; one vocabulary (gris, desgaste, valor inicial); no "de verdad". |
| 15 | Missing frames for en pausa, no-cohort, Tu plan, import review, Cómo se calculó, Lo del coche | Added (B5, F4, H2, H5, D3, G5). |

---

### 9f Judge-panel items declined, with reasons
| Lens item | Why not |
|---|---|
| Growth GR-02: give the city average free in exchange for contributing a week | The city average is the one paid result; a give-to-get makes it free for every contributor and the contribution opt-in already carries no reward by design (F10). |
| Growth GR-06: free = current + previous month | The free window is a decision record item (this week + 3 previous + current month); widening it is a pricing decision for the founder, not a UX defect. |
| Uber-lead D-08: drop the Comparar card from E1 | The card is the one door from "my week" to "how I compare" and was converged in Round 2; it is now one sentence plus one muted line. |
| Data-integrity DH-01: a confirmed week replaces the hero with the statement figure | Rejected in Round 6 by both reviewers: the week hero stays on reader data, names the statement, and the standing uses the statement (§5.6). |
| Driver D-05: carry the gross in the scope line ("De $8,400 que te pagó la app…") | Adds a number to every root in violation of the budget rule (§4); the gross lives in Cómo se calculó. |
| Uber-lead D-09: no pinned footers on drill-ins | E4/E5 are drill-ins (no bottom bar) and keep one pinned primary; F2–F5 are tab roots and their primary ("Probar 14 días gratis", "Confirmar con mi resumen de Uber") sits above the bar as the last in-content element on the Compose build, as §3 says. |
| Codex R7–R9: a mixed-month frame (reader data plus an import whose net is withheld) | Added in Round 9 as E3c, an alternate fixture labelled on the screen itself ("Escenario alterno · lector activo desde el 21 sep"), because the golden fixture has no such month. |

### 9g Round 11 — the craft bar (the v3 rebuild)

Round 10 passed both reviewers on the structural rubric. The founder's verdict on the result (2026-10-26): the chip "still looks like all other apps designed by Claude for that exact same concept" — structure passed, feel did not. A second rubric was added ([`03-rubric-r11-craft.md`](ux-elevation/debate/03-rubric-r11-craft.md): 9 signature idea · 10 glance physics · 11 craft · 12 radical simplicity, with four hard fails of its own). Both reviewers scored the Round 10 version and proposed a redesign independently ([`111`](ux-elevation/debate/111-round11-codex-astra.md), [`112`](ux-elevation/debate/112-round11-opus-5-5.md)), cross-critiqued each other over rendered concepts ([`113`](ux-elevation/debate/113-round11b-codex-astra.md), [`114`](ux-elevation/debate/114-round11b-opus-5-5.md)), and the lead synthesised the build contract ([`120`](ux-elevation/debate/120-round11-synthesis.md)).

| # | Item | Decision | Who held what |
|---|---|---|---|
| R11-1 | Scores on rubric 03 (Round 10 version) | **16/40 FAIL, both reviewers** (Codex 3·5·4·4; Opus 3·4·4·5), every criterion below 8; **all four hard fails present:** the chip was a coloured rounded rectangle with a number and a glyph (C1–C3, C7); screens that were lists of same-weight cards (A2, G1, G3, H1's benefits, H4, G6); a K square as the brand (chip, top bar, H3, H6); the palette was Tailwind defaults in `base.css`. Structural hard fails: still none. | Independent, same total. |
| R11-2 | The signature | **The driver's own floor drawn as a line, with the verdict colour positioned against it** — derived from the product's central computation (net after *your* costs against *your* floor). Other reader apps also judge an offer's net against the driver's own threshold; what is Kompara's is the form (the floor drawn as a line, the colour positioned against it), not the arithmetic. | Both proposed it independently: Codex "cota personal" (two thresholds, a stable number, broken rules for missing data), Opus "raya" (docked two-tone plate, large colour mass). |
| R11-3 | Chip tournament — nine candidates (Codex: cota personal · recibo que queda · margen propio; Opus: raya · brazo · ticket; Fable: full-width raya · marcador · ficha), scored on criteria 9–10 and four safety columns (clear of controls · does not look actionable · gris distinct from a verdict · cue beyond colour) | **Winner: "la raya con carril"** — raya's docked ink plate, colour mass and edge attachment, rebuilt with cota's two boundaries as a 16dp lane and fixed text anchors. Dropped: the tiny side mark, the reversed text order, the all-yellow plate, the moving numeral. | Codex withdrew cota as drawn ("raya is the stronger starting point"); Opus conceded the moving number and the one-line amarillo ("one line cannot tell amarillo truthfully"). Its exact geometry still has to pass criterion 10 on a mount (R14). |
| R11-4 | Visual system | **Paper, ink and the raya:** neutral warm paper (not green-tinted), ink text, Barlow / Barlow Semi Condensed, five type steps, no cards, pills, icon tiles or circled glyphs, verdict colour only on verdicts, errors in ink (no fifth colour), text tabs with a 24 × 2dp underline, follow the system theme with a night set, an unbranded realistic host mock with its control row. | Opus held: neutral neutrals, no fifth colour, Barlow for money. Codex held: 20dp gutter and 4/8/12/20/32 rhythm, the tab underline, both themes, the host's top controls. Both: no cards, totals on the surface. |

Round 13 ([`150`](ux-elevation/debate/150-round13-scoring-brief.md)) scored the Round 13 version on both rubrics, independently ([`151`](ux-elevation/debate/151-round13-codex-astra.md), [`152`](ux-elevation/debate/152-round13-opus-5-5.md)):

|---|---|---|---|
| Codex Astra | **71/80** (9 · 9 · 9 · 9 · 9 · 9 · 8 · 9) | **35/40** (9 · 8 · 9 · 9) | **PASS** — no critical or high defect; 3 low |
| Opus 5.5 | **73/80** (9 · 10 · 10 · 9 · 9 · 9 · 8 · 9) | **35/40** (9 · 8 · 9 · 9) | **PASS** — no high defect; 1 medium, 1 medium-low, 7 low, 1 nit; the signature point withheld in Round 12 is earned |

**Both reviewers pass both rubrics on the same version: the bar of §10 is met.** Hard fails: none on either rubric. Criteria 7 (time-to-value) and 10 (glance physics) stay at 8 for both until a timed install and the mounted glance test (R14). Both listed bounded residuals "before build"; the residual pass ([`160`](ux-elevation/debate/160-round13-residual-brief.md)) applied both lists without changing anything that earned the pass, and the frames and this spec describe the result. Confirmation brief: [`170`](ux-elevation/debate/170-round13b-confirm-brief.md).

### 9j Blind template test (Rounds 13–16)

**Why.** The founder's complaint — the chip "still looks like all other apps designed by Claude for that exact same concept" — had never been tested directly or blindly; Rounds 11–13 passed both reviewers on their own rubrics. Record: [`180`](ux-elevation/debate/180-blind-template-test.md), proposals `182` and `184`, propagation [`190`](ux-elevation/debate/190-blind-winners-propagation.md).

**Method.** (1) **A generic baseline:** fresh models with no project context designed the same chip and the same Hoy from one neutral brief — 12 generic chips (Opus ×4, Sonnet ×4, Haiku ×3, Codex ×1) and 4 generic Hoy screens. (2) **A neutral host:** every design rendered on one identical, unbranded host with only its own CSS; ours were injected the same way, with the Round 10 chip (the version the founder rejected) as a control. (3) **Shuffled letters,** the answer key kept outside the judges' folder. (4) **Six context-free judges** — Claude Plan agents (Opus ×2, Sonnet ×2, Haiku ×1) and Codex CLI — grouped the designs into visual families, flagged templates, and scored distinctiveness, craft, one-second glance and Uber-class rank against Uber Driver screenshots. Chip rounds 1–5, Hoy rounds 1–3. A degraded-image glance proxy (tiny, blur, greyscale, deuteranopia, glare) scored every design full marks — models read degraded images far better than peripheral human vision — so it is not used as evidence.

**Outcome.**
- **The template is real.** 11 of 12 generic chips fall into two families every judge named: the "split verdict tile" (a coloured block with a check and a verdict word, a dark panel, a red-amber-green slider with a thumb between $6 and $8) and the "financial summary card" (a status pill, a big $/km, $/h, a gauge). The Round 10 chip was called a template by 5 of 6 judges.
- **v3 was distinct, not Uber-class** (craft 5.3 against 6.2–7.5 for the generics; glance rank 10.7 of 14; Uber-class rank 10.0 of 11 in round 2): flush-edge bars and tiny grey labels read unfinished, the amarillo band was thin, and the position had to be learned, with no shape or word for a first encounter.
- **The chip winner, "la regla" (r5a, §6):** distinct 7.5 · craft 7.5 (= the best generic) · Uber-class rank **2.8 of 11, the best of all** · template votes **0/6** · point of view **5/6** · glance rank 6.2 of 11 (3.5 in lineup 6, once ◆ had replaced the bar) — still the moving band, which Round 16 replaced (below).
- **The Hoy winner, v5 (§5.1):** Uber-class **8.1, the best of 7** · clarity 8.3 (best generic 8.7) · distinct 7.2 · template votes **0/6** · Uber rank **1.2** (5 of 6 judges ranked it first).
- **Vocabulary, from the judges' reading:** the old pair read backwards ("tu mínimo" for the green limit), so the green limit is **"tu meta"** and the red one **"tu mínimo"** (§4).
- **The amarillo cue (Round 14b):** the shipped solid bar ▬ read as a minus sign to six blind judges of six, because it sat left of the figure beside C7's real negative (the yellow plate read "−$7.16"). In a test of five glyphs before the same six judges the solid diamond ◆ came first for five of six and read as negative for none, so the cue set is ▲ ◆ ▼ ([`180`](ux-elevation/debate/180-blind-template-test.md), Round 14b).
- **Rounds 15–16, a fixed reading order (lineups 7 and 8).** Codex's Round 14 score (glance physics 7: the 40sp rate out-shouted the host's 36sp fare) and six of six judges comparing eight screens with Uber Driver asked for the overlay's number in one place, quieter limits and $/h demoted. *Lineup 7* changed the frame — the crop now shows the host's offer card and its fare, and judges also score reading order and host hierarchy — and the picture changed with it: the generic split tile (a road-sign icon and a verdict word) led the glance (1.2) and the Uber-class rank (2.3), the moving band fell to 6.8 and 7.2 with the worst reading order (3.7), and four fixed-order proposals fixed reading order (8.0–8.8) without winning the glance. The lesson is about method: model judgement of glance is framing-sensitive (the same chip ranked 3.5 in lineup 6 and 6.8 in lineup 7), so every chip lineup since uses this crop. *Lineup 8* asked for a big solid shape and a short position phrase, never the template's judgement word, with $/h gone; **opus-r16a became la regla v16** (§6): first on Uber-class (2.8 of 11), craft 7.0 (tied best), reading order 8.5, host hierarchy 7.5, glance 3.5 against the generic leaders' 3.2–3.3 — the first of ours in the glance lead group under the realistic crop — and template 1/6 against 5 or 6 of 6 for the generic chips. **The trade:** distinctiveness 5.8 against the moving band's 7.5, and the clearest point of view (five of six judges still pick the moving band). A driver's one-second read and Uber-class restraint outrank the most sketchable idea, which survives in the two labelled limits, the marker and the row marks.

**Propagated in this version:** la regla v16 (§6, and every chip preview: A1, B3, G2, N8–N10), Hoy v5 (§5.1) and its root anatomy on every period hero (§4), the white day surface and the system layer (§4), the new pair everywhere, the ◆ cue, the consolidation's shared rules (the type ladder, `.status.fact`, the 16dp placement) and the Round 15 screen fixes the judges asked for (A1's labelled legend, one route from Hoy to Tu día, a date-aware Tu día, comparisons and best days drawn to scale, G2's two live limits; §5). §9g–§9i keep the raya-con-carril chip, the old pair and their type steps as recorded, and the outcome bullets above keep the moving band as it was judged; §4 and §6 are the current law.

### 9l Round 17 — one money definition, the cut rule, the day's display (debate + walkthrough)

**Why.** Both reviewers passed the kit on both rubrics (Round 15c), but two whole-product boards plateaued at a mean Uber-class of 6.5–6.7 and simplicity 6.7–6.8 and kept asking for the same things (§10). The brief ([`240`](ux-elevation/debate/240-round17-debate-brief.md)) put three proposals to both reviewers — **P1** one money definition, **P2** cut the second tier, **P3** a drawn icon set — and asked each for the one change that would most raise "comparable in use and feel to Uber Driver". Codex ([`241`](ux-elevation/debate/241-round17-codex-astra.md)) and Opus ([`242`](ux-elevation/debate/242-round17-opus-5-5.md)) both answered **AGREE WITH CHANGES** on all three; both corrected the brief's B4 figure ($284, not $285: the brief subtracted rounded figures). A **usability walkthrough** then ran the Round 15c kit as a prototype: six context-free model judges, each a first-time CDMX driver, did eight tasks on the contact sheets and a screen list, naming the screen and element tapped at each step, the taps, success, where they hesitated, and ease on the Single Ease Question (1–7), with two Uber Driver screenshots for comparison (§11 R16).

| Walkthrough task (Round 15c kit) | Success (yes / partial / no) | Mean taps | Mean ease (1–7) |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 · Hoy: what I kept today, and is it reading? (B1) | 6 / 0 / 0 | 0.0 | 6.2 |
| 2 · An offer (C2): worth it, and why? | 6 / 0 / 0 | 0.0 | 6.0 |
| 3 · Every offer today with its $/km | 4 / 2 / 0 | 1.0 | 5.5 |
| 4 · Last week after all costs, car included | 6 / 0 / 0 | 1.8 | 6.0 |
| 5 · Which app paid more per km last week | 6 / 0 / 0 | 1.0 | 6.2 |
| 6 · Change tu mínimo to $6.50/km | 6 / 0 / 0 | 8.0 | 4.8 |
| 7 · The overlay stopped: why, and what to do | 3 / 3 / 0 | 3.8 | 4.7 |
| 8 · First day, from A1 to a working overlay | 5 / 1 / 0 | 8.3 | 4.5 |
| **All tasks** | **42 / 6 / 0** | | **mean ease 5.5 / 7** · ease against Uber **6.5 / 10** · would understand every screen **5.7 / 10** |

**The finding, six of six.** Every judge named the two meanings of "Te quedan" among their three biggest problems: Hoy's "Te quedan hoy $762" left the car out while the open week (E8) showed $548 for the same Monday with the car's $214 taken off, and only D3's fine print said why. Repeated by three or four of six: the overlay called "semáforo" in settings and help but "Lector" on Hoy, with status dash patterns nobody decoded; the limits behind an unlabelled person icon, under "Tu semáforo", changed only in $0.10 steps (8 taps, ease 4.8); an overlay that disappeared while Hoy still said "funcionando", and a B2 button that did not say where it led; A3's reassurance in grey under the "control total" dialog, and setup ending at "preparado" with no way to test it.

### 9n Round 19 — the door names its destination, Semáforo in its states, the week's ledger (walkthrough run 3 + both Round 18 lists)

**Why.** Round 18's re-score passed both reviewers on both rubrics — Codex 70/80 + 34/40 ([`261`](ux-elevation/debate/261-round18-codex-astra.md): navigation +1 and consistency +2; still open, the recovery after C11 never drawn populated, the costs' first-day disclosure, H5's "Se suma a septiembre", E1c's negative day and D1's continuation) and Opus 76/80 + 34/40 ([`262`](ux-elevation/debate/262-round18-opus-5-5.md): G4 drawn only in its warning state, D1, G2 only mid-edit, G4's double negative, B3 and B4 without the door, H5b's car, D3b's four-decimal rate, the progress bar, B8's label) — and both kept "comparable in use" at NOT_YET.
